# MissedCall AI — Complete Codebase Audit

> **Purpose.** A self-contained, ground-truth technical reference for the MissedCall AI codebase, generated by reading the **actual source** (not the existing `CLAUDE.md`, which has drifted). Hand this to another AI to give it an exact picture of what the code contains and how it is built.

> **Generated.** 2026-06-16 via a 17-agent parallel audit (each agent read its subsystem in full). Repo: `missedcall-ai` — Next.js 14.2.21 App Router, TypeScript strict, Prisma/Neon Postgres, Clerk auth, Telnyx voice+SMS, Anthropic Claude, Google Calendar + Google Ads, Resend + nodemailer, Vercel Blob. ~33.8k lines across 185 TS/TSX files, 56 API routes, 20 Prisma models.

> **How to read this.** Section 1 is the architectural overview + end-to-end sequence diagrams — start there. Sections 2–17 are exhaustive per-subsystem references. The final section (and Appendix A) catalog every place the current code diverges from the legacy `CLAUDE.md`.

## 1. End-to-End Flow & Architecture

This section is the runtime spine of MissedCall AI. It traces every request entry point through to its side effects (DB writes, Telnyx calls, Claude calls, Resend emails) with exact file:line refs. Ground-truth source: `app/api/webhooks/voice/route.ts` (1113 lines), `app/api/webhooks/sms/route.ts` (2555 lines), `lib/create-booking.ts`, `lib/notify-owner.ts`, `lib/business-features.ts`, `lib/contacts-check.ts`, `prisma/schema.prisma`.

### 1. Architecture Overview

MissedCall AI is a single Next.js 14 App Router deployment serving five distinct request surfaces, all converging on one Postgres tenant root (`Business`). There is no message queue or background worker — everything runs inline in webhook/route handlers, with non-critical writes deferred via fire-and-forget `void promise.then().catch()`.

**Entry points:**

| Surface | Handler | Auth | Tenant resolution |
|---|---|---|---|
| Telnyx Voice (Call Control) | `POST app/api/webhooks/voice/route.ts` | none | `findBusiness(to)` by `telnyxPhoneNumber` (exact, then digits-only fallback) — `voice/route.ts:808` |
| Telnyx SMS | `POST app/api/webhooks/sms/route.ts` | none | `db.business.findFirst({ where: { telnyxPhoneNumber: to } })` — `sms/route.ts:170` |
| Clerk dashboard | `app/(dashboard)/**`, `app/api/dashboard/**` | Clerk session via `requireDashboardBusiness()` | Clerk userId → `User` → `Business` (+ admin view-as cookie) |
| Public booking | `app/book/[businessSlug]`, `POST app/api/bookings/create`, `POST app/api/marketing-bookings` | none (iframe-embeddable) | `businessId` / `businessSlug` from request body |
| Super-admin | `app/admin/**`, `app/api/admin/**` | Clerk + `userId === ADMIN_USER_ID` | per-route check; can act on any `businessId` |

**Data backbone:** `Business` is the tenant root (`schema.prisma:20`). Every other model FK's to it (`Conversation`, `Message`, `Appointment`, `Contact`, `ScreenedCall`, `BlockedNumber`, `ContactCooldown`, `CooldownSkipLog`, `TelnyxUsageRecord`, `Tag`, `Job`, `EmailCampaign`, `Activity`, `WebsiteLead`, `GoogleAdsSnapshot`) with `onDelete: Cascade`. The two non-scoped models are `User` (FK to Business but represents a login) and `PhoneNumber` (a global number pool). Webhook isolation is purely by `telnyxPhoneNumber` uniqueness — one Telnyx number = one tenant.

**Cross-cutting runtime facts:**
- Voice webhook holds **all** Call Control state in base64-encoded `client_state` (`toB64`/`JSON.parse(Buffer.from(...,'base64'))`, `voice/route.ts:92-97, 804`). Telnyx sends JSON events; the handler responds `200` immediately and issues separate `telnyx.calls.actions.*` API calls.
- SMS webhook holds booking state in `Conversation.bookingFlowState` (JSON column) — a persistent state machine across inbound messages.
- AI model everywhere is **`claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`**, `max_tokens: 256` (`sms/route.ts:1033, 2286, 2439`). Booking-mode calls retry up to 3× on HTTP 529 with a 3000 ms delay (`sms/route.ts:2457-2483`); lead-mode and extraction calls do not retry.
- Owner email is sent via **Resend** (`notifications@alignandacquire.com`) in `notify-owner.ts:sendEmail` (line 696-714) — the nodemailer `getTransporter()` exists (line 662-691) but is **dead code, never called**. The voice-webhook voicemail alert also uses Resend directly (`voice/route.ts:730`).
- All outbound SMS DB logging is deferred after `telnyx.messages.send()` succeeds (`sms/route.ts:2542`, `voice/route.ts:1079`, `create-booking.ts:291`) — SMS can be delivered without a `Message` row if the deferred write fails.

### 2. End-to-End Sequence Diagrams

#### (a) Inbound missed call — AI-enabled, no screener, no forwarding → SMS

```
Caller ──► Telnyx ──► POST /api/webhooks/voice  (event: call.initiated, direction inbound)
  voice/route.ts:102  findBusiness(to)                      [DB: business by telnyxPhoneNumber]
  :113  phonesMatch(from, telnyxPhoneNumber)? → forwarding-loop guard (speak msg, return)
  :122  spamFilterEnabled && isSpamCall(from)? → ScreenedCall(blocked) + reject()
  :133  telnyx.calls.actions.answer(callControlId, client_state={businessId,callerPhone,connectionId})
  :135  callScreenerEnabled? NO
  :154  knownContactVoicemailEnabled && isClientVoicemailContact()? NO
  :160  missedCallAiEnabled? YES
        └─► sendMissedCallSMS(telnyx, business, callControlId, from)   voice/route.ts:962
              :970 BlockedNumber match?      → CooldownSkipLog(blocked), return
              :987 isExistingContact()?      → logContactSkip(existing_contact), return   [source IS NULL]
              :994 isCooldownBypassNumber()? → skip cooldown check
              :998 checkCooldown() not allowed? → logCooldownSkip(cooldown), return
              :1005 find/create Conversation (callSid OR same caller <24h)
              :1019 callConnected && duration>5? → skip
              :1024 outbound Message already exists? → skip (idempotency)
              :1053 telnyx.messages.send({from:telnyx#, to:caller, text: aiGreeting})
              :1079 DEFERRED: Message.create(outbound) → recordMessageSent()
  :169  telnyx.calls.actions.speak(missedCallVoiceMessage || DEFAULT_VOICE_MESSAGE)
  :178  return 200

Telnyx ──► POST /api/webhooks/voice  (event: call.speak.ended, no special client_state)
  voice/route.ts:428  telnyx.calls.actions.hangup()
```

#### (b) Spam screener + forwarding → owner answers (bridge)

```
call.initiated (inbound)
  :133 answer(client_state={businessId,callerPhone,connectionId})
  :135 callScreenerEnabled YES
       :136 Conversation.upsert(callSid → status 'screening')
       :144 gatherUsingSpeak("...press 1", minimum_digits=1, maximum_digits=1,
             timeout_millis=8000, valid_digits='0123456789', maximum_tries=1)

call.gather.ended  (payload.digits === '1')
  :454 ScreenedCall(result='passed')
  :460 callScreenerEnabled && forwardingNumber → FORWARDING FLOW
       :464 Conversation.upsert(callSid → status 'forwarding', aLegCallControlId=callControlId)
       :477 ringTimeoutSecs = missedCallAiEnabled ? 25 : 20
       :495 connectionId present → PARALLEL:
            speak(HOLD_MESSAGE_PAYLOAD, client_state fwdState{forwardingPending,dialAlreadyStarted:true})
            telnyx.calls.dial({connection_id, to:forwardingNumber, from:telnyx#,
                               timeout_secs, ringback_tone:true,
                               client_state bLegState{isForwardingLeg, aLegCallControlId, announceCallerPending}})
            Promise.allSettled([speak, dial])  (:510)

B-leg call.answered  (state.isForwardingLeg)        voice/route.ts:184
  :197 look up Contact by phonesMatch(callerPhone) → contactName or spell out digits
  :231 speak(announcement, client_state={...state, announceCallerPending:true})
       (on speak error → bridge immediately + Conversation{callConnected,status 'completed',answeredBy 'human'})

B-leg call.speak.ended (state.announceCallerPending && isForwardingLeg && aLegCallControlId)  :265
  :271 telnyx.calls.actions.bridge(bLeg, {call_control_id: aLeg})
  :274 Conversation.updateMany(callSid=aLeg, status 'forwarding'
        → {callConnected:true, status:'completed', answeredBy:'human'})

B-leg call.hangup (state.isForwardingLeg)            voice/route.ts:596
  :608 conversation.callConnected? → set dialCallStatus 'completed', callEndedAt, durationSeconds; return
```

#### (c) Forwarding goes unanswered → handleForwardingFallback → missed-call SMS

```
B-leg dialed with timeout_secs (25 if AI, 20 if voicemail mode), ringback_tone:true
   ... no answer ...

B-leg call.hangup (state.isForwardingLeg)            voice/route.ts:596
  :604 lookup Conversation by callSid=aLegCallControlId
  :608 callConnected? NO
  :621 status !== 'forwarding'? (fallback already ran) → skip
  :627 handleForwardingFallback(telnyx, state, 'no-answer')

handleForwardingFallback()                            voice/route.ts:828
  :849 IDEMPOTENCY: (callConnected && duration>5) || status!=='forwarding' → skip
  :854 Conversation.update → {status 'active', dialCallStatus 'no-answer', callEndedAt}
  :867 knownContactVoicemailEnabled && isClientVoicemailContact()?
        YES → startClientContactVoicemail(aLeg)   [voicemail + owner alert path]
  :875 ELSE missedCallAiEnabled?
        YES → sendMissedCallSMS(telnyx, business, aLegCallControlId, callerPhone)   (full guard chain)
              then speak(missedCallVoiceMessage || DEFAULT_VOICE_MESSAGE) on A-leg
  :890  NO → speak(VOICEMAIL_GREETING, client_state={voicemailPending:true})   [→ flow (f)]

(also reached from)  call.bridging.failed (state.isForwardingLeg)  voice/route.ts:636
  → hangup(bLeg) + handleForwardingFallback(state, 'failed')
```

#### (d) Customer replies to SMS → AI conversation → BOOKING completed

```
Customer ──► Telnyx ──► POST /api/webhooks/sms  (event: message.received)   sms/route.ts:162
  :170 business by telnyxPhoneNumber(to)
  :177 STOP/UNSUBSCRIBE/CANCEL/QUIT? → opt-out reply, return  (no Message row)
  :185 "never mind"/"not interested"/"no thanks"? → goodbye, status 'closed',
        notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured(service="Partial interest - customer declined"), return
  :227 empty text? → acknowledge, return (don't poison Claude with empty block)
  :237 find Conversation (same caller, createdAt within 90 days, newest by lastMessageAt)
  :248 status ∈ NO_AI_RESPONSE_STATUSES [appointment_booked,lead_captured,closed,
        human_needed,needs_review,completed]? → save inbound, limited canned reply, return
  :303 none found → Conversation.create(status 'active') + void findOrCreateContact(source 'missed_call')
  :325 spam guard: identical inbound text within 30s → ignore
  :338 message-limit guard: !inBookingFlow && messages.length >= maxMessagesPerConversation(25 default)
        → status 'completed', "call us" message, return
  :355 Message.create(inbound); Conversation.lastMessageAt = now
  :368 status now NO_AI_RESPONSE or has appointment? → return
  :376 !calendarEnabled || !smsBookingEnabled → handleSmsLeadFlow() [flow (e)]; returns true only if lead_captured
  :386 handleSmsBookingFlow(business, conversation, text, from)   sms/route.ts:1070

handleSmsBookingFlow — state machine on bookingFlowState.step:
  :1084 !calendarEnabled || !smsBookingEnabled → return false
  :1089 has appointment || appointment_booked → return true (skip)
  Steps (each persists next step to bookingFlowState JSON):
    (no step) detect intent: BOOKING_INTENT_WORDS in msg/history OR (aiOffered && customerAgreeing)
              (:1709-1731) → ask day/time, set step 'awaiting_time' (if name known) or
              'awaiting_name_and_preference'; status 'booking_in_progress'
    awaiting_name_and_preference → parse name+time in one reply (handleAwaitingNameAndPreference :1764)
    awaiting_time            → parseTimePreference → isSpecificSlotAvailable()?
                               open → step 'awaiting_name_after_slot'
                               taken → getTwoClosestSlotsOnDay → suggest 1-2, stay 'awaiting_time'
    awaiting_name_after_slot → parse name → (bookingRequiresAddress) step 'awaiting_address_after_slot'
    awaiting_address_after_slot → capture address → buildConfirmationMessage → step 'awaiting_confirmation'
    awaiting_confirmation    → /yes|yeah|confirm|.../ matched (:1125)
                               :1132 re-check appointment exists → return
                               :1134 createBooking({skipSlotVerification:true, conversationId})

createBooking()                                       create-booking.ts:91
  :116 calendarEnabled / googleCalendarConnected gates (unless allowWithoutCalendar)
  :122 require name/phone/service; :125 valid date; :129 future-slot (TZDate now)
  :134 conversationId already has confirmed appt? → 409
  :145 duplicate (same phone+service ±slotDurationMinutes)? → 409
  :163 DB time-overlap scan (runs even when skipSlotVerification) → 409 on conflict
  :187 !skipSlotVerification && googleCalendarConnected → getAvailableSlots, 60s tolerance match
  :219 createCalendarEvent() — on throw set calendarSyncFailed=true (NON-FATAL)
  :243 Appointment.create(status 'confirmed', source 'sms' if conversationId else 'website')
  :265 telnyx.messages.send confirmation SMS ("You're all set {name}!..."), append calendar-fail note
        :291 DEFERRED Message.create(outbound) + recordMessageSent()
  :311 notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated(business, appointment)   notify-owner.ts:82
        SMS "📅 New Quote Request!..." to ownerPhone||forwardingNumber (normalizeToE164)
        Resend email "New Quote Visit - ..." to ownerEmail
  :327 return {ok:true, appointment, calendarSyncFailed?}

back in handleSmsBookingFlow:1146
  result.ok → Conversation.update(status 'appointment_booked', bookingFlowState DbNull); return true
```

#### (e) Customer replies → LEAD captured (no calendar) → notify owner

```
message.received → (steps :170-:365 as in flow d)
  :376 !calendarEnabled || !smsBookingEnabled → handleSmsLeadFlow() returns false unless already lead_captured
  :386 handleSmsBookingFlow → returns false (calendar not enabled)
  :396 generateAIResponse(business, conversation, text, bookingFlowState, calendarEnabled=false)
        sms/route.ts:2145 → LEAD-ONLY system prompt (:2196), model claude-haiku-4-5-20251001
        Goal: collect name + address + timeframe. Emits [READY_TO_CAPTURE] / [HUMAN_NEEDED].
  :400 aiResult.aiFailed → send AI_FALLBACK_MESSAGE + notifyOwnerOnAIFailed(), return
  :422 strip tags → cleanResponse

  PATH 1 — explicit [READY_TO_CAPTURE]  (:430)
    !calendarEnabled && readyToCapture && status!=='lead_captured'
    :432 extractLeadFromConversation() (Claude JSON: name/email/address/service/timeframe)
    :440 notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured(business, {...})   notify-owner.ts:276
          SMS "New lead! {name} wants {service}..." + Resend email "New Lead - {name} - {service}" w/ transcript
    :453 Conversation.update(status 'lead_captured', customerEmail/Address/Timeframe, bookingFlowState DbNull)
    :467 void findOrCreateContact(source 'missed_call', name/email/address)
    :475 send buildLeadConfirmation(); return

  PATH 2 — [HUMAN_NEEDED: reason="..."]  (:626)
    Conversation.update(status 'human_needed'); notifyOwnerOnHumanNeeded() (SMS "⚠️ needs your help" + email)

  PATH 3 — BACKSTOP  (:656)  when AI forgot [READY_TO_CAPTURE]
    !calendarEnabled && !readyToCapture && !humanNeeded && status not lead/booked
      && inboundCount >= 2
    :666 extractLeadFromConversation() → if hasAddress OR hasTimeframe:
    :679 notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured() — must succeed BEFORE flipping status (else stays 'active', retried next inbound)
    :696 Conversation.update(status 'lead_captured'); send buildLeadConfirmation(); return

  else (:729) send cleanResponse (general AI turn), return
```

#### (f) Spam-screen-only / AI-disabled call → voicemail recording → Resend alert

```
Two entry points set client_state.voicemailPending=true:
  • handleForwardingFallback (missedCallAiEnabled=false) speaks VOICEMAIL_GREETING   voice/route.ts:894
  • startClientContactVoicemail (knownContactVoicemailEnabled + isClientVoicemailContact)  :946
    → upsert Conversation(callSid, status 'active'), client_state adds voicemailReason='client_contact'

call.speak.ended (state.voicemailPending)             voice/route.ts:286
  :289 telnyx.calls.actions.startRecording(format 'mp3', channels 'single', max_length 120,
        timeout_secs 5, play_beep true; carry voicemailReason in client_state if set)

call.recording.saved                                  voice/route.ts:655
  :656 recording_urls.mp3 (or public_recording_urls)
  :659 Conversation by callSid
  :665 fetch mp3 from Telnyx → put('voicemails/{callControlId}.mp3', access 'public', audio/mpeg)
        (Blob fail → fall back to raw Telnyx URL)
  :682 Conversation.update(recordingUrl)
  :691 load business notify fields
  :703 NOTIFY when (!missedCallAiEnabled) OR (voicemailReason === 'client_contact'):
        :705 wait 8000ms for transcription to land
        :710 notifyBySms+ownerPhone → telnyx.messages.send("New voicemail from {caller}\n{transcript}\nView:...")
        :728 notifyByEmail+ownerEmail → Resend.emails.send(from notifications@alignandacquire.com, "New Voicemail - {name}")
  :763 hangup()

call.transcription | call.recording.transcription.saved   voice/route.ts:774
  → Conversation.update(voicemailTranscription = transcription_text/text/content)
```

### 3. Feature-Flag Matrix

All booleans are derived centrally by `getBusinessFeatures()` (`lib/business-features.ts:22`). Webhooks read raw `Business` fields directly; dashboard/analytics read the derived object.

| Business flag (schema.prisma) | Voice webhook behavior | SMS webhook behavior | UI / API gating |
|---|---|---|---|
| `missedCallAiEnabled` (default true) | true → `sendMissedCallSMS` on missed/unanswered (`voice:160,552,875`); false → no SMS, longer ring (20s), voicemail greeting+record on fallback (`voice:890`) | true → AI booking/lead engine runs; false → SMS webhook still matches business but never reaches AI for missed-call convos | `hasMissedCallAi = !== false`; gates Conversations & Website Leads (403 if off), nav items, analytics AI cards |
| `spamFilterEnabled` (default false) | `isSpamCall(from)` → `ScreenedCall(blocked)` + `reject()` at `call.initiated` (`voice:122`) | — | `hasSpamFilter`; `totalCallsMode='screened'`; Blocked-Calls/screening cards |
| `callScreenerEnabled` (default false) | IVR `gatherUsingSpeak("press 1")`; digit 1 → pass; timeout → hangup; wrong → goodbye (`voice:135,438`) | — | `hasIvrScreener`; screening cards; `callScreenerMessage` editable |
| `forwardingNumber` set | with `callScreenerEnabled` → dial B-leg + bridge; alone (no screener) → not dialed (current no-screener path goes straight to SMS/voicemail) | used as owner notify fallback + `getCallUsPhrase` (`sms:102`) | `hasForwarding`; never quoted as customer callback (CRITICAL prompt anchor) |
| `calendarEnabled` + `googleCalendarConnected` | ring timeout still 25s when AI on | both true → `handleSmsBookingFlow` state machine active (`sms:1084`); else lead flow | `hasCalendar`; Appointments dual FeatureGate (locked / needs-setup) |
| `smsBookingEnabled` (default true) | — | false → booking flow skipped even with calendar connected; routes to `handleSmsLeadFlow` lead capture (`sms:376,1064`) | website booking unaffected; SMS = lead-capture only |
| `knownContactVoicemailEnabled` (default false) **[NEW]** | true + `isClientVoicemailContact(from)` → voicemail instead of AI SMS at every fork (`voice:154,329,358,407,513,546,867`); owner alerted even with AI on | — | not surfaced in CLAUDE.md; admin Toggles |
| `massMessagingEnabled` (default false) | — | — | Outreach tab FeatureGate; all campaign/email APIs 403 |
| `googleAdsEnabled` (default false) | — | — | Google Ads nav + `/dashboard/ads`; ads APIs 403 |

`isSpamCall` (`voice:1102`): blocks toll-free prefixes `+1{800,833,844,855,866,877,888}`, <10-digit numbers, and `+1` numbers whose area code starts with 0 or 1.

### 4. Conversation Status Lifecycle

`Conversation.status` default `'active'` (`schema.prisma:244`). Every transition observed in the two webhooks:

| Status | Set by | Transition trigger |
|---|---|---|
| `screening` | `voice:138` (upsert on `call.initiated`) | `callScreenerEnabled` answered, before digit |
| `screening_blocked` | `voice:570` | gather wrong digit / timeout |
| `forwarding` | `voice:471` (upsert on `gather.ended` digit=1) | screener passed + forwardingNumber present |
| `completed` | `voice:248,276` (bridge); `sms:344` (msg-limit) | B-leg bridged (`callConnected:true,answeredBy:'human'`) OR SMS message limit reached |
| `active` | `voice:1037,1032,857` (create/revert); `sms:195,305` | new conversation; forwarding fallback resets `forwarding`→`active`; `screening*`→`active` when SMS finally sends |
| `booking_in_progress` | `sms:511,539,1750` | booking intent detected OR AI guiding/fake-confirming a booking |
| `appointment_booked` | `sms:594,1147` | `createBooking` succeeded (AI-tag path or structured confirm) |
| `lead_captured` | `sms:453,696` | `[READY_TO_CAPTURE]` extraction OR backstop (≥2 inbound + address/timeframe), after owner notified |
| `human_needed` | `sms:629` | AI emitted `[HUMAN_NEEDED]` |
| `closed` | `sms:204,279,297` | "never mind"; or straggler reply after `appointment_booked`/`lead_captured` |

`NO_AI_RESPONSE_STATUSES` (`sms:233`) = `[appointment_booked, lead_captured, closed, human_needed, needs_review, completed]` — inbound to any of these skips the AI engine (canned replies only). Note `needs_review` and `no_response` appear in the set/schema comment but no code path *sets* them. `bookingFlowState` is cleared to `Prisma.DbNull` on `appointment_booked` and `lead_captured`.

### Key gotchas confirmed in code
- **Owner email = Resend, not SMTP.** `notify-owner.ts:sendEmail` (line 696) sends via `new Resend(...).emails.send({from:'notifications@alignandacquire.com'})`. The nodemailer `getTransporter()` (line 662-691) is never invoked — `SMTP_*` env vars are effectively unused for owner notifications. CLAUDE.md's claim that owner notifications go via nodemailer/Gmail SMTP is wrong.
- **Default message limit constant is 25, DB default is 23.** `DEFAULT_MAX_MESSAGES_PER_CONVERSATION = 25` (`sms:91`) is the fallback when `business.maxMessagesPerConversation` is null; the column default is `23` (`schema.prisma:79`). CLAUDE.md says 23 for the code default.
- **Missed-call conversation lookback is 24h, SMS-reply lookback is 90 days.** `sendMissedCallSMS` matches an existing conversation within 24h (`voice:1012`); the SMS handler reuses conversations within 90 days (`sms:241`).
- **`createBooking` always runs a DB overlap scan** (`create-booking.ts:163`) even when `skipSlotVerification` is true, preventing SMS/website slot races. CLAUDE.md only documents the 60s Calendar tolerance.
- **Idempotency everywhere:** outbound-Message-exists short-circuit in `sendMissedCallSMS` (`voice:1024`); `appointment` re-check before each booking write (`sms:1132,1311,1385`); `handleForwardingFallback` status/`callConnected` guard (`voice:849`).

## 2. Database Schema (Prisma)

Single schema file: `prisma/schema.prisma` (581 lines). There is **no `prisma/migrations/` directory** — the project syncs schema with `prisma db push` (per the file header comment line 5: "Run `npm run db:push` to sync this to your database"). Raw-SQL equivalents of recent additive changes live in `scripts/sql/`:

| File | Effect |
|---|---|
| `scripts/sql/2026-06-02_add_isClientContact.sql` | `ALTER TABLE "Contact" ADD COLUMN "isClientContact" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false;` (followed by a separate, un-committed Step-2 backfill to flip the client's own null-source contacts to true) |
| `scripts/sql/2026-06-02_add_knownContactVoicemailEnabled.sql` | `ALTER TABLE "Business" ADD COLUMN "knownContactVoicemailEnabled" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false;` |

### Datasource & Generator

```prisma
generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider  = "postgresql"
  url       = env("DATABASE_URL")   // pooled (runtime)
  directUrl = env("DIRECT_URL")     // direct/non-pooled (db push / migrations)
}
```

There are **20 models**. Every model except `User` and `PhoneNumber` hangs off `Business` via a `businessId` FK. (`User` also FKs to `Business`; the only model with no `businessId` is `PhoneNumber`, plus the two join/child models `Message`, `ContactTag`, `EmailRecipient` which reach Business transitively.)

### Relation map (what hangs off `Business`)

`Business` declares these one-to-many back-relations (all child→Business FKs are `onDelete: Cascade`):

```
Business
├── User[]                (users)            Cascade
├── Conversation[]        (conversations)    Cascade
│     ├── Message[]                          Cascade (Message→Conversation)
│     └── Appointment?                       SetNull (Appointment→Conversation)
├── Appointment[]         (appointments)     Cascade (Appointment→Business)
├── ScreenedCall[]        (screenedCalls)    Cascade
├── BlockedNumber[]       (blockedNumbers)   Cascade
├── Contact[]             (contacts)         Cascade
│     ├── ContactTag[]    (contactTags)      Cascade
│     ├── Job[]           (jobs)             Cascade
│     ├── EmailRecipient[](emailRecipients)  Cascade
│     └── Activity[]      (activities)       Cascade
├── ContactCooldown[]     (contactCooldowns) Cascade
├── CooldownSkipLog[]     (cooldownSkipLogs) Cascade
├── TelnyxUsageRecord[]   (telnyxUsageRecords) Cascade
├── Tag[]                 (tags)             Cascade
│     └── ContactTag[]    (contacts)         Cascade (ContactTag→Tag)
├── Job[]                 (jobs)             Cascade
├── EmailCampaign[]       (emailCampaigns)   Cascade
│     └── EmailRecipient[](recipients)       Cascade
├── Activity[]            (activities)       Cascade
├── WebsiteLead[]         (websiteLeads)     Cascade
└── GoogleAdsSnapshot[]   (googleAdsSnapshots) Cascade

PhoneNumber  — standalone, no relation to any model (assignment is by plain string assignedToBusinessId, NOT a FK)
```

**`onDelete` summary:** all relations are `Cascade` EXCEPT `Appointment.conversation` which is `onDelete: SetNull` (deleting a Conversation nulls the appointment's `conversationId` rather than deleting the appointment). `ContactTag` cascades from both `Contact` and `Tag`.

## 3. Voice / Telnyx Call Control Flow

The "missed call" trigger. Four route handlers; the primary one is `app/api/webhooks/voice/route.ts` (1112 lines), the single endpoint that receives **all** Telnyx Call Control events. Telnyx sends JSON webhooks; the handler responds `200` immediately and issues separate Telnyx API calls (`answer`, `speak`, `gatherUsingSpeak`, `dial`, `bridge`, `startRecording`, `hangup`, `reject`) to control the call. State between events travels in `client_state` (base64-encoded JSON). The other three (`voice-gather`, `voice-after-dial`, `voice-dial-status`) are XML-mode / status-callback handlers that are **not wired into the Call Control flow above** — `route.ts` never sets a callback URL pointing at them; they are legacy/alternate-config endpoints.

### Module-level constants (`route.ts:47-58`)

| Const | Value |
|---|---|
| `VOICE` | `'AWS.Polly.Joanna'` — every `speak`/`gatherUsingSpeak` uses this voice |
| `DEFAULT_VOICE_MESSAGE` | `"We're sorry we can't get to the phone right now. You should receive a text message shortly."` (AI-enabled fallback) |
| `NO_SMS_VOICE_MESSAGE` | `"We're sorry, no one is available. Please try again later. Goodbye."` (AI-disabled fallback) |
| `VOICEMAIL_GREETING` | `"Sorry, no one is available to take your call right now. Please leave a message after the tone."` |
| `FORWARDING_TIMEOUT_SECS` | `25` — B-leg ring timeout when `missedCallAiEnabled` (ring out fast → SMS) |
| `FORWARDING_TIMEOUT_VOICEMAIL_SECS` | `20` — B-leg ring timeout when AI disabled (longer ring → owner voicemail, ~4-5 rings) |
| `HOLD_MESSAGE_PAYLOAD` | Long "We are connecting you. . . . Please stay on the line. . . ." dotted string — TTS hold filler; length intentional so hold continues until B-leg answers/times out |

AMD (answering machine detection) is **intentionally not used** in `route.ts` — it produces false positives with Google Voice / carrier voicemail. Instead a short ring timeout (25s) bounces unanswered calls into the SMS flow.

### ClientState interface (`route.ts:60-71`)

```typescript
interface ClientState {
  businessId?: string            // set on every call after call.initiated
  callerPhone?: string           // caller's From (+1XXXXXXXXXX)
  connectionId?: string          // Telnyx connection_id, for outbound dial
  forwardingPending?: boolean    // after "please hold" speak → dial B-leg on speak.ended
  dialAlreadyStarted?: boolean   // true when B-leg was dialed in parallel with hold speak
  isForwardingLeg?: boolean      // marks the B-leg's client_state
  aLegCallControlId?: string     // B-leg stores A-leg's id, used for bridging
  voicemailPending?: boolean     // after voicemail greeting speak → startRecording on speak.ended
  announceCallerPending?: boolean// B-leg: after "Connecting to..." speak → bridge on speak.ended
  voicemailReason?: string       // 'client_contact' when voicemail chosen via known-contact routing
}
```

**Encoding:** `toB64(obj)` = `Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(obj)).toString('base64')` (`route.ts:804`). **Decoding:** `JSON.parse(Buffer.from(rawClientState, 'base64').toString())` wrapped in `try/catch {}` (`route.ts:93-97`) — a malformed `client_state` silently yields `state = {}`.

> **NEW vs CLAUDE.md:** `voicemailReason` is a 10th ClientState field not documented. The whole known-contact-voicemail subsystem (`knownContactVoicemailEnabled`, `isClientVoicemailContact`, `startClientContactVoicemail`, `voicemailReason='client_contact'`) is new and undocumented.

### Webhook event dispatch table (`route.ts`)

All branches are checked top-to-bottom in one `POST`. Every path returns `200`. The outer `try/catch` also returns `200` on any thrown error (`route.ts:794-797`), so Telnyx never retries.

| Event (+ guard) | Lines | Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `call.initiated` & `direction in (inbound, incoming)` | 102-179 | Entry point — full branching below |
| `call.answered` & `state.isForwardingLeg` | 184-258 | B-leg answered → caller announce → bridge on speak.ended |
| `call.speak.ended` | 263-433 | Multiplexed by state flags (announce / voicemail / forwarding / default-hangup) |
| `call.gather.ended` | 438-591 | IVR digit handling |
| `call.hangup` & `state.isForwardingLeg` | 596-631 | B-leg hangup → completed-record or `handleForwardingFallback` |
| `call.bridging.failed` & `state.isForwardingLeg` | 636-650 | hangup B-leg + `handleForwardingFallback('failed')` |
| `call.recording.saved` | 655-768 | fetch mp3 → Vercel Blob → save URL → owner voicemail notify → hangup |
| `call.transcription` OR `call.recording.transcription.saved` | 774-790 | save transcript text to `Conversation.voicemailTranscription` |
| any other event | 793 | acknowledge `200` |

### `call.initiated` branch logic (`route.ts:102-179`)

1. `findBusiness(to)` (`route.ts:808-821`): exact `telnyxPhoneNumber == to`, else digit-normalized (`replace(/\D/g,'')`) match across all businesses with a non-null number. No business → `reject({cause:'CALL_REJECTED'})`, return.
2. **Forwarding-loop prevention** (`route.ts:113-120`): if `phonesMatch(from, business.telnyxPhoneNumber)` (Telnyx calling itself), `answer` + `speak(missedCallVoiceMessage || NO_SMS_VOICE_MESSAGE)`, return. **No conversation, no SMS.**
3. **Spam filter** (`route.ts:122-129`): if `business.spamFilterEnabled && isSpamCall(from)` → create `ScreenedCall{result:'blocked'}`, `reject({cause:'CALL_REJECTED'})`, return.
4. `answer(callControlId, {client_state})` with `{businessId, callerPhone:from, connectionId}`.
5. Branch on `business.callScreenerEnabled`:
   - **true** → `upsert` Conversation `{status:'screening', callSid:callControlId}`, then `gatherUsingSpeak` (config below).
   - **false** → compute `routeToVoicemail = knownContactVoicemailEnabled && isClientVoicemailContact(businessId, from)`. If true → `startClientContactVoicemail`. Else: if `missedCallAiEnabled` → `sendMissedCallSMS` (async-awaited); then `speak(normalMsg)` where `normalMsg = missedCallAiEnabled ? (missedCallVoiceMessage||DEFAULT_VOICE_MESSAGE) : (missedCallVoiceMessage||NO_SMS_VOICE_MESSAGE)`.

### `isSpamCall(phone)` heuristic (`route.ts:1102-1112`)

```typescript
function isSpamCall(phone: string): boolean {
  const tollFreePatterns = ['+1833','+1844','+1855','+1866','+1877','+1888','+1800']
  if (tollFreePatterns.some(p => phone.startsWith(p))) return true   // toll-free
  const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, '')
  if (digits.length < 10) return true                                 // too short
  if (phone.startsWith('+1')) {
    const area = digits.substring(1, 4)                               // area code
    if (area.startsWith('0') || area.startsWith('1')) return true     // invalid US area code
  }
  return false
}
```
> **DRIFT vs CLAUDE.md:** the toll-free list is `833,844,855,866,877,888,800` — it does **not** include `+1822` and there is **no `+1888`-vs-list** difference, but note CLAUDE.md's Flow-2 prose lists the same set. Accurate. (No `+1822`.)

### IVR / call-screener gather config (`route.ts:144-152`)

```typescript
gatherUsingSpeak(callControlId, {
  payload: business.callScreenerMessage || `Thank you for calling ${business.name}. To be connected, please press 1.`,
  voice: VOICE,
  minimum_digits: 1,
  maximum_digits: 1,
  timeout_millis: 8000,        // 8s
  valid_digits: '0123456789',
  maximum_tries: 1,            // never repeats the prompt
})
```

### `call.gather.ended` branch (`route.ts:438-591`)

- No `state.businessId` or business not found → `hangup`, return.
- `digits === '1'` (passed): create `ScreenedCall{result:'passed'}`, then:
  - **`callScreenerEnabled && forwardingNumber`** → **forwarding flow** (`route.ts:460-543`): `upsert` Conversation `{status:'forwarding', aLegCallControlId:callControlId}`. `connectionId = state.connectionId || TELNYX_CONNECTION_ID`. `ringTimeoutSecs = missedCallAiEnabled ? 25 : 20`. If `connectionId` present → **parallel** `Promise.allSettled([speak(HOLD_MESSAGE_PAYLOAD, client_state=fwdState{forwardingPending:true,dialAlreadyStarted:true}), dial(...)])`. If `dialResult.rejected` → known-contact-voicemail-or-`sendMissedCallSMS`-or-skip + speak fallback. If no `connectionId` → `speak(HOLD_MESSAGE_PAYLOAD, fwdState{dialAlreadyStarted:false})` only (B-leg dialed later on `speak.ended`).
  - **else (no forwarding number)** → **standard screening flow** (`route.ts:545-565`): `routeToVoicemail` check → `startClientContactVoicemail`, else `sendMissedCallSMS` (if AI on) + `speak(missedMsg)`.
- `digits !== '1'` (`route.ts:567-585`): `noInput = digits == null || digits === ''`. Update Conversation `status='screening_blocked'`, create `ScreenedCall{result:'blocked'}`. If `noInput` (robocall) → `hangup` immediately, no message, no SMS. Else (wrong key) → `speak('Thanks for calling. Goodbye.')` (hung up on subsequent `speak.ended`).

**dial config** (both parallel `route.ts:502-509` and sequential `route.ts:395-402`):
```typescript
telnyx.calls.dial({
  connection_id: connectionId,
  to: business.forwardingNumber,
  from: business.telnyxPhoneNumber!,
  timeout_secs: ringTimeoutSecs,        // 25 or 20
  client_state: bLegState,              // {isForwardingLeg:true, aLegCallControlId, announceCallerPending:true, ...}
  ringback_tone: true,                  // caller hears ringing
})
```

### `call.speak.ended` multiplexer (`route.ts:263-433`)

Checked in order:
1. **`announceCallerPending && isForwardingLeg && aLegCallControlId`** (`route.ts:265-283`): B-leg's "Connecting to..." finished → `bridge(bLegId, {call_control_id: aLegId})`, then `updateMany` Conversation where `{callSid:aLegId, status:'forwarding'}` → `{callConnected:true, status:'completed', answeredBy:'human'}`. On bridge error → `handleForwardingFallback('failed')`.
2. **`voicemailPending`** (`route.ts:286-316`): voicemail greeting finished → `startRecording`:
   ```typescript
   startRecording(callControlId, { format:'mp3', channels:'single', max_length:120, timeout_secs:5, play_beep:true,
     ...(state.voicemailReason ? { client_state: toB64({businessId, callerPhone, voicemailPending:true, voicemailReason}) } : {}) })
   ```
   (the `client_state` is **omitted** unless `voicemailReason` is set, keeping the legacy spam-screening path byte-identical). On error → `hangup`.
3. **`forwardingPending`** (`route.ts:318-427`): the sequential-dial fallback (only reached when parallel dial wasn't possible). If `dialAlreadyStarted` → no-op. Else re-fetch business; if no `forwardingNumber` or no `connectionId` → voicemail/SMS/skip fallback. Else `dial(...)` the B-leg. On `dial` error → voicemail/SMS/skip + speak fallback.
4. **else** (`route.ts:429`): `hangup` — this is how the simple no-screener flow and the wrong-key "Goodbye." both terminate.

### `call.answered` (B-leg, `route.ts:184-258`)

Builds caller announcement. Strips `state.callerPhone` to digits; if a `Contact` (any source) in this business `phonesMatch`es and has a `name` → `"Connecting to {name}"`. Else formats the 10-digit number as `"Connecting to {area} {prefix} {line}"`, or digit-by-digit, or `"Connecting to unknown caller"`. Then `speak(announcement, client_state={...state, announceCallerPending:true})`. If the speak call throws → bridge immediately and mark Conversation `completed`; if that bridge also throws → `handleForwardingFallback('failed')`.

### `call.hangup` (B-leg, `route.ts:596-631`)

If `aLegCallControlId` set, look up Conversation by `callSid:aLegId`:
- `conversation.callConnected` → call ended normally: update `dialCallStatus:'completed', callEndedAt:now, durationSeconds:payload.duration_secs`, return.
- `conversation.status !== 'forwarding'` → fallback already handled, return.
- Otherwise → `handleForwardingFallback('no-answer')`.

### `handleForwardingFallback(telnyx, state, dialCallStatus='no-answer', answeredBy?, timing?)` (`route.ts:828-910`)

Guard: requires `businessId && callerPhone && aLegCallControlId`. Loads Conversation by `callSid:aLegCallControlId`. **Idempotency:** skips if `(callConnected && durationSeconds > 5) || status !== 'forwarding'` (`route.ts:849`). Otherwise updates Conversation `{status:'active', dialCallStatus, answeredBy, callEndedAt:now}`, then:
- `routeToVoicemail` (`knownContactVoicemailEnabled && isClientVoicemailContact`) → `startClientContactVoicemail(aLegId)`.
- else if `missedCallAiEnabled` → `sendMissedCallSMS(aLegId)` + `speak(missedCallVoiceMessage || DEFAULT_VOICE_MESSAGE)` on A-leg (on speak error, hangup).
- else (spam-screening-only) → `speak(VOICEMAIL_GREETING, client_state={voicemailPending:true})` → recording flow begins.

### `sendMissedCallSMS(telnyx, business, callControlId, callerPhone, timing?)` — ordered guard checks (`route.ts:962-1100`)

| # | Check | Action if hit | Line |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `BlockedNumber` exists `{businessId, phoneNumber:callerPhone}` (exact match, not normalized) | create `CooldownSkipLog{reason:'blocked', lastMessageSent:Date(0), messageType:'missed_call'}`, return | 970-984 |
| 1 | `isExistingContact()` — Contact with `source IS NULL` (normalized last-10 match) | `logContactSkip(reason='existing_contact')`, return | 987-991 |
| 2 | `isCooldownBypassNumber(callerPhone, cooldownBypassNumbers)` | log, skip cooldown (continue) | 994-995 |
| 3 | `checkCooldown()` — `ContactCooldown.lastMessageSent` within cooldown window (default 7d, `business.smsCooldownDays` > `SMS_COOLDOWN_DAYS` env > 7) | `logCooldownSkip`, return | 997-1003 |
| 4 | find Conversation: `callSid==callControlId` OR `{businessId, callerPhone, createdAt >= now-24h}` | — | 1005-1016 |
| 5 | if found & `callConnected && durationSeconds > 5` | skip (owner talked), return | 1019-1022 |
| 6 | if found & outbound `Message` already exists | skip (idempotent), return | 1024-1030 |
| 7 | if found & `status in (screening, screening_blocked)` | update `status='active'` | 1031-1033 |
| — | else create Conversation `{status:'active', callSid}` (on unique-constraint race, re-find by callSid) | — | 1034-1043 |
| 8 | `telnyx.messages.send({from:telnyxPhoneNumber, to:callerPhone, text:greeting})` | greeting = `aiGreeting || "Sorry we missed your call at {name}. How can we help?"` | 1045-1057 |
| 9 | **Deferred (`void`, not awaited):** `Message.create({direction:'outbound', content, telnyxSid, telnyxStatus})` `.then(recordMessageSent)` `.catch(log)` | SMS already sent; DB write failure is silent | 1078-1090 |

> **Deferred-write gotcha:** if the deferred `Message.create` / `recordMessageSent` fails, the SMS was still sent but won't appear in the dashboard and the cooldown won't be recorded. Intentional, to keep the webhook fast.

### `startClientContactVoicemail(telnyx, callControlId, businessId, callerPhone)` (`route.ts:929-960`)

Known-contact voicemail entry. `upsert` Conversation on `callSid` (`create:{status:'active'}`, `update:{}`) so `call.recording.saved` (which only UPDATEs by callSid, never creates) has a row. Then `speak(VOICEMAIL_GREETING, client_state={voicemailPending:true, voicemailReason:'client_contact'})`. Speak errors are swallowed (caller may have hung up — Telnyx error 90018).

### `call.recording.saved` → voicemail persistence + notify (`route.ts:655-768`)

`recordingUrls = payload.recording_urls ?? payload.public_recording_urls`; `telnyxRecordingUrl = recordingUrls?.mp3`. Find Conversation by `callSid`. `fetch(telnyxRecordingUrl)` → `Buffer` → `put('voicemails/{callControlId}.mp3', buffer, {access:'public', contentType:'audio/mpeg'})` (Vercel Blob; requires `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN`). On any fetch/upload error, falls back to saving the raw Telnyx URL. Update `Conversation.recordingUrl`.

**Owner notification condition** (`route.ts:703`): `!business.missedCallAiEnabled || state.voicemailReason === 'client_contact'`. If met: `await sleep(8000)` (let transcription land), re-read Conversation for `voicemailTranscription`, then:
- **SMS** (if `notifyBySms && ownerPhone && telnyxPhoneNumber`): `telnyx.messages.send` body = `"New voicemail from {callerPhone}\n{transcript||'Transcription not available'}\nView: https://www.alignandacquire.com/dashboard/voicemails"`.
- **Email** (if `notifyByEmail && ownerEmail`): **Resend** (`new Resend(RESEND_API_KEY)`), from `notifications@alignandacquire.com`, subject `"New Voicemail - {name}"`, HTML body with from/date/transcript (HTML-escaped) + dashboard link.

Finally `hangup`.

> **DRIFT:** the voicemail email is sent via **Resend** here (not nodemailer), and is now sent for **known-contact voicemails even while `missedCallAiEnabled` is true** — CLAUDE.md says voicemail notification only fires when `missedCallAiEnabled == false`.

### `call.transcription` / `call.recording.transcription.saved` (`route.ts:774-790`)

`text = payload.transcription_text ?? payload.text ?? payload.content`. If a Conversation matches `callSid`, save to `voicemailTranscription`.

---

### ASCII flows

**Flow 1 — Simple (no screener, no forwarding, AI on):**
```
call.initiated(inbound) → findBusiness → answer
   ├─ loop-guard? caller==own number → speak msg, END (no conv/SMS)
   ├─ spam? → ScreenedCall(blocked) + reject, END
   └─ known-contact? → startClientContactVoicemail
      else → sendMissedCallSMS (guards 0-9) → speak(DEFAULT_VOICE_MESSAGE)
call.speak.ended (default branch) → hangup
```

**Flow 2 — IVR screener, no forwarding number:**
```
call.initiated → answer → Conversation(screening) → gatherUsingSpeak("press 1", 8s, tries=1)
call.gather.ended:
   digits=='1'  → ScreenedCall(passed) → [known-contact→voicemail | sendMissedCallSMS] → speak(missedMsg)
                  └─ call.speak.ended → hangup
   digits==''   → Conversation(screening_blocked) + ScreenedCall(blocked) → hangup (silent, no SMS)
   digits!='1'  → Conversation(screening_blocked) + ScreenedCall(blocked) → speak("Goodbye")
                  └─ call.speak.ended → hangup
```

**Flow 3 — IVR screener + forwarding (parallel optimization):**
```
call.initiated → answer → Conversation(screening) → gatherUsingSpeak
call.gather.ended (digits=='1') → ScreenedCall(passed) → Conversation(forwarding, aLeg=cc)
   connectionId present → Promise.allSettled[
        speak(HOLD_MESSAGE, fwdState{forwardingPending,dialAlreadyStarted:true}),
        dial(forwardingNumber, timeout=25/20, bLegState{isForwardingLeg,aLeg,announceCallerPending}, ringback) ]
        dial rejected → fallback (voicemail|SMS) + speak
   connectionId absent → speak(HOLD_MESSAGE, fwdState{dialAlreadyStarted:false})
        └─ call.speak.ended(forwardingPending,!dialAlreadyStarted) → dial(B-leg)
B-leg call.answered(isForwardingLeg) → resolve name/number → speak("Connecting to ...", announceCallerPending)
B-leg call.speak.ended(announceCallerPending) → bridge(B,A) → Conversation(callConnected,completed,human)
B-leg call.hangup(isForwardingLeg):
   callConnected → mark completed, END
   status!='forwarding' → already handled, END
   else → handleForwardingFallback('no-answer')
call.bridging.failed(isForwardingLeg) → hangup(B) → handleForwardingFallback('failed')
```

**Flow 4 — Forwarding fallback / voicemail (AI off):**
```
handleForwardingFallback → Conversation(active, dialCallStatus, callEndedAt)
   known-contact → startClientContactVoicemail(aLeg)
   AI on  → sendMissedCallSMS(aLeg) + speak(DEFAULT_VOICE_MESSAGE)
   AI off → speak(VOICEMAIL_GREETING, voicemailPending)
            └─ call.speak.ended(voicemailPending) → startRecording(mp3,120s,beep)
               └─ call.recording.saved → fetch mp3 → Vercel Blob → save URL
                  → if(!AI || voicemailReason=='client_contact'): sleep 8s → notify owner (Telnyx SMS + Resend email)
                  → hangup
            call.transcription / .recording.transcription.saved → save voicemailTranscription
```

---

### The three non-Call-Control handlers (legacy / XML mode)

These return TwiML-style XML or status `200` and are **not referenced** by `route.ts`. They exist for a Telnyx config where the number runs in XML/dial-status-callback mode.

**`voice-gather/route.ts`** — XML gather callback. Reads `businessId` & `callSid` from query, `digits` & `from` from body. `digits==='1'` → `ScreenedCall(passed)`; if `missedCallAiEnabled===false` → XML `<Say>Please hold while we connect…</Say>`; else `triggerMissedCallSMS()` (same guard chain: blocked → `isExistingContact` → bypass → cooldown → 24h-existing-conversation check → create Conversation → `messages.send` → **deferred** Message+`recordMessageSent`), then XML `<Say>…we will text you shortly…Goodbye.</Say>`. Non-`'1'` → `Conversation(screening_blocked)` + `ScreenedCall(blocked)` + XML goodbye. Greeting default here differs: `"Hi! Sorry we missed your call at {name}. I'm an automated assistant - how can I help you today?"`.

**`voice-after-dial/route.ts`** — XML after-dial. `state = payload.state`, `duration = duration_secs ?? duration`. `silentHangup = state==='completed' && duration>0` → `<Response><Hangup/></Response>`; else `<Response><Say>{missedCallVoiceMessage||DEFAULT}</Say><Hangup/></Response>` (XML-escaped). **No DB writes.**

**`voice-dial-status/route.ts`** — dial-outcome status callback (`callSid`, `businessId`, `callerPhone` from query). Early-returns if `missedCallAiEnabled===false`, blocked, or `isExistingContact`. Upserts the parent Conversation with `dialCallStatus / answeredBy / durationSeconds / callEndedAt`. SMS trigger logic: `isTriggerStatus = status in (no-answer, busy, failed)` (always SMS, **no duration check**); `isCompletedMachine = status==='completed' && answeredBy.includes('machine'|'unknown')` (SMS only if `durationSeconds >= 2`; `null` duration → skip). `completed` + non-machine → set Conversation `status='completed'`, no SMS. On trigger: 24h existing-conversation guard (excluding the call record), set `status='no_response'`, cooldown bypass/check, `messages.send`, deferred Message+`recordMessageSent`. Greeting default matches voice-gather's.

> **DRIFT — `voice-dial-status` minimum duration:** CLAUDE.md says `completed + machine` triggers SMS "if duration >= 2s" — confirmed (`minDuration = 2`, `route.ts:177`). But CLAUDE.md omits that `no-answer/busy/failed` have **no** duration check and that `answeredBy.includes('unknown')` also counts as machine.

---

## 4. SMS Webhook Inbound Pipeline, Delivery Status, and Send Helpers

**File:** `app/api/webhooks/sms/route.ts` (2555 lines). Single Next.js route handler exporting `POST`. No `GET`. No webhook signature verification (open endpoint). Imports `Telnyx`, `Anthropic`, Prisma `db`, `date-fns` (`addDays/addMonths/addYears`), `@date-fns/tz` `TZDate`, calendar helpers, `createBooking`, `cleanServiceForOwner`, owner-notify functions, `recordMessageSent`, `formatPhoneNumber`, `findOrCreateContact`.

Module-level Anthropic client: `const anthropic = new Anthropic({ apiKey: process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY ?? '' })` (route.ts:22). Telnyx clients are instantiated per-send inside `sendSMS`/`sendSMSAndLog`, not module-level.

### Key module constants

| Constant | Value | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `DEFAULT_MAX_MESSAGES_PER_CONVERSATION` | `25` (route.ts:91) | Fallback only when `business.maxMessagesPerConversation` is null/undefined. Prisma default is **23**, so effective limit is usually 23. |
| `SPAM_WINDOW_SECONDS` | `30` (route.ts:115) | Duplicate-content guard window. |
| `CONVERSATION_TIMEOUT_HOURS` | `24` (route.ts:114) | Declared but **not referenced** anywhere in the handler. |
| `BOOKING_INTENT_WORDS` | array (route.ts:92-99) | book, appointment, schedule, booking, reserve, quote, estimate, come out, come by, stop by, take a look, set up, set me up, sign me up, available, availability, free quote, free estimate, in-person, in person, get on the schedule, when can you, what times, open slots. |
| `AI_FALLBACK_MESSAGE` | "Thanks for reaching out! Let me have someone from the team get back to you shortly." (route.ts:2143) | |
| `closedWindowDays` | `90` (route.ts:234, local) | Conversation lookback window. |
| Anthropic model id | `'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001'` | Used in all 3 Anthropic calls (route.ts:1033, 2286, 2439). `max_tokens: 256` everywhere. |

### POST entry + event-type routing

```
POST(request)
  timing = createTiming()                    // { webhookReceivedAt, now() }
  body = await request.json()
  eventType = body.data?.event_type
  payload   = body.data?.payload
```

Three branches, all returning HTTP 200 on success; a top-level try/catch returns `new NextResponse('Error', { status: 500 })` on throw (route.ts:742-745).

1. **Delivery status** (route.ts:148-159): when `eventType === 'message.finalized' || eventType === 'message.sent'`.
   - `messageId = payload.id`; `status = payload.to[0].status`.
   - If both present: `db.message.updateMany({ where: { telnyxSid: messageId }, data: { telnyxStatus: status } })`. Returns `'OK'` 200.
2. **Inbound SMS** (route.ts:162-738): when `eventType === 'message.received'` — the entire pipeline below.
3. **Any other event** (route.ts:741): returns `'OK'` 200.

### Inbound field extraction & business lookup

```
messageSid = payload.id
from = payload.from.phone_number ?? payload.from     // customer E.164
to   = payload.to[0].phone_number ?? payload.to      // the Telnyx number that received it
text = payload.text
business = await db.business.findFirst({ where: { telnyxPhoneNumber: to } })
```
If no business matches `to`, returns `'OK'` 200 (route.ts:170-174). This is the multi-tenant routing key.

### Opt-out (STOP / UNSUBSCRIBE / CANCEL / QUIT)

route.ts:177-182. **Exact-match only**: `['stop','unsubscribe','cancel','quit'].includes(text?.toLowerCase().trim())`. On match: `await sendSMS(business, from, "You've been unsubscribed. Reply START to resubscribe.")` and return 200. No suppression-list DB write; `START` is never handled in this file (resubscribe is a no-op aside from the fact that the next message just processes normally). "please stop" / "STOP texting me" do **not** match — they fall through to AI.

### "Never mind" / "not interested"

route.ts:185-222. **Contains-match**: any of `['never mind','nevermind','not interested','no thanks','no thank you']` appears in the lowercased trimmed text. On match:
- Finds latest conversation by `(businessId, callerPhone)` ordered by `lastMessageAt desc` (no 90-day filter here), creates one (`status:'active'`) if none.
- Saves inbound `Message`.
- Sends goodbye via **`sendSMSAndLog`** (this IS logged): `'No worries! If you change your mind, feel free to reach out anytime.'`
- Updates conversation `status:'closed'`, `summary:'Customer said not interested'`.
- Calls `notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured(...)` with `service:'Partial interest - customer declined'` and the full transcript (wrapped in try/catch). Returns 200.

### Empty inbound guard

route.ts:227-230. If `!text || !text.trim()` → log and return 200 **without saving**. Prevents blank bubbles and Anthropic 400s on empty content blocks.

### Conversation lookup (90-day) + status-based early returns

route.ts:232-300.
```
NO_AI_RESPONSE_STATUSES = ['appointment_booked','lead_captured','closed','human_needed','needs_review','completed']
conversation = db.conversation.findFirst({
  where: { businessId, callerPhone: from, createdAt: { gte: now - 90d } },
  orderBy: { lastMessageAt: 'desc' },
  include: { messages: { orderBy: { createdAt:'asc' }, take: 50 }, appointment: true },
})
```
If a conversation is found **and** its status ∈ `NO_AI_RESPONSE_STATUSES` (route.ts:248): saves the inbound message, bumps `lastMessageAt`, then branches:
- **`appointment_booked`** (route.ts:256-279): detects a question (contains `?` or matches `/is he|will you|are you|when|where|what time|who is coming|coming to|show up/`). If a question and an appointment exists, replies with the booked address/date/time. Else replies "You're welcome! If anything comes up, just {getCallUsPhrase}." Then sets `status:'closed'`. (Uses `sendSMSAndLog`.)
- **`lead_captured`** (route.ts:280-298): detects pleasantry (long phrases like 'thank','thanks','appreciate','sounds good' contains-match; short tokens 'ty','thx' whole-word). Pleasantry → "Anytime! Talk soon." else → "Got it, I'll pass that along. Someone from {name} will be in touch with you soon!" Then sets `status:'closed'`. **Never re-notifies owner.**

Returns 200 after handling.

If no conversation found (route.ts:303-314): creates `status:'active'` and fires `void findOrCreateContact({ businessId, phoneNumber: from, source:'missed_call' })` (background, errors swallowed).

Then re-fetches full conversation (route.ts:317-322) with messages asc + appointment.

### Spam/duplicate guard (30s)

route.ts:324-334. Finds an existing inbound message with identical `content` and `createdAt > now - 30s`. If found → log + return 200 (ignore).

### Message-limit cutoff + in-booking-flow exemption

route.ts:336-352.
```
maxMessages = business.maxMessagesPerConversation ?? 25
inBookingFlow = Boolean((conversation.bookingFlowState)?.step)
if (!inBookingFlow && conversation.messages.length >= maxMessages):
    status='completed', summary='Conversation ended - message limit reached'
    sendSMS(business, from, `Thanks for chatting! For further help, please ${getCallUsPhrase(business)}.`)  // NOT logged
    return 200
```
The exemption means an in-progress booking is never cut off.

### Save inbound + final pre-routing status check

route.ts:354-370. Saves inbound `Message`, bumps `lastMessageAt`, re-fetches. Then: if status ∈ `NO_AI_RESPONSE_STATUSES` **or** `conversation.appointment` exists → return 200 (no AI). (Second guard, since status could have changed.)

### Routing decision tree (handoff points)

route.ts:372-396. Order:

| Step | Condition | Handler | On true |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Lead-flow short-circuit | `!business.calendarEnabled || !business.smsBookingEnabled` | `handleSmsLeadFlow(business, conversation, text, from)` (route.ts:1058) | Returns `true` **only** when `status==='lead_captured'` (already handled). Otherwise returns `false` → falls through. |
| 2. Booking state machine | always called next | `handleSmsBookingFlow(business, conversation, text, from)` (route.ts:1070) | Returns 200 if it handled the message. (State machine — documented by another agent.) |
| 3. General AI | booking returned false | `generateAIResponse(business, conversation, text, conversation.bookingFlowState, business.calendarEnabled && business.smsBookingEnabled !== false)` (route.ts:396, 2145) | Continues to post-processing below. |

**Important:** `handleSmsLeadFlow` does NOT do conversational lead capture. Lead capture is performed by `generateAIResponse` with `calendarEnabled=false` (a distinct lead-only system prompt) plus the post-processing handlers. `generateAIResponse`'s last arg `calendarEnabled` is `business.calendarEnabled && business.smsBookingEnabled !== false` — so `smsBookingEnabled=false` routes to the lead-only AI prompt even with a connected calendar.

### Post-AI processing (route.ts:400-737)

1. **AI failure** (route.ts:400-419): if `aiResult.aiFailed`, send the fallback via `sendSMSAndLog`, call `notifyOwnerOnAIFailed(...)`, return 200.
2. **Tag stripping** (route.ts:422-427): strips `[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED:...]`, `[LEAD_CAPTURED:...]`, `[READY_TO_CAPTURE]`, `[HUMAN_NEEDED...]` → `cleanResponse`.
3. **[READY_TO_CAPTURE] lead capture** (route.ts:430-479): only when `!calendarEnabled && readyToCapture && status!=='lead_captured'`. Calls `extractLeadFromConversation` (extra Haiku call). If a name extracted: notifies owner via `notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured`, sets `status:'lead_captured'` + customer fields + `bookingFlowState: Prisma.DbNull`, background `findOrCreateContact`, sends `buildLeadConfirmation(business)`, returns 200.
4. **Calendar-enabled safety nets** (route.ts:486-555): only when `calendarEnabled && googleCalendarConnected && status!=='appointment_booked' && !appointment` and no current flow step.
   - **SCENARIO 1 — fake confirmation intercept**: regex detects AI saying "you're all set / booked for / confirmed for / see you on…" without an offer word. Replaces with a redirect message and sets `status:'booking_in_progress'`, `bookingFlowState.step='awaiting_name_and_preference'`. Returns 200.
   - **SCENARIO 2 — guiding to booking**: if AI asked for BOTH name and time, sets the same flow state and sends the AI's own message. Returns 200.
5. **[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED:] tag** (route.ts:566-622): honored only when `canAiBook = (!calendarEnabled || !googleCalendarConnected)`. Regex requires ordered named fields `name`, `service`, `datetime`, optional `notes`, optional `address`. Parses datetime in business tz, calls `createBooking({ ..., skipSlotVerification:true, allowWithoutCalendar:true })`. On success sets `status:'appointment_booked'` and returns (createBooking already sent the confirmation SMS; cleanResponse is intentionally NOT re-sent). On failure sends a friendly fallback.
6. **[HUMAN_NEEDED] tag** (route.ts:626-646): sets `status:'human_needed'`, `intent:'human_needed'`, calls `notifyOwnerOnHumanNeeded`. Does **not** return — falls through to the generic send unless the backstop fires.
7. **Lead BACKSTOP** (route.ts:648-727): fires when `!calendarEnabled && !readyToCapture && !humanNeededMatch && status not lead_captured/appointment_booked && !appointment && inboundCount>=2`. Calls `extractLeadFromConversation`; if an address OR timeframe present, notifies owner, and **only after a successful notify** flips `status:'lead_captured'` (failed notify leaves `active` for retry), upserts contact, sends `buildLeadConfirmation`, returns 200.
8. **Generic send** (route.ts:729-737): `sendSMSAndLog(business, conversation.id, from, cleanResponse, timing)`, return 200.

### `generateAIResponse` (route.ts:2145-2501)

```ts
async function generateAIResponse(
  business, conversation, latestMessage,
  bookingFlowState?, calendarEnabled?
): Promise<{ response: string; aiFailed?: boolean }>
```
- Builds `conversationHistory` from `conversation.messages`, dropping empty/whitespace content (avoids Anthropic 400s), mapping inbound→`user`, outbound→`assistant`.
- Computes today/weekday in `business.timezone` (default `America/New_York`). When calendar connected, fetches up to 14 days of `getAvailableSlots`, dedupes dates, injects up to 7 into the prompt.
- **Two distinct system prompts**, both injecting `conversation.callerPhone` in a top `CRITICAL` block and inline in the phone-verification rule (to stop the AI from quoting `forwardingNumber`), and both labeling `forwardingNumber` as "Owner's business line (mention only when redirecting…)":
  - **Lead-only mode** (`!calendarEnabled`, route.ts:2195-2309): goal = capture name/address/timeframe, never offer slots, never ask for email or phone, emits `[READY_TO_CAPTURE]` as final token. **Single attempt, no retry** — any error returns `aiFailed:true`.
  - **Booking mode** (route.ts:2311-2500): includes `flowGuidance` when `inBookingFlow`; forbids `[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED]` (structured flow only). **Retry loop: 3 attempts, 3000ms delay, only on HTTP 529** (overloaded). Other errors return `aiFailed:true` immediately.
- Both reference `(business as any).website`, but **no `website` field exists** on the Business model — always undefined.

### `extractLeadFromConversation` (route.ts:1021-1055)

Extra Anthropic call (`claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`, `max_tokens:256`). Builds a transcript, asks for JSON `{ customerName(required), customerEmail, customerAddress, customerService, customerTimeframe }`. Parses the first `{...}` match. Returns `null` if no JSON or no name; otherwise the typed object. Errors → `null`.

### Send helpers

```ts
async function sendSMS(business, to, message)        // route.ts:2503-2506 — NO DB log
  new Telnyx({ apiKey: process.env.TELNYX_API_KEY! })
  telnyxClient.messages.send({ from: business.telnyxPhoneNumber!, to, text: message })
```
Used for STOP opt-out and the message-limit cutoff. (Note: the "never mind" goodbye uses `sendSMSAndLog`, not `sendSMS`, so it IS logged — contrary to CLAUDE.md's claim that the no-log helper covers all cut-offs.)

```ts
async function sendSMSAndLog(business, conversationId, to, message, timing?)   // route.ts:2508-2555
```
Side effects:
- Sends via Telnyx (`from: business.telnyxPhoneNumber!`). Reads `data.id` → `messageId`, `data.to[0].status ?? 'sent'` → `status`.
- Optionally records timing fields (`telnyxSendAt`, `telnyxResponseAt`, `telnyxMessageId`, `telnyxStatus`).
- **Deferred (non-awaited) DB write** (route.ts:2540-2545): `void db.message.create({ data: { conversationId, direction:'outbound', content:message, telnyxSid: messageId ?? null, telnyxStatus: status } }).then(() => recordMessageSent(business.id, to)).catch(...)`. So the outbound `Message` is seeded with both `telnyxSid` and an initial `telnyxStatus`, and `recordMessageSent` updates `ContactCooldown` — both run after the webhook responds. If the SMS send itself throws, it is caught and logged; no Message is created.

### Misc helper inventory (defined in this file, used by the pipeline)

`getTodayInTimezone`, `getTomorrowInTimezone`, `parseDatetimeInBusinessTz`, `formatDateFull`, `buildConfirmationMessage`, `getCallUsPhrase` (uses `forwardingNumber`, never the Telnyx number), `buildLeadConfirmation`, `isBusinessClosedOnDate`, `formatBusinessHoursSummary`, `extractServiceFromConversation`, `cleanServiceForDisplay`, `getServicesList`, `looksLikeQuestion`, `parseNameFromMessage`, `capitalizeName`, `parseServiceSelection`, `parseNotesFromMessage`, `parseEmailFromMessage`, `parseAddressFromMessage`, `looksLikeTimeChange`, `parseTimeframeFromMessage`, `parseTimePreference`, `getNext3BusinessDays`. The booking state machine (`handleSmsBookingFlow`, `handleAwaitingNameAndPreference`, `handleTimeChangeFromSlotChoice`) and the full `BookingFlowState`/`LeadFlowState` types live here too but are documented by another agent.

---

## 5. SMS AI Brain — Prompt Construction, Lead Capture, Booking State Machine & Special Tags

Ground-truth reference for the AI/lead/booking logic in `app/api/webhooks/sms/route.ts` (2555 lines). This covers the inbound `message.received` orchestration, `generateAIResponse()`, the lead-capture flow, the full booking state machine, special-tag handling, and the AI-failure fallback. The Telnyx webhook framing (event routing, STOP handling, conversation lookup) is summarized only where it gates the AI logic.

### Module-level constants & config

| Constant | Value | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `anthropic` | `new Anthropic({ apiKey: process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY ?? '' })` | module-scope singleton (sms/route.ts:22) |
| `DAY_NAMES` | `['sunday',...,'saturday']` | index = `Date.getDay()` (sms/route.ts:24) |
| `MONTH_NAMES` | full + 3-letter map → 0-based month idx | includes `sept` (sms/route.ts:38) |
| `DEFAULT_MAX_MESSAGES_PER_CONVERSATION` | **25** | CLAUDE.md says default 23 — see drift (sms/route.ts:91) |
| `BOOKING_INTENT_WORDS` | 30+ phrases (see below) | substring-matched against lowercased text (sms/route.ts:92) |
| `CONVERSATION_TIMEOUT_HOURS` | 24 | declared but not referenced in flow logic (sms/route.ts:114) |
| `SPAM_WINDOW_SECONDS` | 30 | duplicate-text guard window (sms/route.ts:115) |
| `AI_FALLBACK_MESSAGE` | `"Thanks for reaching out! Let me have someone from the team get back to you shortly."` | sent whenever `aiFailed` (sms/route.ts:2143) |
| `AI_MODEL` | `'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001'` | hard-coded in BOTH lead-mode and booking-mode branches (sms/route.ts:2286, 2439) |

`BOOKING_INTENT_WORDS` (exact): `book, appointment, schedule, booking, reserve, quote, estimate, come out, come by, stop by, take a look, set up, set me up, sign me up, available, availability, free quote, free estimate, in-person, in person, get on the schedule, when can you, what times, open slots`.

### Inbound routing & gating (the part that decides whether AI runs)

`POST` handler (sms/route.ts:138). Two event branches:
- `message.finalized` / `message.sent` → `db.message.updateMany({ where:{telnyxSid}, data:{telnyxStatus} })`, return 200.
- `message.received` → main pipeline.

Pre-AI guards, in order:
1. **Business lookup** by `telnyxPhoneNumber === payload.to`; missing → 200 no-op.
2. **STOP words** `['stop','unsubscribe','cancel','quit']` (exact-match on trimmed lowercase) → `sendSMS` opt-out, return. Uses `sendSMS` (no DB log).
3. **"Never mind" phrases** `['never mind','nevermind','not interested','no thanks','no thank you']` (substring) → find/create conversation, save inbound msg, send goodbye via `sendSMSAndLog`, set `status='closed'`, `summary='Customer said not interested'`, then **`notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured`** with `service='Partial interest - customer declined'`. Return.
4. **Empty/blank body** (MMS no text) → 200 without saving. Intentional: blank content blocks 400 the Anthropic API.
5. **Conversation lookup**: `findFirst` where `businessId+callerPhone+createdAt >= now-90d`, `orderBy lastMessageAt desc`, includes last 50 messages + appointment.
6. **NO_AI_RESPONSE_STATUSES** = `['appointment_booked','lead_captured','closed','human_needed','needs_review','completed']`. If found conversation is in one of these → save inbound msg, update `lastMessageAt`, and:
   - `appointment_booked`: detect question (`?` or regex `is he|will you/they/he|are you|when|where|what time|who is coming|coming to|show up`). If question + appointment present → reply with appt address/date/time; else `"You're welcome!..."`. Then set `status='closed'`.
   - `lead_captured`: detect pleasantry (`THANK_YOU_PHRASES` substring OR `SHORT_THANK_YOU_TOKENS=['ty','thx']` whole-word). Pleasantry → `"Anytime! Talk soon."`; else `"Got it, I'll pass that along..."`. Then `status='closed'`. **Never re-notifies owner.**
   - Return 200 for any NO_AI status.
7. If no conversation → **create** `status='active'` + background `findOrCreateContact({source:'missed_call'})` (fire-and-forget).
8. Re-fetch full conversation (messages asc + appointment).
9. **Spam guard**: identical inbound `content` within `SPAM_WINDOW_SECONDS` → ignore.
10. **Message-limit guard**: `maxMessages = business.maxMessagesPerConversation ?? 25`. `inBookingFlow = Boolean(bookingFlowState?.step)`. If `!inBookingFlow && messages.length >= maxMessages` → `status='completed'`, `summary='Conversation ended - message limit reached'`, send cut-off via `sendSMS` (no log), return. **Booking-in-progress conversations are never cut off.**
11. Save inbound message, update `lastMessageAt`, re-fetch.
12. Re-check NO_AI status or `conversation.appointment` → return.

**Flow dispatch** (sms/route.ts:376):
- If `!business.calendarEnabled || !business.smsBookingEnabled` → `handleSmsLeadFlow()`. It returns `true` **only** when `status==='lead_captured'` (skip), else `false` (fall through to AI). It performs NO capture itself.
- `handleSmsBookingFlow()` → if it returns `true`, request ends.
- Else `generateAIResponse(business, conversation, text, bookingFlowState, calendarEnabled && smsBookingEnabled !== false)`.

### `generateAIResponse()` — signature, model, prompt structure

```ts
async function generateAIResponse(
  business: any,
  conversation: any,
  latestMessage: string,          // NOTE: not used to build messages array — see below
  bookingFlowState?: unknown,
  calendarEnabled?: boolean
): Promise<{ response: string; aiFailed?: boolean }>
```

**Conversation history mapping** (sms/route.ts:2155): `conversation.messages` is filtered to non-empty string content, then mapped to Anthropic turns — `direction==='inbound' → role:'user'`, else `role:'assistant'`. `latestMessage` param is NOT separately appended (the latest inbound was already persisted before this call, so it is in `conversation.messages`). The empty-content filter is deliberate (one blank inbound would 400 every subsequent call for the thread).

**Anthropic call params (both modes):**
```ts
anthropic.messages.create({ model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001', max_tokens: 256, system: <prompt>, messages: conversationHistory })
```
No `temperature` is set (SDK default). Lead mode: single attempt, no retry. Booking mode: **3 retries** (`maxRetries=3`, `retryDelayMs=3000`) but **only on HTTP 529** (`is529`); any other error fails immediately. On API-key empty/missing → `{ aiFailed:true }`. Returns first `text` content block; missing text → fallback + `aiFailed`.

**Datetime/timezone block** (sms/route.ts:2162): `tz = business.timezone ?? 'America/New_York'`. `todayStr` via `toLocaleDateString('en-CA',{timeZone})`. `todayFormatted` = `"Weekday, Month Nth"`. Prompt header: `TODAY: ${weekday}, ${todayFormatted} (${tz})`.

**Calendar availability note** (booking mode only, sms/route.ts:2168): when `calendarEnabled && googleCalendarConnected`, calls `getAvailableSlots(businessId, todayStr, +14d)`, derives unique dates, takes **first 7**, formats as `"\n- Available dates for quotes (next 2 weeks): <dates>"`. Errors swallowed.

**Two prompt variants** selected by the `calendarEnabled` param:

#### Lead-only prompt (`!calendarEnabled`) — sms/route.ts:2196
Blocks in order: header + `TODAY` + **CRITICAL callback-number anchor** + `YOUR GOAL` (collect name/address/timeframe, hand off; never offer slots) + `PERSONALITY` + `GREETING` + `IDEAL CONVERSATION` (4-step script ending in `[READY_TO_CAPTURE]`) + `LEAD CAPTURE RULES` + `SERVICES & KNOWLEDGE` + `PRICING — STRICT RULE` (never quote) + `BUSINESS INFO` + `UNKNOWN QUESTIONS` + `FRUSTRATED CUSTOMER` (`[HUMAN_NEEDED: reason="Customer frustrated"]`) + `OFF TOPIC` + `YES/NO WITHOUT CONTEXT` + `REPEAT CUSTOMERS` + `LANGUAGE` (English only) + `THINGS YOU MUST NEVER DO`. Lead mode explicitly: **"Do NOT ask for email. Ever."** (despite `customerEmail` being extracted downstream via a separate AI call).

#### Booking-capable prompt (`calendarEnabled`) — sms/route.ts:2327
Same skeleton plus `${availableDatesForPrompt}`, a `DATE/TIME RULES` block, expanded `BOOKING RULES`, and a conditionally-injected `${flowGuidance}` block (only when `inBookingFlow = flowState?.step && !leadFlow`). `flowGuidance` (sms/route.ts:2311) enumerates per-step instructions and repeats **"NEVER output [APPOINTMENT_BOOKED]. The structured booking flow handles all booking."** The booking prompt's NEVER list also forbids `[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED]`.

**The callerPhone CRITICAL anchor (both prompts)** — appears twice per prompt, intentionally:
1. Top-of-prompt `CRITICAL:` block: `The customer's callback number is ${conversation.callerPhone}. ... NEVER use any phone number from BUSINESS INFO, About, or Special Instructions as the customer's callback number.`
2. Inline in the phone-verification rule: `Ask: "Is ${conversation.callerPhone} the best number to reach you at?" Use that EXACT number.`

`BUSINESS INFO` labels `forwardingNumber` as `Owner's business line (mention only when redirecting customers to call, never as their callback number)`. This double-injection prevents the model from quoting the owner's `forwardingNumber` as the customer's callback number (a confirmed production bug). The prompt also references `business.website` (`(business as any).website`) — a field NOT in CLAUDE.md's schema.

`BUSINESS INFO` injects: `business.name`, `JSON.stringify(business.servicesOffered)`, `business.aiContext` (as `About:`), `business.aiInstructions` (as `Special instructions:`), `hoursSummary` (from `formatBusinessHoursSummary`), `forwardingNumber`, `website`. There is no separate `aiGreeting` injection — the greeting is described as already sent.

### Lead capture (`!business.calendarEnabled` path, post-AI)

`handleSmsLeadFlow()` (sms/route.ts:1058) is a no-op gate: returns `false` unless calendar+SMS-booking enabled or already `lead_captured`. **All actual lead capture happens inline in the POST handler after `generateAIResponse`**, via two mechanisms:

**A. Explicit `[READY_TO_CAPTURE]`** (sms/route.ts:430): gated on `!business.calendarEnabled && response.includes('[READY_TO_CAPTURE]') && status !== 'lead_captured'`. Calls `extractLeadFromConversation()` (separate Haiku call, see below). If `customerName` present:
- Builds `thanksMsg = buildLeadConfirmation(business)` (`"Perfect! Someone from <name> will follow up..."`).
- **`notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured`** with `service = cleanServiceForOwner(customerService)`, plus email/address/timeframe.
- Updates conversation: `status='lead_captured'`, `callerName`, `intent='lead_capture'`, `serviceRequested`, `customerEmail`, `customerAddress`, `customerTimeframe`, `bookingFlowState=Prisma.DbNull`.
- Background `findOrCreateContact({source:'missed_call', email, name, address})`.
- Sends `thanksMsg` via `sendSMSAndLog`, returns.

**B. Backstop capture** (sms/route.ts:648): when AI finished the lead chat without emitting `[READY_TO_CAPTURE]`. Gate: `!calendarEnabled && !readyToCapture && !humanNeededMatch && status not lead_captured/appointment_booked && !appointment && inboundCount >= 2`. Runs `extractLeadFromConversation`; fires when `hasAddress || hasTimeframe` (name not required — phone already known). **Notify must succeed before flipping status** — failed `notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured` leaves `status='active'` so the next inbound retries. On success: same conversation update + `findOrCreateContact`, sends `buildLeadConfirmation` (NOT the raw AI turn, which is often a question), returns.

`extractLeadFromConversation()` (sms/route.ts:1021): separate `anthropic.messages.create` call, `model:'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001'`, `max_tokens:256`, no system prompt — a single user message asking for JSON with keys `customerName` (required, default `"Customer"`), `customerEmail`, `customerAddress`, `customerService`, `customerTimeframe`. Parses first `{...}` match. Returns `null` if no JSON or no name.

There is **no** AI `[LEAD_CAPTURED: ...]` parsing path in the code (comment at sms/route.ts:624 confirms it was removed). CLAUDE.md still documents `[LEAD_CAPTURED:...]` as an active tag — see drift.

### Special tags — exact format, parsing, actions

| Tag | Regex / detection | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `[READY_TO_CAPTURE]` | `aiResponse.includes('[READY_TO_CAPTURE]')` | Lead capture A (above). Lead-mode prompt requires it be the **last token, never with a question**. |
| `[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED: name="..", service="..", datetime="..", notes=".."?, address=".."?]` | `/\[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED: name="([^"]+)", service="([^"]+)", datetime="([^"]+)"(?:, notes="([^"]*)")?(?:, address="([^"]*)")?\]/` (sms/route.ts:567) | **Only honored when `canAiBook = !calendarEnabled \|\| !googleCalendarConnected`.** Parses datetime via `parseDatetimeInBusinessTz`, calls `createBooking({skipSlotVerification:true, allowWithoutCalendar:true, conversationId})`. On ok → `status='appointment_booked'`. `createBooking` already sends the confirmation SMS, so the cleaned AI text is intentionally NOT re-sent. **Note the field ORDER is `name, service, datetime` — differs from CLAUDE.md's `datetime, service, name`.** |
| `[HUMAN_NEEDED]` or `[HUMAN_NEEDED: reason=".."]` | `/\[HUMAN_NEEDED(?:: reason="([^"]*)")?\]/` (sms/route.ts:626) | `status='human_needed'`, `intent='human_needed'`, then **`notifyOwnerOnHumanNeeded`** with parsed reason + transcript. Cleaned response still sent. |
| `[LEAD_CAPTURED:...]` | none | **No parsing exists.** Stripped from output but never acted on. |

**Tag-stripping** (sms/route.ts:422) before any customer SMS:
```ts
cleanResponse = aiResponse
  .replace(/\[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED:.*?\]/g, '')
  .replace(/\[LEAD_CAPTURED:.*?\]/g, '')
  .replace(/\[READY_TO_CAPTURE\]/g, '')
  .replace(/\[HUMAN_NEEDED(?:: reason="[^"]*")?\]/g, '')
  .trim()
```

### Calendar-enabled "fake confirmation" interceptors (post-AI, before send)

When `calendarEnabled && googleCalendarConnected && status !== 'appointment_booked' && !appointment && !flowState.step` (sms/route.ts:486):
- **SCENARIO 1 — fake confirmation**: regex `/you're all set|your (appointment|booking) is (set|confirmed|booked)|booked (you )?for|confirmed for|see you (on|at)|appointment is scheduled/i` AND NOT `/would you like|want to|shall we|should we|let me/i`. If matched, the AI text is **suppressed** (logged, not sent) and a redirect message is sent; conversation set to `status='booking_in_progress'`, `bookingFlowState.step='awaiting_name_and_preference'`. Prevents the AI from claiming a booking the calendar never made.
- **SCENARIO 2 — guiding to booking**: AI asks for BOTH name (`what's your name|your name`) AND time (`when works|what day|what time|day/time|when... best`). Sets `step='awaiting_name_and_preference'` for the next message, sends the AI's natural text.

### Booking state machine — `handleSmsBookingFlow()` (sms/route.ts:1070)

Entry guards: returns `false` if `!calendarEnabled || !smsBookingEnabled`; returns `true` (consumed) if `appointment` exists or `status==='appointment_booked'` or `flowState.step==='confirmed'`. Reads `conversation.bookingFlowState`. **Legacy step mapping** (sms/route.ts:1102): `awaiting_slot|awaiting_time|awaiting_selection|awaiting_preference → awaiting_time`; unknown/empty → `greeting`. `bookingRequiresAddress = business.bookingRequiresAddress ?? true`.

`BookingFlowState` type (sms/route.ts:751):
```ts
step: 'greeting' | 'awaiting_name' | 'awaiting_name_and_preference' | 'awaiting_service'
    | 'awaiting_notes' | 'awaiting_address' | 'awaiting_time' | 'awaiting_confirmation'
    | 'awaiting_name_and_address' | 'awaiting_name_after_slot' | 'awaiting_address_after_slot' | 'confirmed'
customerName?, serviceType?, notes?, customerAddress?,
selectedSlot?: {start,end,display}, timePreference?, suggestedSlots?: {start,end,display}[],
lastDiscussedDate?: string /*YYYY-MM-DD*/, services?: {value,label}[], sentAt?: string
```
