State is persisted via `JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({...}))` into `bookingFlowState`; cleared via `Prisma.DbNull` on success. Most steps short-circuit with `if (looksLikeQuestion(rawText)) return false` to route questions to the general AI.

#### State-transition table

| Current `step` | Condition / parse | Reply | Next `step` |
|---|---|---|---|
| (no step) | `BOOKING_INTENT_WORDS` in msg/history, OR AI offered booking + customer agreeing (`implicitBookingIntent`), AND calendar+connected | `"I'd love to set that up! What day and time...?"` | `existingName ? 'awaiting_time' : 'awaiting_name_and_preference'`; sets `status='booking_in_progress'` |
| (no step) | no intent / no calendar | — | returns `false` (→ general AI) |
| `awaiting_name_and_preference` | `handleAwaitingNameAndPreference()`: parse name + `parseTimePreference` | depends (see below) | `awaiting_time` / `awaiting_name_after_slot` |
| `awaiting_name` (legacy) | parse name | `"Thanks <name>! What day and time...?"` | `awaiting_time` |
| `awaiting_time` | match `suggestedSlots`, OR `parseTimePreference` → `isSpecificSlotAvailable` | slot open → ask name; taken → suggest `getTwoClosestSlotsOnDay`; closed/past/fully-booked → re-ask | `awaiting_name_after_slot` (open) or `awaiting_time` (alt/retry) |
| `awaiting_service` (legacy) | `parseServiceSelection` | `"Got it! ...Anything we should know...?"` | `awaiting_notes` |
| `awaiting_notes` (legacy) | `parseNotesFromMessage` | `"What day and time...?"` | `awaiting_time` |
| `awaiting_address` (legacy) | raw text as address | `"What day and time...?"` | `awaiting_time` |
| `awaiting_name_after_slot` | (requires `selectedSlot`) time-change check first; `parseNameFromMessage`; rejects `NOT_A_NAME` | if `bookingRequiresAddress` → ask address; else if address parsed → confirm; else ask address | `awaiting_address_after_slot` or `awaiting_confirmation` |
| `awaiting_name_and_address` (legacy) | same as above, combined-intent | ask address (always when `requiresAddress`) | `awaiting_address_after_slot` / `awaiting_confirmation` |
| `awaiting_address_after_slot` | (requires `selectedSlot`) time-change check; raw text as address | `buildConfirmationMessage(...)` (`"Perfect! Just to confirm: <date> at <time> for <name> at <addr> — <service>. Reply yes to confirm."`) | `awaiting_confirmation` |
| `awaiting_confirmation` | (requires `selectedSlot`) `confirmed` regex `yes\|yeah\|yep\|yup\|sure\|ok\|okay\|confirm\|confirmed\|that works\|sounds good`; `rejected` regex `no\|nope\|wrong\|actually\|different\|change` | confirmed → **`createBooking`**; rejected → re-ask time; else `"Reply yes to confirm..."` | confirmed → `status='appointment_booked'`, `bookingFlowState=DbNull`; rejected → `awaiting_time` |
| `confirmed` | guard at entry | — | returns `true` (no-op) |

**Slot-taken handling** (multiple steps): `getTwoClosestSlotsOnDay(businessId, dateStr, hour, minute, tz)` → if any, message `"Sorry, ... is taken. How about <times> that same day? Or tell me another day..."` and store `suggestedSlots`+`lastDiscussedDate`, step `awaiting_time`. Fully booked → `"... is fully booked. Tell us another day..."`.

**`createBooking` calls** are made from two places only: (1) `awaiting_confirmation === yes` with `{skipSlotVerification:true, conversationId}` (NO `allowWithoutCalendar`), and (2) the AI `[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED]` tag path with `{skipSlotVerification:true, allowWithoutCalendar:true}`. Before each, a fresh `db.conversation.findUnique(... include appointment)` re-check prevents double-booking. On failure, customer gets `"Sorry, we had trouble saving that..."` (never the raw error).

#### `handleAwaitingNameAndPreference()` (sms/route.ts:1764)
Parses name (`parseNameFromMessage`), rejects `NOT_A_NAME` (`yes/no/sure/ok/...`) unless prior `customerName` exists; if `parseTimePreference` succeeds and parsed name equals the whole raw text or is >30 chars, falls back to stored name (heuristic to avoid treating "Friday at 2pm" as a name). No time → ask for time (`awaiting_time`). Time present → run availability path identical to `awaiting_time` (open → `awaiting_name_after_slot` with `selectedSlot`; taken → suggest; past/closed/booked → `awaiting_time`). `serviceType` resolved via `extractServiceFromConversation`.

#### Time-change handling — `handleTimeChangeFromSlotChoice()` (sms/route.ts:1187)
Active when a `selectedSlot` exists and `looksLikeTimeChange(rawText)` (standalone `5pm`, bare digit `1-7` → +12, or `lets/how about/can we do/instead <time>`). Re-checks `isSpecificSlotAvailable` on the same date; open → `awaiting_name_after_slot`; taken → suggest closest, `awaiting_time`.

### `parseTimePreference()` (sms/route.ts:1952) — natural-language date/time

Returns `{ startStr, endStr, timeOfDay?, preferredHour?, preferredMinute?, isPastDate?, notBeforeMinutes? } | null`. All math uses `TZDate` in business tz (never UTC). Recognizes: `anytime/whenever/asap/soonest` → `getNext3BusinessDays`; `this weekend` (next Sat); `the Nth`/`6th` (day-of-month, rolls to next month if past); `Month Nth` (rolls to next year if past); `this <weekday>` (incl today); `next <weekday>` (+1 week); `next week` (+7); `tomorrow`; `today`; standalone weekday (next occurrence incl today). Time: `at 1pm`/`1:30pm` (prefers token after `at`), `noon`, `until/after/from N` → `notBeforeMinutes`, and `morning/afternoon/evening` time-of-day buckets. `tomorrow`/`today` fetch a single day; otherwise `endStr` extends 1–6 days. Default hour when only time-of-day given: morning→9, evening→17, else 14. `getTodayInTimezone`/`getTomorrowInTimezone` use `toLocaleDateString('en-CA')`.

### Owner-notification call sites (`lib/notify-owner`)

| Function | Trigger in this file |
|---|---|
| `notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured` | "never mind" partial-interest (line 209); `[READY_TO_CAPTURE]` capture (440); backstop capture (679) |
| `notifyOwnerOnHumanNeeded` | `[HUMAN_NEEDED]` tag (636) |
| `notifyOwnerOnAIFailed` | `aiResult.aiFailed === true` (408) |
| `createBooking` | `awaiting_confirmation` yes (1134); `[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED]` tag (579). `createBooking` internally calls `notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated`. |

### AI-failure fallback (sms/route.ts:400)
When `generateAIResponse` returns `aiFailed`, the fallback text (`AI_FALLBACK_MESSAGE`) is sent via `sendSMSAndLog`, then `notifyOwnerOnAIFailed(business, {customerName, customerPhone, conversationTranscript, conversationId})`. Booking mode retries only on HTTP **529** (×3, 3s delay); lead mode and all non-529 errors fail straight to fallback.

### SMS send helpers
- `sendSMS(business, to, message)` (sms/route.ts:2503): `telnyx.messages.send`, **no DB log**. Used for STOP and message-limit cut-offs.
- `sendSMSAndLog(business, conversationId, to, message, timing?)` (sms/route.ts:2508): sends, captures `messageId`/`status`, then **defers** `db.message.create({direction:'outbound', telnyxSid, telnyxStatus})` + `recordMessageSent(businessId, to)` via `void ...catch` (non-blocking; if the deferred DB write fails the SMS was still sent).

### Key helper functions (parsing/formatting)
`parseNameFromMessage` (strips time/date after `and`/comma, capitalizes), `capitalizeName`, `parseServiceSelection` (number picks from list or uses raw words), `parseNotesFromMessage` (treats `no/none/skip/...` as empty), `parseEmailFromMessage`, `parseAddressFromMessage` (needs ≥5 chars, rejects email-only/short-no-digits), `parseTimeframeFromMessage`, `extractServiceFromConversation` (`SERVICE_KEYWORDS` + `SERVICE_BLOCKLIST`, default `"a free in-person quote"`), `cleanServiceForDisplay`, `formatBusinessHoursSummary` (`"Mon-Fri 9am-5pm"`), `isBusinessClosedOnDate`, `looksLikeQuestion`, `looksLikeTimeChange`, `buildConfirmationMessage`, `buildLeadConfirmation`, `getCallUsPhrase` (uses `forwardingNumber`, falls back to `"call us directly"`).

---

## 6. Booking Creation & Google Calendar

Ground-truth reference for the appointment-booking pipeline and Google Calendar integration. Covers `lib/create-booking.ts`, `lib/google-calendar.ts`, and all `/api/bookings/*`, `/api/appointments`, `/api/marketing-bookings` routes.

### File / entry-point map

| File | Exports / Routes | Auth |
|---|---|---|
| `lib/create-booking.ts` | `cleanServiceForOwner()`, `createBooking()`, `CreateBookingParams`, `CreateBookingResult` | n/a (lib) |
| `lib/google-calendar.ts` | `getOAuth2Client`, `getAuthUrl`, `exchangeCodeForTokens`, `getValidAccessToken`, `getCalendarClient`, `parseBusinessHours`, `getAvailableSlots`, `getAvailableSlotsWithMeta`, `getAvailableSlotsWithDebug`, `isSpecificSlotAvailable`, `getTwoClosestSlotsOnDay`, `getBusyTimes`, `createCalendarEvent`, `createMarketingCalendarEvent`, `deleteCalendarEvent`, `calendarEventExists`, plus types `TimeSlot`, `AvailableSlotsDebug`, `CalendarEventSource`, `CreateMarketingCalendarEventOptions`; re-exports `DEFAULT_BUSINESS_HOURS` | server-only |
| `app/api/bookings/create/route.ts` | `POST` | Public (no auth) |
| `app/api/bookings/available-slots/route.ts` | `GET` (`dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`) | Public (no auth) |
| `app/api/bookings/[id]/route.ts` | `DELETE` (permanent delete) — **there is NO `GET`** | Clerk + owner/admin |
| `app/api/bookings/[id]/cancel/route.ts` | `POST` | Clerk + owner/admin |
| `app/api/bookings/delete-past/route.ts` | `POST` (`dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`) — **NOT `DELETE`** | Clerk + business |
| `app/api/appointments/route.ts` | `GET` | Clerk + business; 403 if `!calendarEnabled` |
| `app/api/marketing-bookings/route.ts` | `GET` (availability), `POST` (create) | Public (no auth) |

`createBooking()` is called by `app/api/bookings/create/route.ts` and `app/api/webhooks/sms/route.ts`. `getAvailableSlots` / `getTwoClosestSlotsOnDay` / `isSpecificSlotAvailable` / `parseBusinessHours` are consumed by the SMS webhook booking state machine. `getAvailableSlotsWithDebug` is an **exported but currently uncalled** dead function (only its internal `getAvailableSlotsInternal(withDebug=true)` path is reachable).

---

### `cleanServiceForOwner(service: string): string`

`lib/create-booking.ts:29`. Produces the owner-facing/calendar/DB `serviceType` string. Exported (also used in lead-capture flow).

Rules, in order:
1. `s = service.trim()`. If empty **or** `length > 100` → return `'Free quote'`.
2. If `s` matches (case-insensitive, full-string) one of: `a free in-person quote`, `a/an estimate`, `a/an consultation`, `quote visit`, `appointment` → return `'Free quote'`.
3. Strip leading `your |my |the ` prefix.
4. Strip leading `(i )?(need|want) (a )?(quote )?(for )?(my|the)?` (global, case-insensitive).
5. If resulting `s.length` is between 2 and 80 inclusive → return `s` with first char upper-cased.
6. Otherwise → `'Free quote'`.

A second, **non-exported** helper `cleanServiceForConfirmation(service)` (`lib/create-booking.ts:47`) produces the *customer-facing* SMS phrasing (e.g. `"your lawn"`, default fallback `"a free in-person quote"`). It tries `(my|the) <thing>` and `your <thing>` regex captures, then strips filler like `quote for/on/about`, `to book a (quote )?(for )?`, returning `'your <x>'` or the default. The two cleaners diverge: owner sees `"Lawn"`, customer SMS says `"your lawn"`.

`formatDateFullForConfirm(d, tz)` (`lib/create-booking.ts:16`) renders `"Friday, March 6th"` with ordinal-day suffix using `toLocaleDateString` in the business tz.

---

### `createBooking(params): Promise<CreateBookingResult>`

```ts
export type CreateBookingParams = {
  business: Business
  customerName: string
  customerPhone: string
  customerEmail?: string | null
  slotStart: string | Date
  serviceType: string
  notes?: string | null
  customerAddress?: string | null
  conversationId?: string | null
  skipSlotVerification?: boolean   // default false — skip Google "is slot in available list" check
  allowWithoutCalendar?: boolean   // default false — allow even if calendarEnabled/connected false (AI path)
  logPrefix?: string               // default '[BOOKING CREATE]'
}

export type CreateBookingResult =
  | { ok: true; appointment: { id: string; scheduledAt: Date; serviceType: string }; calendarSyncFailed?: boolean }
  | { ok: false; error: string; status?: number }
```

Note `CreateBookingResult.appointment` is a **3-field subset** (`id`, `scheduledAt`, `serviceType`) — NOT the full Prisma row.

Pipeline (`lib/create-booking.ts:91`):

1. **Normalize**: trim name/phone/service; `ownerFacingService = cleanServiceForOwner(service)`; `startDate = new Date(slotStart)` if string; `tz = business.timezone ?? 'America/New_York'`.
2. **Calendar gating** (status 400):
   - `!business.calendarEnabled && !allowWithoutCalendar` → `'Booking not available'`.
   - `!business.googleCalendarConnected && !allowWithoutCalendar` → `'Google Calendar not connected'`.
3. **Field validation** (400): missing `name|phone|service` → `'Missing required fields: customerName, customerPhone, serviceType'`. `isNaN(startDate)` → `'Invalid slotStart'`.
4. **Past-time guard** (409): compares `startDate.getTime()` against `new TZDate(new Date(), tz).getTime()` → `'This time slot is no longer available'`. (TZDate `getTime()` returns the same epoch ms as the wall-clock `new Date()`, so this is effectively a "start is in the past" check.)
5. **Per-conversation idempotency** (409): if `conversationId` set and a `confirmed` appointment already exists for it → `'This conversation already has a confirmed appointment'`.
6. **Duplicate-booking window** (409): `slotDuration = business.slotDurationMinutes ?? 30`; `marginMs = slotDuration*60_000`. Finds a `confirmed` appointment with **same businessId + same customerPhone + same `ownerFacingService`** whose `scheduledAt` is within `[startDate - marginMs, startDate + marginMs]` → `'You already have a quote visit scheduled for this service at this time'`. The duplicate window is **±slotDurationMinutes**, not ±60 min.
7. **DB time-overlap check** (409) — runs **regardless of `skipSlotVerification`**, so SMS and website flows can't race into the same slot. `slotEnd = startDate + slotDuration*60_000`. Loads non-`cancelled` appointments with `scheduledAt ∈ [startDate - 4h, slotEnd)`; for each computes `apptEnd = scheduledAt + appt.duration*60_000` and tests true overlap (`scheduledAt < slotEnd && apptEnd > startDate`). A conflict is **ignored** when it is the same `customerPhone` + same `serviceType` (already handled by step 6). Any other overlapping appointment → `'This time slot was just booked. Please pick a different time.'` (`4h` is the assumed upper bound on appointment duration.)
8. **Google slot verification** — only when `!skipSlotVerification && business.googleCalendarConnected`. `dateStr = startDate.toLocaleDateString('en-CA', {timeZone: tz})`; calls `getAvailableSlots(business.id, dateStr, dateStr)`; slot is valid if any available slot's start is within **`TOLERANCE_MS = 60_000`** of `slotStart` (`Math.abs(...) < 60_000`). The 60 s tolerance absorbs tz/parsing rounding (e.g. 12:00 PM Eastern vs UTC). Not found → logs a `warn` with the first 5 available slots and returns 409 `'This time slot is no longer available'`.
9. **Google Calendar event creation** — `shouldCreateCalendar = calendarEnabled && googleCalendarConnected`. If true, `createCalendarEvent(...)` is wrapped in try/catch: on throw, `calendarSyncFailed = true`, logs error, **continues** (non-fatal). `endDate = startDate + slotDuration*60_000`. `source = conversationId ? 'sms' : 'website'`.
10. **DB insert** — `db.appointment.create` with `status: 'confirmed'`, `duration: slotDuration`, `googleCalendarEventId`, `calendarSyncFailed`, `source`, trimmed email/notes/address (empty → null).
11. **Confirmation SMS** — only if `business.telnyxPhoneNumber`. Builds `new Telnyx({ apiKey: TELNYX_API_KEY })`, formats date via `formatDateFullForConfirm` and time via `toLocaleTimeString` (12h, business tz). `cleanService = cleanServiceForConfirmation(service)` (NOT the owner string). **Wording branches on `conversationId`**:
    - **SMS source** (`conversationId` set): `"You're all set ${name}! ${business.name} will meet you on ${dateStr} at ${timeStr}${addressPart} for ${cleanService}. See you then!"` (where `addressPart = " at <address>"` when present).
    - **Website source** (`conversationId` null): `"Confirmed! Your quote visit with ${business.name} is scheduled for ${dateStr} at ${timeStr}. They'll come out, take a look, and give you a quote for ${cleanService}. Reply to this number if you need to reschedule."`
    - If `calendarSyncFailed`, appends: `" Note: We had a small technical issue syncing to our calendar, but you're definitely booked. We'll reach out if anything changes."`
    - SMS send is `await`-ed in try/catch (failure logged, non-fatal). For SMS-source bookings, a `Message` row (`direction:'outbound'`, `telnyxSid:null`, `telnyxStatus:'sent'`) is created via a **fire-and-forget `void db.message.create(...).then(() => recordMessageSent(business.id, phone))`** — DB write is deferred (gotcha: SMS can succeed while Message row fails silently).
12. **Owner notify** — `await notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated(business, {...})` in try/catch (logged, non-fatal). Passes `ownerFacingService` as `serviceType` and `source`.
13. **Return** `{ ok: true, appointment: { id, scheduledAt, serviceType }, calendarSyncFailed: calendarSyncFailed || undefined }`.

---

### Google Calendar — OAuth helpers

```ts
getOAuth2Client(): OAuth2Client       // GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET, GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI
getAuthUrl(state: string): string     // param is `state`, NOT `businessId`; caller passes businessId as state
exchangeCodeForTokens(code, businessId): Promise<Tokens>
getValidAccessToken(businessId): Promise<string | null>
getCalendarClient(businessId): Promise<calendar_v3.Calendar | null>
```

- `SCOPES = ['https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar']` (full read/write, not `.readonly`).
- `getAuthUrl(state)` — `access_type: 'offline'`, `prompt: 'consent'` (forces refresh-token issuance every time). `state` is the businessId. Caller: `app/api/auth/google/route.ts:42`.
- `exchangeCodeForTokens(code, businessId)` — `oauth2.getToken(code)`; writes `googleAccessToken = tokens.access_token ?? null`, `googleCalendarConnected = !!(access_token && (refresh_token ?? true))`, and only sets `googleRefreshToken` when a new refresh token is returned (preserves existing). Caller: `app/api/auth/google/callback/route.ts:27` (passes the OAuth `state` as businessId).
- `getValidAccessToken(businessId)` — selects `googleAccessToken`/`googleRefreshToken`; returns `null` if no refresh token. Sets both credentials, then **always** calls `oauth2.refreshAccessToken()` (typed as `void` by googleapis but returns `{ credentials }` at runtime — cast via `RefreshResult`). **On refresh failure**: logs error, sets `googleCalendarConnected = false` on the Business (best-effort), and **re-throws**. On success: persists new `access_token` (+ `refresh_token` if returned, else keeps old) and returns it. Returns `null` only if `credentials.access_token` is falsy.
- `getCalendarClient(businessId)` — returns `null` if no access token, else `google.calendar({version:'v3', auth})`. Every calendar-side function short-circuits to a benign default (`null`/`[]`/`false`) when this returns null.

---

### `parseBusinessHours(hours: unknown)`

`lib/google-calendar.ts:115`. Returns `Record<string, { open: string; close: string } | null>` for the 7 lowercase weekday keys. Non-object input → `DEFAULT_BUSINESS_HOURS` (from `lib/business-hours.ts`, re-exported here). Per-day: a valid `{open,close}` string pair is kept; invalid type or missing day falls back to that day's `DEFAULT_BUSINESS_HOURS` entry (Mon–Fri 9–5 open, Sat/Sun `null`/closed). A `null` value in the result means "closed that day".

---

### Slot generation — `getAvailableSlotsInternal` (core)

Public wrappers:
```ts
getAvailableSlots(businessId, startDate: Date|string, endDate: Date|string): Promise<TimeSlot[]>
getAvailableSlotsWithMeta(businessId, startStr, endStr): Promise<{ slots: TimeSlot[]; noMoreAvailabilityToday?: boolean }>
getAvailableSlotsWithDebug(businessId, startStr, endStr, businessSlug?): Promise<{ slots; debug: AvailableSlotsDebug }> // UNUSED
interface TimeSlot { start: string /*ISO*/; end: string /*ISO*/; display: string /*"9:00 AM"*/ }
```

`getAvailableSlots` derives `startStr/endStr` by slicing the first 10 chars (`Date` → `toISOString().slice(0,10)`, i.e. **UTC date**, which can drift vs. the business tz for `Date` inputs; string inputs are sliced as-is).

`getAvailableSlotsInternal(businessId, startStr, endStr, withDebug, businessSlug?)` (`lib/google-calendar.ts:224`):

1. Loads business (`businessHours, slotDurationMinutes, bufferMinutes, timezone, googleCalendarConnected, calendarEnabled, googleAccessToken, googleRefreshToken`). Defaults: `tz='America/New_York'`, `slotMins=30`, `bufferMins=0`.
2. **Early exit**: if `!googleCalendarConnected` → returns empty slots (with debug if requested). Booking availability is impossible without a connected calendar.
3. Parses `startStr`/`endStr` via `parseDateString` (strict `^YYYY-MM-DD$`); malformed → empty.
4. Builds freebusy window in business tz: `timeMin = TZDate(start, 00:00:00.000, tz).toISOString()`, `timeMax = TZDate(end, 23:59:59.999, tz).toISOString()`.
5. **Busy times** = Google freebusy (`getBusyTimesWithRange`, primary calendar) merged with a **DB safety net**: all non-`cancelled` `Appointment`s with `scheduledAt ∈ [timeMin, timeMax]` are pushed as busy `{start, end=scheduledAt+duration}` so already-booked slots are blocked even when Google is unreachable / token revoked. Google failure is caught (recorded in `debug.googleCalendarError`) and does not abort.
6. **Buffer expansion**: if `bufferMins > 0`, each busy block's `end` is extended by `bufferMins` (can't start a slot until buffer after an event ends).
7. **Slot stepping**: `slotStepMins = slotMins + bufferMins`. Iterates each day from `start` 00:00 to `end` 23:59:59.999 (business tz via TZDate cursor). For each day, `dayHours = hours[DAY_NAMES[cursor.getDay()]]`; `null` → skip day. Generates slots from `open` to `close` (TZDate); a slot is emitted only if `slotEnd <= dayEnd`, **not overlapping** any buffered-busy block, and **not in the past**.
8. **Past filter** uses `nowInTz = new TZDate(new Date(), tz)`; a slot is past if `slotStart.getTime() < nowInTz.getTime()`. `display` = `toLocaleTimeString('en-US', {hour:'numeric', minute:'2-digit', hour12:true, timeZone:tz})`.
9. **`noMoreAvailabilityToday`** = `true` only when `startStr === endStr === todayStrTz` AND today had slots before the past-filter (`todaySlotsBeforePast > 0`) AND none survived (`todaySlotsAfterPast === 0`) — i.e. "it's a business day but every remaining slot is in the past."

`AvailableSlotsDebug` captures `calendarEnabled, googleCalendarConnected, tokensExist, businessHours, timezone, dateRangeQueried, timeMin, timeMax, googleCalendarBusyTimes, googleCalendarError?, slotsBeforeFiltering, slotsAfterPastFilter, finalSlotCount, finalSlots`.

#### Slot-query helpers
- `isSpecificSlotAvailable(businessId, dateStr, hour, minute, tz): Promise<TimeSlot | null>` — fetches that day's slots, returns the one whose tz-local hour/minute match, else null. (Note: returns the **slot**, not a boolean, despite CLAUDE.md's "Returns boolean".)
- `getTwoClosestSlotsOnDay(businessId, dateStr, targetHour, targetMinute, tz): Promise<TimeSlot[]>` — sorts day's slots by absolute minute-distance to target, returns up to 2.
- `getBusyTimes(businessId, startDate: Date, endDate: Date)` — thin wrapper over `getBusyTimesWithRange` using `.toISOString()` bounds; **Google-only**, does NOT include the DB safety-net merge.

---

### Calendar event creation

```ts
type CalendarEventSource = 'website' | 'sms'
createCalendarEvent(businessId, start: Date, end: Date, customerName, serviceType, customerPhone,
  options?: { customerEmail?; notes?; customerAddress?; source?: CalendarEventSource }): Promise<string | null>
```
- Returns `null` if no calendar client. Reads `business.timezone` (default ET).
- Summary: `"${source==='sms'?'📱 ':'🌐 '}${customerName} - Free Quote (${serviceType})"`.
- Description lines: source line (`Source: Missed Call SMS …` / `Source: Website …`), Customer, Phone, optional Email/Address, Service, optional Notes, blank, `"Booked via MissedCall AI - Align and Acquire"`.
- `start`/`end` are sent as `{ dateTime: <ISO UTC>, timeZone: tz }`. If `customerAddress` present, sets event `location`.
- `events.insert({ calendarId: 'primary' })`; returns `event.data.id ?? null`.

```ts
type CreateMarketingCalendarEventOptions = {
  customerPhone; customerEmail?; businessName; servicesInterested: string[]; serviceType?; message?
}
createMarketingCalendarEvent(businessId, start, end, customerName, options): Promise<string | null>
```
- Title: `"${serviceType.trim()} — ${customerName}"` when `serviceType` present, else `"Discovery Call — ${customerName}"`.
- Description: Name, Phone, optional Email, Business, optional "Services interested in: …", optional Message, `"Booked via /book (Align and Acquire)"`.
- **Adds reminders** (not present on `createCalendarEvent`): `useDefault:false`, popup + email both at 15 min before.

`deleteCalendarEvent(businessId, eventId): Promise<boolean>` — `events.delete` on primary; returns `false` on no-client or any throw (swallowed). `calendarEventExists(businessId, eventId): Promise<boolean>` — `events.get`; `false` on no-client/not-found/error.

---

### Booking API routes

#### `POST /api/bookings/create` (public)
Body: `{ businessId | businessSlug, customerName, customerPhone, customerEmail?, slotStart, serviceType, notes?, customerAddress?, conversationId? }`.

Validation before delegating to `createBooking`:
- Business lookup by `businessId` then `businessSlug`; 404 if none.
- 400 if `!calendarEnabled`; 400 if `!googleCalendarConnected`.
- 400 if missing `customerName|customerPhone|slotStart|serviceType`.
- `isWebsite = !conversationId`. **For website bookings only**: `notes` is required (400 `'Missing required field: notes'`); and if `business.bookingRequiresAddress ?? true`, `customerAddress` is required (400). SMS bookings (`conversationId` present) skip these.
- 400 on invalid `slotStart`; 409 past-time guard (`new TZDate(new Date(), tz)`).
- Calls `createBooking({ ..., logPrefix:'[BOOKING CREATE]' })` (no `skipSlotVerification`/`allowWithoutCalendar` → full slot verification path).
- On `!result.ok`: returns `{ error }` with `result.status ?? 500`.
- Success: `{ appointment: { id, scheduledAt, serviceType, timezone: tz } }`. The route **adds `timezone`** to the returned appointment.

#### `GET /api/bookings/available-slots` (public, `force-dynamic`)
Query: `businessId | businessSlug`, `start?`, `end?` (`YYYY-MM-DD`).
- 404 if business not found.
- `!calendarEnabled` → **HTTP 200** `{ error:'Booking not available', businessName, calendarEnabled:false }`.
- `!googleCalendarConnected` → **HTTP 200** `{ error:'Google Calendar not connected', businessName, slots:[] }`.
- `todayStr`/`defaultEnd` computed in business tz; default range = today → +14 days. `effectiveStart` is clamped up to `todayStr` if earlier. 400 on regex-invalid dates or `start > end`.
- Returns: `{ slots, businessName, slotDurationMinutes, calendarEnabled:true, servicesOffered, noMoreAvailabilityToday, bookingPageTitle, bookingPageServiceLabel, bookingPageConfirmation, bookingRequiresAddress, today, timezone }`.
- `servicesOffered` is normalized to `{ value, label }[]` — supports `{name, price?}` objects (label `"Name - $price"`) or plain strings. Page-text defaults: title `'Schedule a Free In-Person Quote'`, service label `'What do you need a quote for?'`, confirmation default contains `{businessName}` placeholder.

#### `DELETE /api/bookings/[id]` (Clerk)
Permanent **hard delete**. 401 if no userId; 404 if appointment not found; 403 unless caller's business owns it or `userId === ADMIN_USER_ID`. Resolves business via `getBusinessForDashboard(userId, user?.business)`. If `googleCalendarEventId && status==='confirmed'`, best-effort `deleteCalendarEvent` (swallowed). Then `db.appointment.delete`. Returns `{ success:true }`. **No `GET` handler exists on this route** (contradicts CLAUDE.md "GET: appointment detail").

#### `POST /api/bookings/[id]/cancel` (Clerk)
401/404/403 same gating. 400 if already `cancelled`. If `googleCalendarEventId`, best-effort `deleteCalendarEvent`. Sets `status:'cancelled'` (soft — row retained). Sends cancellation SMS via Telnyx from `business.telnyxPhoneNumber` to `customerPhone`: `"Your appointment with ${name} for ${dateStr} at ${timeStr} has been cancelled. Please contact us to reschedule."` (date `weekday short, month short, day`; tz-aware). SMS failure logged, non-fatal. Returns `{ success:true }`.

#### `POST /api/bookings/delete-past` (Clerk, `force-dynamic`)
Method is **POST**, not DELETE. 401/404. Finds appointments where `scheduledAt < now` OR `status='cancelled'` OR `status='completed'`. For each, if `googleCalendarEventId && status==='confirmed'` best-effort `deleteCalendarEvent`, then `db.appointment.delete`. Returns `{ success:true, deleted: <count> }`. **No 90-day age filter** (CLAUDE.md says "older than 90 days" — actual is all past/cancelled/completed).

#### `GET /api/appointments` (Clerk)
401 / 404 (no business) / **403 if `!business.calendarEnabled`** (`{ error:'Feature not available' }`). Side-effecting GET with three sync passes before returning:
1. Past `confirmed` appointments (`scheduledAt < now`) → updated to `status:'completed'`.
2. `confirmed` appointments with a `googleCalendarEventId` **created > 1 hour ago** → `calendarEventExists()` check; if gone, `status:'cancelled'` (1h grace prevents cancelling freshly-synced events).
3. Re-fetches all appointments (`orderBy scheduledAt desc`, `include conversation`) and returns `{ appointments }`.

---

### Marketing bookings vs. regular bookings

`app/api/marketing-bookings/route.ts` is a **self-contained `/book` discovery-call flow** that does NOT use `createBooking()` and has its own constants and slot logic.

| Aspect | `/api/bookings/create` (+ `createBooking`) | `/api/marketing-bookings` |
|---|---|---|
| Business resolved via | `businessId`/`businessSlug` in body | `MARKETING_BUSINESS_ID` → `MARKETING_BUSINESS_SLUG` env |
| Timezone | per-business `business.timezone` | hard-coded `TIMEZONE = 'America/New_York'` |
| Hours / slots | per-business `businessHours`, `slotDurationMinutes`, `bufferMinutes` via Google freebusy | hard-coded `START_HOUR=8`, `END_HOUR=16`, `SLOT_MINUTES=30`, `BUFFER_MINUTES=15`, step 45 (8:00, 8:45, 9:30 … 3:30); `MIN_NOTICE_HOURS=2`, `MAX_DAYS_AHEAD=14` |
| Availability source | `getAvailableSlots` (Google + DB merge) | DB `confirmed` appointments only (`overlapsWithExisting`, buffer-aware); has its own `GET` returning `{ days: [{ date, isToday, label, timezoneLabel, slots:[{iso,display}] }] }` |
| Calendar event | `createCalendarEvent` (📱/🌐, no reminders) | `createMarketingCalendarEvent` (Discovery-Call title, 15-min popup+email reminders) |
| Confirmation SMS to customer | yes (`createBooking`) | **no SMS to customer**; instead Resend confirmation **email** to customer |
| Owner notification | `notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated` (SMTP + Telnyx) | direct Resend email to `YOUR_EMAIL ‖ business.ownerEmail ‖ jacob@…` + Telnyx SMS from `MARKETING_TELNYX_NUMBER` to `OWNER_PHONE ‖ business.ownerPhone` |
| serviceType | from caller, cleaned by `cleanServiceForOwner` | `deriveServiceType(extraNeeds)`: only "Just the Missed-Call AI system"/empty → `'Missed Call AI'`; extras → `'Consultation — interested in: Missed Call AI + …'`; non-array → `'General Consultation'` |
| `calendarEnabled` gate | required (400) | **not checked**; only `business.googleCalendarConnected` gates event creation |
| DB insert | `createBooking` | inline `db.appointment.create` (`source:'website'`, `duration:SLOT_MINUTES`, notes assembled from businessName/tradeType/extras/notes) |

`POST /api/marketing-bookings` body (`BookingPayload`): `{ name, phone, email, businessName, tradeType?, extraNeeds?, interests?(legacy), notes?, smsConsent, slotStart }`. Validation: required name/phone/email/businessName (400), `smsConsent` required (400), `slotStart` required + valid (400/`isNaN`). Slot re-validated against `isWithinBookingWindow` (≥2h notice, ≤14 days), `isWithinHours` (8–16 ET), `isValidSlotStart` (the 45-min cadence) → 409 if invalid; then same-day DB overlap → 409. If no marketing business configured → 503 with a "email jacob@…" message. Calendar-event failure sets `calendarSyncFailed` but proceeds. All three notifications (owner email, owner SMS, customer email) are independent best-effort try/catch blocks gated on their env vars. Error responses scrub Prisma/internal messages (regex on `unique constraint|foreign key|prisma|connection|ECONNREFUSED|timeout` or `length>120`) to a generic message. Success: `{ success:true, appointment: { id, scheduledAt } }`.

---

## 7. Owner Notifications, Transactional Email & Website Contact Intake

This subsystem covers all owner-facing notifications (SMS + email), the shared email-rendering helper, and the public `/api/contact` and `/api/book-demo` intake endpoints. **Critical ground truth: all owner-notification emails now send via Resend, not SMTP/nodemailer** (commit `735b7ce`). nodemailer is still imported but its code path is dead.

### Email transport — the full map

There are **four distinct email send mechanisms** in the codebase. They do NOT share a single helper:

| Send path | Transport | `from` address | Used by | Env required |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sendEmail()` in `lib/notify-owner.ts:696` | **Resend SDK** (`new Resend(...)`) | `notifications@alignandacquire.com` | All 6 owner notifiers | `RESEND_API_KEY` |
| `/api/contact` marketing branch | **Raw fetch** to `https://api.resend.com/emails` | `Align and Acquire <onboarding@resend.dev>` | Unattributed contact form → Jacob | `RESEND_API_KEY`, `YOUR_EMAIL` |
| `/api/book-demo` | **Raw fetch** to `https://api.resend.com/emails` | `MissedCall AI <onboarding@resend.dev>` | Demo request → Jacob | `RESEND_API_KEY`, `YOUR_EMAIL` |
| `/api/webhooks/voice` voicemail email (`route.ts:730`) | **Resend SDK** | `notifications@alignandacquire.com` | Voicemail alerts (`missedCallAiEnabled=false`) | `RESEND_API_KEY` |
| `/api/marketing-bookings` (out of subsystem, noted) | Raw fetch to Resend API | `Align and Acquire <onboarding@resend.dev>` | Owner + customer booking emails | `RESEND_API_KEY` |
| `/api/dashboard/emails` (out of subsystem, noted) | Resend SDK | `{senderName} <notifications@alignandacquire.com>` | Bulk email campaigns | `RESEND_API_KEY` |

**nodemailer is dead code.** `lib/notify-owner.ts:7` imports `nodemailer`; `:662` declares a module-level `transporter`; `:664-691` defines `getTransporter()` (reads `SMTP_USER`, `SMTP_PASS`, `SMTP_HOST`, `SMTP_PORT`, throws if user/pass missing). **Nothing calls `getTransporter()` anymore** — `sendEmail()` was rewritten in commit `735b7ce` to use Resend. The `SMTP_*` env vars are no longer consumed by any active code path.

### `sendEmail()` — the shared owner-email helper (`lib/notify-owner.ts:696-714`)

```typescript
async function sendEmail(to: string, subject: string, text: string, html?: string): Promise<void>
```

- Sends via `new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY)` → `resend.emails.send({ from: 'notifications@alignandacquire.com', to, subject, html })`.
- `html` arg: when provided (only `notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead` does this), sent as-is. When omitted, `text` is wrapped via `plainTextToEmailHtml(text)` (escapes + `white-space: pre-wrap` div so newlines survive).
- **Throws** if `result.error` is truthy (`Resend error: {message}`) or on any caught exception — this lets each caller's own `try/catch` log `sent`/`FAILED` correctly. Logs with `[NOTIFY OWNER]` prefix via `console.error`.

### Exported notify functions (`lib/notify-owner.ts`)

All notifiers gate SMS on `business.notifyBySms` and email on `business.notifyByEmail && business.ownerEmail`. **SMS recipient** is always `business.ownerPhone || business.forwardingNumber` (then `.trim()`; `notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated` and `notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead` additionally run it through `normalizeToE164()`). SMS is sent via a freshly-constructed `new Telnyx({ apiKey: process.env.TELNYX_API_KEY! })` `from: business.telnyxPhoneNumber`. SMS requires BOTH a recipient phone AND `business.telnyxPhoneNumber`, else it's skipped (logged). Timezone defaults to `'America/New_York'`.

`BusinessWithPhone` = `Pick<Business, 'id'|'name'|'slug'|'telnyxPhoneNumber'|'forwardingNumber'|'ownerEmail'|'ownerPhone'|'notifyBySms'|'notifyByEmail'|'timezone'>`.

| Function | Trigger / caller | Returns | SMS copy (key phrasing) | Email subject |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated(business, appointment)` | `createBooking()` (`lib/create-booking.ts:311`) after any appointment is created | `{ smsSent, emailSent }` | `📅 New Quote Request! {name} wants a quote for {service} on {date} at {time}.\nPhone: {phone}{📍 Address}{Notes}\nFull details in your email and dashboard.` | `New Quote Visit - {name} - {service} - {date}` |
| `notifyOwnerOnBookingRequestNoCalendar(business, params)` | **ORPHANED — no callers** | `void` | `📅 New Quote Request! {name} wants a quote for {service} around {date} at {time}. ...` | `New Quote Request - {name} - {service}` (body includes full transcript + "NOT been added to your calendar" warning) |
| `notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured(business, params)` | SMS webhook on `[READY_TO_CAPTURE]`/lead capture (`app/api/webhooks/sms/route.ts:209,440,679`) | `void` | `New lead! {name} wants {service} around {timeframe}. Address: {addr}. Phone: {phone}. Email: {email}. Check your email for the full conversation.` | `New Lead - {name} - {service}` (body has details + full transcript) |
| `notifyOwnerOnHumanNeeded(business, params)` | SMS webhook on `[HUMAN_NEEDED]` (`route.ts:636`) | `void` | `⚠️ A customer needs your help! {name} ({phone}) needs a personal follow-up. Check your email for the full conversation.` | `Follow-Up Needed - {name}` (body has reason + transcript) |
| `notifyOwnerOnAIFailed(business, params)` | SMS webhook when Claude API fails (`route.ts:408`) | `void` | `⚠️ AI was temporarily unavailable. We sent {name} ({phone}) a fallback message. Please follow up when you can.` | `AI Unavailable - {name} - Please Follow Up` (body has transcript) |
| `notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead(business, params)` | `/api/contact` for attributed client-tenant leads (`app/api/contact/route.ts:106`) | `{ smsSent, emailSent }` | `📩 New website lead! {name} just submitted your contact form. Phone: {phone}. Email: {email}. Check your dashboard for details.` | `New Website Lead - {name}` |

**`notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead` is the only notifier sending custom HTML.** It builds a branded HTML table email (`lib/notify-owner.ts:602-642`, commit `227bab6`) — header bar `#1A4A70`, rows for Name/Phone/Email/Message/Received — and passes both a plain-text fallback AND the `htmlBody` to `sendEmail(ownerEmail, subject, textBody, htmlBody)`. All user-supplied values are run through the local `escapeHtml()` (`:55-62`, escapes `& < > " '`) before interpolation. The other five notifiers build plain-text bodies (via a `.filter(Boolean).join('\n')` array pattern) and rely on `plainTextToEmailHtml` wrapping inside `sendEmail`.

**Transcript rendering** (LeadCaptured/HumanNeeded/AIFailed/BookingRequestNoCalendar): each `ConversationMessage` (`{ direction, content, createdAt }`) → `[{ts}] {label}: {content}` where `label` = `'Customer'` if `direction==='inbound'` else `business.name`, `ts` is `createdAt.toLocaleString('en-US', { timeZone: tz })`, joined by blank lines, bracketed by `'─'.repeat(40)`.

**Helpers:** `parseDatetimeInTimezone(str, tz)` parses `"YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm"` as business-local via `TZDate` (`:15-30`). `baseUrl` = `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL ?? (VERCEL_URL ? https://{VERCEL_URL} : 'https://alignandacquire.com')` (`:32-34`) — used for `/dashboard/conversations/{id}` links. **Note:** `notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated` hardcodes `https://alignandacquire.com/dashboard/appointments` (`:93`) instead of using `baseUrl`.

### `lib/email-format.ts`

```typescript
export function bodyContainsHtml(text: string): boolean   // /<[a-z][\s\S]*?>/i.test(text) — true if any tag-like token
export function plainTextToEmailHtml(text: string): string // escapeHtml(text) wrapped in <div style="white-space: pre-wrap; font-family: Arial; font-size:14px; line-height:1.5; color:#111">
```

`escapeHtml` here (module-private) escapes `& < > " '`. Shared by the email-campaign compose preview and the send route so preview matches delivered output. Used inside `sendEmail()` for the plain-text-only notifiers.

### `/api/contact` (`app/api/contact/route.ts`) — tenant-routed contact intake

`POST` body: `{ name, phone, message, smsConsent, businessId?, businessSlug?, email }`. Open endpoint (no auth, no rate limiting). CORS wide open (`Access-Control-Allow-Origin: '*'`, `OPTIONS` handler returns 204) so client-tenant sites can POST cross-origin. Local `escapeHtml()` (`:19-26`) escapes `& < > " '`.

**Validation:** `name` AND `smsConsent` required → else 400.

**Tenant routing** (`bid` = trimmed `businessId`, `slug` = trimmed `businessSlug`):

1. **Marketing branch** (`!bid && !slug && RESEND_API_KEY && YOUR_EMAIL`, `:47-68`): sends a Resend email to `process.env.YOUR_EMAIL` (Jacob) via raw fetch, `from: 'Align and Acquire <onboarding@resend.dev>'`, subject `New Contact Form: {name}`. Body interpolates all fields through `escapeHtml()`. **This is the ONLY branch that emails Jacob.**

2. **Client-tenant branch** (`bid || slug`, `:75-121`): looks up business by id or slug. If found, runs **sequentially and all AWAITED** (commit `965e758` — explicit comment: Vercel freezes the function after the response so unawaited work is dropped; sequential to avoid Prisma pool exhaustion):
   - `findOrCreateContact({ ..., source: 'website_form', notes: message })`
   - `db.websiteLead.create({ ..., status: 'new' })`
   - `notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead(business, {...})` wrapped in its own `try/catch` (`:105-119`) — a notify failure is logged but must NOT fail the request since the lead is already persisted.
   - **No email to Jacob in this branch** — client tenants get owner notifications via `notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead`.

Always returns `{ success: true }` (200) on the happy path; `{ success: false }` (500) on a thrown error. CORS headers on every response.

### `/api/book-demo` (`app/api/book-demo/route.ts`)

`POST` body: `{ name, business, email, phone, businessType }`. Logs the request, then if `RESEND_API_KEY && YOUR_EMAIL` sends a raw-fetch Resend email to `YOUR_EMAIL` (Jacob), `from: 'MissedCall AI <onboarding@resend.dev>'`, subject `New Demo Request: {business}`. **No HTML escaping** — fields are interpolated raw into the HTML body (minor XSS-into-email concern, but recipient is Jacob's own inbox). No DB writes, no auth, no CORS. Returns `{ success: true }` (200) or `{ success: false }` (500).

### Env vars consumed by this subsystem

| Env var | Where | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `RESEND_API_KEY` | `sendEmail()`, `/api/contact`, `/api/book-demo` | All email sends |
| `YOUR_EMAIL` | `/api/contact` (marketing), `/api/book-demo` | Jacob's recipient address |
| `TELNYX_API_KEY` | all notifiers | Owner SMS |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` / `VERCEL_URL` | `baseUrl` | Dashboard links in emails |
| `SMTP_USER` / `SMTP_PASS` / `SMTP_HOST` / `SMTP_PORT` | `getTransporter()` only | **DEAD** — no live caller |

---

## 8. CRM, Contacts, Cooldown, Conversation Buckets & Phone Utils

This section documents the contact/CRM data layer (`lib/crm-utils.ts`, `lib/contacts-check.ts`, `lib/sms-cooldown.ts`, `lib/import-contacts.ts`, `lib/conversation-buckets.ts`, `lib/phone-utils.ts`) and the dashboard contact/tag/job API routes. The single most important drift from CLAUDE.md: the `Contact` model now has an `isClientContact` boolean column, and a whole new split exists between "the client's own saved contacts" (`isClientContact = true`) and "system-generated leads" (`isClientContact = false`). The old `source IS NULL` rule still exists but only governs the automated-SMS suppression check, NOT the Contacts dashboard listing.

---

### `Contact` model — current schema (prisma/schema.prisma)

The model gained a field not in CLAUDE.md:

```prisma
isClientContact Boolean @default(false)
// True = client's own saved contact (their customer list / future voicemail list).
// Written & displayed only; NOT read during call routing.  [per the schema comment]
```

Full field list (confirmed from schema): `id`, `businessId`, `phoneNumber`, `name`, `createdAt`, `email`, `address`, `city`, `state`, `zip`, `source` (nullable: `missed_call, website_form, manual, referral, google_ad`), **`isClientContact` (Boolean, default false)**, `status` (default `"new"`: new/contacted/quoted/booked/completed/lost), `notes` (`@db.Text`), `lastContactedAt`, `updatedAt`, `totalRevenue` (Float, default 0). Relations: `contactTags[]`, `jobs[]`, `emailRecipients[]`, `activities[]`. Constraints: `@@unique([businessId, phoneNumber])`, `@@index([businessId])`.

> NOTE: the schema comment says `isClientContact` is "NOT read during call routing," but `lib/contacts-check.ts` DOES read it in `isClientVoicemailContact()`, which IS called from the voice webhook routing. See the contacts-check section below — this is an internal inconsistency between the comment and the code.

---

### `lib/phone-utils.ts`

Three pure functions, no DB, no env. US-centric.

```typescript
normalizePhoneNumber(phone: string): string
// digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, '')
// 11 digits starting with '1' → drop leading 1 (return last 10)
// else → return digits as-is (10-digit kept; international kept full)

normalizeToE164(phone: string | undefined | null): string
// non-string/empty → ''
// 10 digits        → "+1" + digits
// 11 digits, starts '1' → "+" + digits
// >=10 digits (other) → "+1" + last 10 digits   ← lossy for true international
// <10 digits        → ''

phonesMatch(a: string, b: string): boolean
// normalize both; if both >=10 digits → compare last 10; else strict equality of normalized
```

Gotcha: `normalizeToE164` forces `+1` on any input ≥10 digits that isn't exactly an 11-digit `1`-prefixed number, so a real international number gets mangled to `+1` + its last 10 digits.

---

### `lib/crm-utils.ts`

Constant: `PLACEHOLDER_PHONE_PREFIX = '__email_only__'` — used to synthesize a unique placeholder `phoneNumber` for email-only contacts (the `Contact.phoneNumber` column is non-null and unique per business). Placeholder form: `` `${PLACEHOLDER_PHONE_PREFIX}${Date.now().toString(36)}` ``.

#### `findExistingContact(businessId, phoneNumber, email)`

```typescript
findExistingContact(
  businessId: string,
  phoneNumber: string | undefined | null,
  email: string | undefined | null
): Promise<{ id, phoneNumber, email, name, address, city, state, zip, notes } | null>
```

Dedupe order: **phone first, then email.**
- Phone path: only if `normalizePhoneNumber(phone).length >= 10`. Loads `db.contact.findMany({ where: { businessId } })` (ALL contacts for the business, no `isClientContact`/`source` filter) and finds the first where `phonesMatch(phone, c.phoneNumber)`. This is an in-memory scan, not a DB phone equality query — intentional, because stored phones are in mixed formats (E.164 vs bare 10-digit).
- Email path (only if no phone match): `db.contact.findFirst({ where: { businessId, email: <trimmed> } })` — exact, case-sensitive equality.
- Returns `null` if neither matches.

#### `findOrCreateContact(params)`

```typescript
type FindOrCreateContactParams = {
  businessId: string
  phoneNumber?: string | null
  email?: string | null
  name?: string | null
  source: string                 // REQUIRED
  address?: string | null
  city?: string | null
  state?: string | null
  zip?: string | null
  notes?: string | null
  skipUpdateIfExists?: boolean    // if existing found → return {isDuplicate:true} without writing
  isClientContact?: boolean       // CREATE-ONLY; applied only on insert, never on update
}
findOrCreateContact(params): Promise<{ id, phoneNumber, name, email, source, isDuplicate? } | null>
```

Control flow:
1. Requires phone≥10 digits OR a non-empty email; else returns `null`.
2. `findExistingContact(businessId, rawPhone, email)`.
3. **If existing + `skipUpdateIfExists`** → returns `{ id, phoneNumber, name, email, source: null, isDuplicate: true }`. (Note: `source` is hard-coded `null` in this branch, not the existing row's source.)
4. **If existing (no skip flag)** → builds a partial-update object that only fills fields currently empty on the existing row (`name`, `email`, `address`, `city`, `state`, `zip` use `if (incoming && !existing.x)`); `notes` is **appended** (`existing.notes + "\n\n" + new`); `lastContactedAt = now()`. **`isClientContact` is NOT touched on update** — never upgraded or downgraded for an existing row. Then creates an `Activity` (`type = source`, description from `activityDescription`).
5. **If not found** → creates a `Contact`:
   - `phoneNumber` = `normalizeToE164(rawPhone) || rawPhone.trim()` when a real phone exists, else the `__email_only__<base36>` placeholder.
   - `source` set; `status: 'new'`; `lastContactedAt: now()`; **`isClientContact: isClientContact === true`** (strict; defaults false).
   - Then creates an `Activity` (`type = source`).
6. On any thrown DB error → logs `[findOrCreateContact]` and returns `null` (never throws).

`activityDescription(type, source)` maps known activity types to friendly text (`missed_call`→"New lead from missed call", `website_form`→"Contact form submission", `sms_conversation`→"SMS conversation", `voicemail`→"Voicemail received", `manual`→"Added manually", `referral`→"Referral", `google_ad`→"Lead from Google ad"); unknown types fall back to `type` with underscores replaced by spaces.

Callers of `findOrCreateContact` (and the `isClientContact` they pass):
| Caller | source | isClientContact |
|---|---|---|
| `app/api/contact/route.ts:82` (website form) | `website_form` | not set → false |
| `app/api/dashboard/messages/campaign/route.ts:162` | (campaign) | not set → false |
| `app/api/dashboard/messages/send/route.ts:106` | (manual SMS) | not set → false |
| `app/api/dashboard/contacts/import/route.ts:79` | client-chosen | **`true`** + `skipUpdateIfExists: true` |
| `app/api/webhooks/sms/route.ts` (309, 467, 711) | `missed_call` | not set → false (fire-and-forget `void`) |

---

### `lib/contacts-check.ts` — THREE functions now (not two)

#### `isExistingContact(businessId, callerPhone): Promise<boolean>`
Still queries `source: null` (verbatim: `where: { businessId, source: null }`). Loads only those contacts' `phoneNumber`, returns false if `normalizePhoneNumber(callerPhone).length < 10`, else `phonesMatch` against any. **This is the gate that suppresses automated missed-call SMS** — only contacts with `source IS NULL` (admin-curated whitelist) block SMS. It does NOT consult `isClientContact`.

#### `isClientVoicemailContact(businessId, callerPhone): Promise<boolean>` — NEW, undocumented in CLAUDE.md
```typescript
where: { businessId, isClientContact: true }
```
Same `phonesMatch` last-10 logic. Used by the **voice webhook (`app/api/webhooks/voice/route.ts`, ~10 call sites)** to detect whether a caller is one of the client's own saved contacts, for known-contact voicemail routing. This contradicts the schema column comment ("NOT read during call routing").

#### `logContactSkip(businessId, phoneNumber, messageType?): Promise<void>`
Inserts a `CooldownSkipLog` with `reason: 'existing_contact'`, `lastMessageSent: new Date(0)` (epoch sentinel — required non-null column, never actually sent), `messageType: messageType ?? 'missed_call'`. Swallows errors (try/catch + console.error). Called from `voice/route.ts:989`, `voice-gather/route.ts:134`, `voice-dial-status/route.ts:87`.

---

### `lib/sms-cooldown.ts`

Module constants: `DEFAULT_COOLDOWN_DAYS = 7`; `ENV_COOLDOWN_DAYS = process.env.SMS_COOLDOWN_DAYS ? parseInt(...,10) : null` (parsed once at module load).

```typescript
interface CooldownCheckResult { allowed: boolean; lastMessageSent?: Date }

getCooldownDays(business: { smsCooldownDays?: number|null } | null): number
// priority: business.smsCooldownDays (if != null AND > 0) > ENV_COOLDOWN_DAYS (if != null AND > 0) > 7
// NOTE: a business value of 0 is ignored (must be > 0) — you cannot disable cooldown via 0.

isCooldownBypassNumber(callerPhone: string, cooldownBypassNumbers: unknown): boolean
// coerces non-array to []; returns true if any string entry phonesMatch(callerPhone, entry.trim())

checkCooldown(businessId, phoneNumber, business?): Promise<CooldownCheckResult>
// cutoff = now - cooldownDays*86_400_000ms
// looks up ContactCooldown by composite unique (businessId_phoneNumber)
// no record → { allowed: true }
// record.lastMessageSent >= cutoff → { allowed: false, lastMessageSent }
// else → { allowed: true }

recordMessageSent(businessId, phoneNumber): Promise<void>
// upsert ContactCooldown (businessId_phoneNumber); set lastMessageSent = now() on create+update

logCooldownSkip(businessId, phoneNumber, lastMessageSent, messageType?): Promise<void>
// inserts CooldownSkipLog with reason DEFAULTING to 'cooldown' (column default; not passed here),
// messageType: messageType ?? null. Swallows errors.
```

`checkCooldown` matches `ContactCooldown` by **exact `phoneNumber` string** (composite unique lookup), NOT by `phonesMatch`. So cooldown correctness depends on callers passing the same normalized phone form they recorded with — they pass the raw `callerPhone` consistently across `checkCooldown`/`recordMessageSent`. Callers: `voice/route.ts`, `voice-gather/route.ts`, `voice-dial-status/route.ts`, plus `recordMessageSent` from `sms/route.ts:2544` and `lib/create-booking.ts:301`.

---

### `lib/import-contacts.ts` — `parseContactFile(file): Promise<ParseResult>`

Client-side-style parser (uses `FileReader` + `readAsBinaryString` + `XLSX.read(type:'binary')`). Note: this is the browser-side column-detection parser; the actual import API route (`/api/dashboard/contacts/import`) receives an already-parsed `contacts[]` JSON array (see below), so `parseContactFile` runs in the import UI, not the route.

```typescript
interface ParsedContact { phoneNumber: string; name: string | null }
interface ParseResult { contacts: ParsedContact[]; totalRows: number; invalidSkipped: number }
```

Column auto-detection:
- Phone header names: `['phone','mobile','cell','number','telephone','phone number','contact','phonenumber']` (lowercased/trimmed exact match). Fallback if no header matches: scans up to first 100 rows; picks the column where ≥50% of values "look like a phone" (`looksLikePhone`: 10–15 digits after stripping non-digits) and that has the highest phone-like count.
- Name header names: `['name','first name','firstname','full name','fullname','contact name','contactname']`. No fallback (returns -1 → name `null`).

Behavior: first sheet only; `header:1`, `raw:false`. `<2` rows → empty result. Rejects with an error if no phone column found. De-dupes within the file via a `Set` of normalized phones. Skips rows whose normalized phone `< 10` digits (counts toward `invalidSkipped`). `name = String(row[nameCol]).trim() || null`. `totalRows` = data rows (excludes header).

---

### `lib/conversation-buckets.ts`

```typescript
type ConversationBucket = 'cold' | 'active' | 'stalled' | 'closed'
type ConversationForBucket = {
  status: string
  messages: { direction: string }[]
  lastMessageAt: Date | string
  customerEmail: string | null
  customerAddress: string | null
  customerTimeframe: string | null
  appointment: { id: string } | null
}
getConversationBucket(conv): ConversationBucket
```

Classification order:
1. **`closed`** if ANY of: `status === 'lead_captured'`, `status === 'appointment_booked'`, `appointment != null`, OR any of `customerEmail`/`customerAddress`/`customerTimeframe` truthy.
2. else **`cold`** if no message has `direction === 'inbound'` (only the outbound AI greeting exists).
3. else compare `lastMessageAt` to `now - 48h`: newer → **`active`**, older → **`stalled`**.

The 48h cutoff is hard-coded here (`48 * 60 * 60 * 1000`).

```typescript
BUCKET_LABELS = { cold:'Cold', active:'Active', stalled:'Stalled', closed:'Closed' }
BUCKET_COLORS = {
  cold:'bg-gray-500/10 text-gray-400', active:'bg-green-500/10 text-green-400',
  stalled:'bg-yellow-500/10 text-yellow-400', closed:'bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400',
}
```

IMPORTANT: `BUCKET_LABELS` now holds the **plain bucket names** (`Cold/Active/Stalled/Closed`), contradicting CLAUDE.md which claims the client-facing labels (`No Reply / In Progress / Went Quiet / Closed`) live in `BUCKET_LABELS`. Those client-facing labels are now a **local `TAB_LABELS` map inside `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/conversations/ConversationsClient.tsx`** (`all:'All', cold:'No Reply', active:'In Progress', stalled:'Went Quiet', closed:'Closed'`). `BUCKET_LABELS` is consumed by the admin per-business conversations page (`app/admin/[businessId]/conversations/page.tsx`) and `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/leads/CombinedLeadsList.tsx` — those surfaces show "Cold/Active/Stalled".

---

### Contact / Tag / Job API routes

All routes call `requireDashboardBusiness()` first; if it returns a `NextResponse` (401/404), that is returned immediately. All are scoped to `business.id`. No explicit method-level auth beyond that helper.

#### `GET /api/dashboard/contacts`
Query: `search?` (matches `name` ILIKE, `phoneNumber` contains, `email` ILIKE via `OR`), `status?` (exact; `all` or empty = no filter). **Hard-filters `isClientContact: true`** — the Contacts page shows ONLY the client's own saved contacts; system-generated leads (`missed_call`/`website_form`/etc., which are `isClientContact:false`) are excluded and live in the Leads area. Orders by `updatedAt desc`, includes `contactTags.tag`. Returns `{ contacts: [{ id, name, phoneNumber, email, source, status, tags:[{id,name,color}], lastContactedAt, totalRevenue, updatedAt }], total }`.

#### `POST /api/dashboard/contacts`
Body: `{ name?, phone?, email?, address?, city?, state?, zip?, source, notes? }`. Validation: requires (phone with ≥10 digits) OR email → else 400 "Phone or email is required"; requires `source` → else 400 "Source is required". `phoneNumber` = `normalizeToE164(phone) || phone` when phone present, else `__email_only__<base36>` placeholder. Creates `Contact` with **`isClientContact: true`** (manual add = client's own), `status:'new'`. Also creates an `Activity` (`type:'manual'`, "Contact added manually"). Returns the created contact (same shape as list rows minus `updatedAt`). Does NOT use `findOrCreateContact` — direct `db.contact.create`, so a duplicate phone within the business would hit the `@@unique([businessId, phoneNumber])` constraint and throw (uncaught → 500).

#### `GET /api/dashboard/contacts/[id]`
`params` is a Promise (`await params`). 404 if not found in business. Includes `contactTags.tag`, `activities` (desc, take 100), `jobs` (by `scheduledDate desc`). Also loads ALL business conversations with `messages: { some: {} }`, then **in-memory filters** by `phonesMatch(conv.callerPhone, contact.phoneNumber)` to attach related conversations (`id, callerPhone, status, lastMessageAt, lastMessage`). Returns full contact detail object.

#### `PATCH /api/dashboard/contacts/[id]`
404 if not in business. Allow-list: `name, phoneNumber, email, address, city, state, zip, source, status, notes`. For `phoneNumber`: `normalizeToE164(trim) || trim`. Other strings: `trim() || null`. Explicit `null` passes through. **`isClientContact` is NOT in the allow-list** — cannot be toggled via this route. Returns updated contact (with tags).

#### `DELETE /api/dashboard/contacts/[id]` — NEW, not in CLAUDE.md
404 if not found; otherwise `db.contact.delete({ where: { id } })`, returns `{ ok: true }`. Hard delete.

#### `GET /api/dashboard/contacts/[id]/activities`
404 if contact not in business. Returns `{ activities }` (all for `contactId`+`businessId`, `createdAt desc`, no limit).

#### `POST /api/dashboard/contacts/[id]/activities` — NEW, not in CLAUDE.md
Body: `{ content? }`. Creates an `Activity` with `type: 'note_added'`, `description = content.trim() || 'Note added'`. Returns `{ activity }`. (CLAUDE.md documents only GET for this path.)

#### `POST /api/dashboard/contacts/import`
Body: `{ source, contacts: ImportContact[], dryRun? }` where `ImportContact = { name?, phone?, email?, address?, city?, state?, zip?, notes?, serviceHistory? }`. Requires non-empty `source` (else 400). Each contact must have phone≥10 digits OR an email containing `@`.
- **dryRun=true** → counts duplicates only (via `findExistingContact`), returns `{ imported:0, duplicates, errors:0 }` (no writes).
- **real run** → processes in batches of `BATCH_SIZE = 25` (sequential within batch). For each: merges `notes` + `serviceHistory` (joined by `\n\n`), calls `findOrCreateContact({ ..., source, skipUpdateIfExists: true, isClientContact: true })`. Tallies: `null`→`errors++`, `isDuplicate`→`duplicates++`, else `imported++`. Returns `{ imported, duplicates, errors }`.

#### `GET /api/dashboard/tags`
Returns `{ tags }` for business, `name asc`.

#### `POST /api/dashboard/tags`
Body `{ name, color? }`. 400 if no name. 409 if `(businessId, name)` already exists (checked via `businessId_name` unique). Creates with `color || '#6B7280'`. Returns `{ tag }`.

#### `GET /api/dashboard/jobs`
Query `status?` (`all`/empty = no filter). Returns `{ jobs: [{ id, contactId, contactName (contact.name ?? phoneNumber), serviceName, description, scheduledDate, completedDate, amount, status, notes, createdAt }] }`, ordered `scheduledDate desc`.

#### `POST /api/dashboard/jobs`
Body `{ contactId, serviceName, description?, scheduledDate?, amount?, notes? }`. 404 if contact not in business; 400 if no `serviceName`. Creates `Job` (`status:'scheduled'`, `scheduledDate` parsed via `new Date()` or null). Also creates `Activity` (`type:'job_created'`, description ``Job created: <serviceName>``, `metadata:{ jobId }`). Returns `{ job }`.

#### `PATCH /api/dashboard/jobs/[id]`
404 if not in business. Allow-list: `serviceName, description, scheduledDate, completedDate, amount, status, notes`. Date fields parsed via `new Date()` or null. Other fields passed through raw (no trim/validation). Returns `{ job }`. No DELETE handler exists in this file (CLAUDE.md lists `DELETE` for `jobs/[id]` — it is NOT implemented).

---

### `scripts/backfill-isClientContact.ts`

One-off migration helper establishing the `isClientContact` data. Dry-run by default; `--apply` writes. It only ever flips rows to `true` (the `false` bucket is the column default). Client-own rule (`OR`):
- `source IS NULL` (admin-curated hand lists)
- `source ENDS WITH '_import'` (e.g. `jobber_import`, `excel_import`, `servicetitan_import`)
- `source IN ['manual','manual_list']` (and `'referral'` only if `INCLUDE_REFERRAL=true`, default false)

Left `false`: `missed_call`, `website_form`, `sms_conversation`, `google_ad`. This is the canonical definition of which historical contacts count as "the client's own saved contacts" vs system-generated leads — and it explains why the live `POST /contacts` and import routes set `isClientContact:true`, while webhook/website/campaign-created contacts leave it false.

---

## 9. Dashboard Read APIs (analytics, conversations, messages, voicemails, screened-calls, website-leads)

Ground-truth reference for the read-oriented dashboard API routes. All paths are under `app/api/dashboard/`. Two distinct auth patterns are in use:

- **`requireDashboardBusiness()`** (`lib/dashboard-auth.ts`) — returns `{ business }` or a `NextResponse` (401 `Unauthorized` if no Clerk `userId`, 404 `No business found` if no business resolves). Internally: `auth()` → `db.user.findUnique({ where: { clerkId }, include: { business } })` → `getBusinessForDashboard(userId, user?.business ?? null)` (honors the admin `adminViewAs` cookie). Used by: analytics, conversations, messages (list/detail/contacts/send), voicemails DELETE, website-leads.
- **Raw `auth()` + manual lookup** — `voicemails` GET and `screened-calls` GET do NOT use `requireDashboardBusiness()`. They inline the exact same three steps (`auth()`, `db.user.findUnique`, `getBusinessForDashboard`) and return the same 401/404 shapes. Functionally equivalent, just not refactored.

`getBusinessFeatures(business)` (`lib/business-features.ts`) derives feature flags. `totalCallsMode` is `'screened'` when `spamFilterEnabled || callScreenerEnabled`, else `'calls'`. `hasMissedCallAi = missedCallAiEnabled !== false`.

### Route summary

| Route | Method | Auth | Feature gate (403) | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `/api/dashboard/analytics` | GET | `requireDashboardBusiness()` | none | `period` query param; returns `features` + `totalCallsMode` |
| `/api/dashboard/conversations` | GET | `requireDashboardBusiness()` | `!business.missedCallAiEnabled` → 403 `Feature not available` | excludes screening/forwarding statuses |
| `/api/dashboard/messages` | GET | `requireDashboardBusiness()` | none | conversation list w/ contact join + last message |
| `/api/dashboard/messages/[conversationId]` | GET | `requireDashboardBusiness()` | none | 404 if not found/not owned |
| `/api/dashboard/messages/contacts` | GET | `requireDashboardBusiness()` | none | top 200 contacts for compose |
| `/api/dashboard/messages/send` | POST | `requireDashboardBusiness()` | none | sends Telnyx SMS + Message record |
| `/api/dashboard/voicemails` | GET | raw `auth()` | none | conversations w/ `recordingUrl != null` |
| `/api/dashboard/voicemails/[id]` | DELETE | `requireDashboardBusiness()` | none | soft-delete (clears recording fields) |
| `/api/dashboard/screened-calls` | GET | raw `auth()` | none | `days` query param; blocked/passed stats |
| `/api/dashboard/website-leads` | GET, PATCH | `requireDashboardBusiness()` | `!business.missedCallAiEnabled` → 403 (GET only) | PATCH updates lead status |

> Only `conversations` and `website-leads` GET are feature-gated. `analytics`, `voicemails`, and `screened-calls` are NOT gated server-side (gating is done in the UI / `getBusinessFeatures`).

---

### `GET /api/dashboard/analytics`

`app/api/dashboard/analytics/route.ts`. Query param: `period` (default `'month'`). Accepted values:

```typescript
type Period = 'today' | 'week' | 'month' | 'year' | 'all'
```

Any unrecognized value falls back to `'month'`. **`'year'` is a real, supported period** (not in CLAUDE.md). `getDateRanges(period)` (lines 8–63) computes a `current` and `previous` window:

| period | current window | previous window |
|---|---|---|
| `today` | start-of-today → now | yesterday-midnight → today-midnight |
| `week` | (today − 7d) → now | (today − 14d) → (today − 7d) |
| `month` | (today − 30d) → now | (today − 60d) → (today − 30d) |
| `year` | Jan 1 this year → now | Jan 1 last year → Jan 1 this year |
| `all` | `{ since: null, until: null }` | `{ since: null, until: null }` |

`currentDateFilter` becomes `{ gte: current.since }` or `undefined` (all-time). `previousDateFilter` becomes `{ gte, lt }` or `undefined`. All windows are computed in **server local time** via `new Date(y, m, d)` — no business-timezone (TZDate) handling here.

**`totalCalls` computation** depends on `features.totalCallsMode`:
- `'screened'` → `db.screenedCall.count({ where: { businessId, createdAt? } })` (all screened calls, both blocked + passed).
- `'calls'` → `db.conversation.count({ where: { businessId, callSid: { not: null }, createdAt? } })` (inbound call-originated conversations only).

`previousTotalCalls` mirrors the same mode; resolves to `Promise.resolve(0)` when no `previousDateFilter`.

All other metrics run in one `Promise.all` (lines 126–223):

| Metric | Query |
|---|---|
| `callsBlocked` | `screenedCall.count` where `result: 'blocked'` |
| `callsPassed` | `screenedCall.count` where `result: 'passed'` |
| `leadsCaptured` | `contact.count` where `source IN ['missed_call','website_form']` — **NOT** Conversation status |
| `websiteLeads` | `websiteLead.count` |
| `messagesSent` | `message.count` where `direction: 'outbound'` AND `conversation.businessId == business.id` (note: `createdAt` filter applied directly on Message) |
| `previousLeadsCaptured` | same as `leadsCaptured` over previous window (or 0) |
| `voicemailsCount` | `conversation.count` where `recordingUrl: { not: null }` |
| `leadSourcesRaw` | `contact.groupBy({ by: ['source'], where: { source: { not: null }, createdAt? }, _count: { _all } })` |
| `recentActivityRaw` | `activity.findMany` ordered `createdAt desc`, `take: 20`, includes `contact { name, phoneNumber }` |

`leadSources` is post-processed into fixed buckets (lines 225–265). Recognized direct keys: `missed_call`, `website_form`, `referral`, `google_ad`, `manual` (null/unknown source → counted as `manual`). A set of import sources (`jobber_import`, `servicetitan_import`, `housecallpro_import`, `quickbooks_import`, `square_import`, `google_contacts_import`, `excel_import`, `other_crm_import`, `manual_list`) all collapse into a single `imports` bucket. Sources not in either list are silently dropped.

**Response shape:**

```json
{
  "totalCalls": 0,
  "callsBlocked": 0,
  "callsPassed": 0,
  "leadsCapured": 0,          // typo'd duplicate key (sic) — same value as leadsCaptured
  "leadsCaptured": 0,
  "websiteLeads": 0,
  "messagesSent": 0,
  "voicemailsCount": 0,
  "previousTotalCalls": 0,     // null when period === 'all'
  "previousLeadsCaptured": 0,  // null when period === 'all'
  "leadSources": { "missed_call": 0, "website_form": 0, "referral": 0, "google_ad": 0, "imports": 0, "manual": 0 },
  "recentActivity": [ { "id", "type", "description", "createdAt", "contactName", "contactPhone" } ],
  "features": { /* full BusinessFeatures object */ },
  "totalCallsMode": "screened" | "calls"
}
```

**Gotcha:** the response intentionally(?) emits BOTH `leadsCapured` (misspelled) and `leadsCaptured` with identical values (lines 280–281). Any client reading `leadsCapured` will keep working; do not remove either without auditing consumers. On error: `500 { error: 'Failed to load analytics' }`, logged as `[dashboard-analytics]`.

---

### `GET /api/dashboard/conversations`

`app/api/dashboard/conversations/route.ts`. Feature gate: `if (!business.missedCallAiEnabled) → 403 { error: 'Feature not available' }`. No query params.

Query: `conversation.findMany` where `businessId` AND `messages: { some: {} }` (must have ≥1 message) AND `NOT { status IN ['screening','screening_blocked','forwarding'] }` — i.e. screening-only and forwarding-only conversations are excluded. Ordered `lastMessageAt desc`. Selects scalar conversation fields plus `appointment: { select: { id } }` and **all** `messages` (`{ id, direction, content, createdAt }` ordered `createdAt asc`).

```json
{ "conversations": [ {
  "id", "callerPhone", "callerName", "status", "summary", "intent",
  "serviceRequested", "createdAt", "lastMessageAt",
  "customerEmail", "customerAddress", "customerTimeframe",
  "appointment": { "id" } | null,
  "messages": [ { "id", "direction", "content", "createdAt" } ]
} ] }
```

No pagination/search/status query params (despite CLAUDE.md implying generic filtering exists on the conversations API — that lives on `/messages`, not here). Bucketing (`cold/active/stalled/closed`) is done client-side via `lib/conversation-buckets.ts`.

---

### `GET /api/dashboard/messages` (conversation list)

`app/api/dashboard/messages/route.ts`. **No query params** — there is no `page`, `search`, `status`, or `limit` handling in the actual code (CLAUDE.md claims these exist; they do not).

Query: `conversation.findMany` where `businessId`, `messages: { some: {} }`, and (if `business.telnyxPhoneNumber` set) `callerPhone: { not: business.telnyxPhoneNumber }` (filters out self-calls / forwarding-loop artifacts). Ordered `lastMessageAt desc`. Includes the single most-recent message (`messages: { orderBy: createdAt desc, take: 1 }`).

Then per-conversation it issues an N+1 `contact.findFirst({ where: { businessId, phoneNumber: callerPhone } })` to resolve a CRM name/id. `unreadCount` is hardcoded `0`.

```json
{ "conversations": [ {
  "conversationId",
  "contactId": string | null,
  "contactName": contact.name ?? conversation.callerName ?? null,
  "contactPhone": contact.phoneNumber ?? conversation.callerPhone,
  "lastMessage": string | null,
  "lastMessageAt",
  "unreadCount": 0
} ] }
```

---

### `GET /api/dashboard/messages/[conversationId]`

`app/api/dashboard/messages/[conversationId]/route.ts`. Path param `conversationId`. `conversation.findFirst({ where: { id, businessId }, include: { messages: { orderBy: createdAt asc } } })`. 404 `Conversation not found` if not found or not owned.

```json
{ "messages": [ {
  "id",
  "body": message.content,        // renamed: content → body
  "direction": "inbound" | "outbound",
  "createdAt",
  "status": message.telnyxStatus ?? null
} ] }
```

---

### `GET /api/dashboard/messages/contacts`

`app/api/dashboard/messages/contacts/route.ts`. `contact.findMany` where `businessId`, ordered `updatedAt desc`, `take: 200`, selecting `id, name, phoneNumber, status` and `contactTags.tag { id, name }`.

```json
{ "contacts": [ {
  "id", "name", "phoneNumber", "status",
  "tags": [ { "id", "name" } ]
} ] }
```

---

### `POST /api/dashboard/messages/send`

`app/api/dashboard/messages/send/route.ts`. **Request body uses `to`/`body`, NOT `conversationId`/`text`** (CLAUDE.md is wrong on the body shape):

```typescript
type SendBody = { to: string; body: string; conversationId?: string }
```

Validation: invalid JSON → 400 `Invalid JSON`; missing `to` or `body` (after `.trim()`) → 400 `to and body are required`; no `business.telnyxPhoneNumber` → 400 `Business is not configured with a Telnyx phone number`.

Flow:
1. `to = normalizeToE164(toRaw) || toRaw`.
2. Resolve `conversationId`: if not supplied in body, find newest conversation with `callerPhone == to`; else create a new `Conversation` (`status: 'active'`, `manualMode: true`, `lastMessageAt: now`).
3. Re-fetch conversation scoped to business (404 `Conversation not found` if missing).
4. Send via `new Telnyx({ apiKey: TELNYX_API_KEY }).messages.send({ from: business.telnyxPhoneNumber, to, text })`. `messageId = data.id`; `status = data.to[0].status ?? 'sent'`.
5. Create `Message` (`direction: 'outbound'`, `content: text`, `telnyxSid`, `telnyxStatus`). **DB write is awaited here** (unlike the deferred-write pattern in the SMS webhook).
6. `conversation.update` → `lastMessageAt: now`, `manualMode: true` (every manual send forces the conversation into manual mode → AI stops auto-replying).
7. Fire-and-forget `findOrCreateContact({ businessId, phoneNumber: conversation.callerPhone, source: 'sms_conversation' }).catch(() => {})`.

Telnyx failure → 500 `Failed to send SMS`, logged `[dashboard-messages-send] Telnyx error`.

```json
{
  "conversationId",
  "message": { "id", "body", "direction", "createdAt", "status" },
  "contactLabel": null
}
```

Note: it does NOT call `recordMessageSent()` (no cooldown bump for manual sends) and does NOT go through `sendSMSAndLog`. Env: `TELNYX_API_KEY`.

---

### `GET /api/dashboard/voicemails`

`app/api/dashboard/voicemails/route.ts`. **Auth: raw `auth()` + manual lookup, not `requireDashboardBusiness()`.** Not feature-gated.

Two parallel queries: conversations where `recordingUrl: { not: null }` (ordered `createdAt desc`, selecting `id, callerPhone, recordingUrl, voicemailTranscription, createdAt`) and all business contacts (`{ phoneNumber, name }`). Builds a `Map<normalizePhoneNumber(phone), name>` for name resolution.

```json
{ "voicemails": [ {
  "conversationId",
  "callerPhone",
  "contactName": string | null,
  "recordingUrl",
  "voicemailTranscription": string | null,
  "createdAt"
} ] }
```

Error → 500 `Failed to fetch voicemails`.

---

### `DELETE /api/dashboard/voicemails/[id]`

`app/api/dashboard/voicemails/[id]/route.ts`. Auth: `requireDashboardBusiness()`. Path param `id`.

**Soft-delete:** finds conversation `where { id, businessId, recordingUrl: { not: null } }`. 404 `Voicemail not found` if no such conversation. Then `conversation.update` sets `recordingUrl: null` and `voicemailTranscription: null` — the Conversation row itself is preserved; only the recording/transcription fields are cleared. Does NOT delete the underlying Vercel Blob mp3 (orphaned blob remains in storage). Returns `{ success: true }`.

---

### `GET /api/dashboard/screened-calls`

`app/api/dashboard/screened-calls/route.ts`. **Auth: raw `auth()` + manual lookup.** Not feature-gated. Query param `days` (default `30`, clamped to `[1, 90]`).

`since` computation: when `days === 1`, `since` = start-of-today (local midnight) for "today" stats; otherwise rolling window `Date.now() - days*24h`.

Four parallel `screenedCall` queries scoped to `createdAt >= since`: `blocked` (count `result: 'blocked'`), `passed` (count `result: 'passed'`), `total` (count all), `recentCalls` (`findMany` ordered `createdAt desc`, `take: 100`).

```json
{
  "stats": {
    "blocked": 0, "passed": 0, "total": 0,
    "blockRate": 0,            // round(blocked/total*100), or 0 when total==0
    "days": 30
  },
  "recentCalls": [ { "id", "callerPhone", "result", "createdAt" } ]
}
```

Error → 500 `Failed to fetch screened calls`.

---

### `GET` / `PATCH /api/dashboard/website-leads`

`app/api/dashboard/website-leads/route.ts`. Auth: `requireDashboardBusiness()`.

**GET:** feature gate `if (!business.missedCallAiEnabled) → 403 { error: 'Feature not available' }`. `websiteLead.findMany({ where: { businessId }, orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' } })` (full rows, no select). Returns `{ leads }`.

**PATCH (undocumented in CLAUDE.md):** body `{ leadId, status }`. **No `missedCallAiEnabled` gate on PATCH** (only GET is gated). Validation: missing `leadId`/`status` → 400; `status` must be in `VALID_STATUSES = ['new','contacted','converted','closed']` else 400 `Invalid status. Must be one of: ...`. Verifies lead belongs to business (404 `Lead not found`), then `websiteLead.update`. Returns `{ lead: updated }`.

---

## 10. Outreach: Bulk Email + SMS Campaigns + Image Upload

The Outreach subsystem unifies bulk **email** (Resend) and bulk **SMS** (Telnyx) campaigns under one `/dashboard/outreach` page, gated by `business.massMessagingEnabled`. All five backend routes return **403 `{ error: 'Feature not available' }`** when `massMessagingEnabled` is falsy (checked AFTER `requireDashboardBusiness()`).

### Route inventory

| Route | Method | File | Gating | Returns |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `/api/dashboard/emails` | GET | `app/api/dashboard/emails/route.ts` | 403 if `!massMessagingEnabled` | `{ campaigns: [{id, subject, status, recipientCount, sentAt, createdAt}] }` |
| `/api/dashboard/emails` | POST | same | 403 if `!massMessagingEnabled` | `{ campaign: {id, subject, status:'sent', recipientCount, sentAt} }` (synchronous full send) |
| `/api/dashboard/emails/[id]` | GET | `app/api/dashboard/emails/[id]/route.ts` | 403 if `!massMessagingEnabled` | `{ campaign: {id, senderName, subject, body, status, recipientCount, sentAt, createdAt} }`, 404 if not found |
| `/api/dashboard/messages/campaign` | POST | `app/api/dashboard/messages/campaign/route.ts` | 403 if `!massMessagingEnabled` | `{ sent, failed, skipped }` |
| `/api/dashboard/messages/campaign/preview` | POST | `app/api/dashboard/messages/campaign/preview/route.ts` | 403 if `!massMessagingEnabled` | `{ recipientCount, sampleMessage }` |
