| `/api/campaigns/upload-image` | POST | `app/api/campaigns/upload-image/route.ts` | auth only (`requireDashboardBusiness`), **no massMessaging check** | `{ url, filename }` |

---

### Email campaign create/send — `POST /api/dashboard/emails`

**Request body:**
```ts
{
  senderName?: string                 // trim()'d; defaults to 'Align and Acquire'
  subject: string                     // required; 400 'Subject is required' if empty
  body: string                        // trim()'d; defaults to '<p>No content.</p>'
  bodyIsHtml?: boolean                // controls send-as-is vs wrap; see below
  images?: { url: string; filename: string; order: number }[]
  recipientSelection: RecipientSelection   // required; 400 if .type missing
}
type RecipientSelection =
  | { type: 'all' }
  | { type: 'tags'; tagIds: string[] }
  | { type: 'status'; statuses: string[] }
  | { type: 'manual'; contactIds: string[] }
```

**Recipient resolution** (`route.ts:82-125`) — every branch filters `email: { not: null }`, scoped to `businessId`:
- `all` → all contacts with non-null email.
- `tags` → contacts whose `contactTags.some.tagId ∈ tagIds` (only if `tagIds.length > 0`).
- `status` → contacts whose `status ∈ statuses` (only if `statuses.length > 0`).
- `manual` → contacts whose `id ∈ contactIds` AND `businessId` matches (only if `contactIds.length > 0`).
- A second `findMany` re-confirms `email: { not: null }` over the resolved IDs; `recipientCount = contactsWithEmail.length`.

**Side effects, in order:**
1. `images` sorted ascending by `order` (`sortedImages`).
2. `EmailCampaign.create` with `status: 'sending'`, `images: sortedImages.length ? sortedImages : undefined`, `senderName`, `subject`, `body: htmlBody`, `recipientCount`.
3. For each resolved contact: `EmailRecipient.create({ campaignId, contactId, email, status: 'pending' })` (awaited one-by-one in a loop).
4. **Build `fullHtml`:**
   - If `body.bodyIsHtml === true` → `fullHtml = htmlBody` (sent as-is, no image injection).
   - Else → `renderedBody = plainTextToEmailHtml(plainSource)` (from `lib/email-format.ts`), prepend `imagesHtml` (centered `<img>` tags, `max-width:600px`, built from `sortedImages`). `fullHtml = imagesHtml + renderedBody`.
5. **Resend send loop** (`route.ts:174-193`): `new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY)`; `from = \`${campaign.senderName} <notifications@alignandacquire.com>\``. Iterates recipients in slices of `BATCH_SIZE = 50`, but inside each slice sends **one email at a time** via `resend.emails.send({ from, to, subject, html: fullHtml })`, incrementing `sent`. Errors are caught **per-batch** (`try/catch` around the inner loop) and only `console.error`'d — a throw skips the rest of that batch.
6. **Status finalization** (`route.ts:195-202`): `EmailRecipient.updateMany({ campaignId }, { status: 'sent', sentAt: now })` for ALL recipients (regardless of individual Resend success), then `EmailCampaign.update({ status: 'sent', sentAt: now })`.
7. Returns `{ campaign: {...status:'sent'} }`.

> **Gotcha:** The entire send is synchronous and awaited; the client never observes `status:'sending'`. EmailRecipient is only ever written `pending → sent` — `delivered`/`bounced`/`failed` are never written despite the schema. Per-recipient failures (Resend throw) are silently swallowed and still marked `'sent'`.

**Status transitions (email):** `'sending'` (at create) → `'sent'` (after loop). `'draft'`/`'failed'` exist in schema but are never written by this route.

---

### Email campaign list/get

- `GET /api/dashboard/emails` — `findMany` by `businessId`, `orderBy createdAt desc`, returns minimal projection (`id, subject, status, recipientCount, sentAt, createdAt`). `EmailsClient` then client-side filters `status === 'sent'` and sorts by `sentAt || createdAt` desc.
- `GET /api/dashboard/emails/[id]` — `findFirst` scoped `{ id, businessId }`, returns full campaign incl. `senderName, body`. 404 `{ error: 'Campaign not found' }` if not found. Sole consumer is the compose "Reuse as Template" flow.

---

### SMS campaign send — `POST /api/dashboard/messages/campaign`

**Request body:**
```ts
{
  body: string                        // required; 400 'body is required' if empty
  recipientFilter: RecipientFilter    // required; 400 if .type missing
  mergeFields?: boolean               // default true
}
type RecipientFilter = { type:'all' } | { type:'tags'; tagIds } | { type:'status'; statuses } | { type:'manual'; contactIds }
```

Also 400s if `!business.telnyxPhoneNumber`.

**Contact resolution** (`route.ts:70-95`): base `where = { businessId, phoneNumber: { not: '' } }`; adds `contactTags.some.tagId ∈ tagIds` / `status ∈ statuses` / `id ∈ contactIds` only when the respective array is non-empty. `type:'all'` (or any filter with an empty array) → all contacts with a non-empty phone.

**Merge fields** (`applyMergeFields`): replaces `{{name}}` (→ `contact.name?.trim() || ''`) and `{{business_name}}` (→ `business.name`), both via case-insensitive whitespace-tolerant regex (`/\{\{\s*name\s*\}\}/gi`). Skipped entirely when `mergeFields === false`.

**Batched send** (`BATCH_SIZE = 10`, `BATCH_DELAY_MS = 2000`):
- Outer loop slices contacts into 10s; each batch runs `Promise.all` over the 10.
- Per contact: `to = normalizeToE164(toRaw) || toRaw`; `text = mergeFields===false ? template : applyMergeFields(...)`.
- `telnyxClient.messages.send({ from: business.telnyxPhoneNumber, to, text })`; reads `data.id` and `data.to[0].status ?? 'sent'`.
- Finds-or-creates a `Conversation` by `{ businessId, callerPhone: to }` (`orderBy lastMessageAt desc`); if absent, creates one with `status:'active'`, **`manualMode: true`**.
- Creates an outbound `Message` (`direction:'outbound'`, `content:text`, `telnyxSid`, `telnyxStatus`).
- Updates conversation `lastMessageAt` + `manualMode:true`.
- Fire-and-forget `findOrCreateContact({ businessId, phoneNumber: to, source: 'sms_conversation' }).catch(()=>{})`.
- Increments `sent` on success, `failed` on Telnyx throw, `skipped` when `phoneNumber` is falsy.
- After each batch (except the last), `await sleep(2000)`.

> **Critical gotcha (not in CLAUDE.md):** SMS campaigns bypass ALL missed-call SMS guardrails — no `checkCooldown`, no `recordMessageSent`, no `BlockedNumber` lookup, no `isExistingContact`. Every matched contact is messaged. Campaign sends also flip the per-contact conversation to `manualMode:true`, disabling the inbound AI for that thread.

---

### SMS campaign preview — `POST /api/dashboard/messages/campaign/preview`

Same body shape and same `where`-building logic. Fetches up to `take: 50` contacts, computes `recipientCount = db.contact.count({ where })`, and renders `sampleMessage` from `contacts[0]` (merge-applied unless `mergeFields===false`, else raw template). Returns `{ recipientCount, sampleMessage }`. No DB writes, no Telnyx calls.

---

### Image upload — `POST /api/campaigns/upload-image`

- Auth via `requireDashboardBusiness()` only (no massMessaging gate).
- `multipart/form-data`: `file` (required), `campaignId?` (optional).
- `ALLOWED_TYPES = ['image/png','image/jpeg','image/jpg','image/gif','image/webp']` → 400 on mismatch.
- `MAX_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024` (5 MB) → 400 if exceeded.
- Path: `campaign-images/${campaignId}/${sanitizedFilename}` or `campaign-images/draft/${sanitizedFilename}`. `sanitizedFilename = file.name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g,'_')`.
- `put(path, file, { access: 'public', contentType: file.type })`.
- Returns `{ url: blob.url, filename: file.name }` — **note `filename` is the original, unsanitized name**, while the blob key uses the sanitized name.
- 500 `{ error: 'Failed to upload image' }` on `put` throw.

---

### Outreach UI

**`outreach/page.tsx`** (server): Clerk `auth()` → user → `getBusinessForDashboard()` → redirect if no business. Wraps `<OutreachClient />` in:
```tsx
<FeatureGate mode="locked" enabled={business.massMessagingEnabled}
  feature="Mass Outreach"
  valueProp="Send targeted email and SMS campaigns to your contact list"
  businessName={business.name}>
```

**`FeatureGate.tsx`** (actual signature — **differs from CLAUDE.md**):
```ts
type LockedProps     = { mode:'locked';      feature:string; valueProp:string;        businessName:string }
type NeedsSetupProps = { mode:'needs-setup'; feature:string; setupDescription:string; setupLabel:string; setupHref:string }
type FeatureGateProps = (LockedProps | NeedsSetupProps) & { enabled: boolean; children: React.ReactNode }
```
Renders children directly when `enabled===true`; otherwise blurs (`blur-sm opacity-40 pointer-events-none`) and overlays a dark card. Locked → `mailto:jacob@alignandacquire.com?subject=Unlock {feature} for {businessName}` (encodeURIComponent), blue "Contact us to unlock" CTA. Needs-setup → green CTA `<a href={setupHref}>{setupLabel}</a>`.

**`OutreachClient.tsx`**: `useState<Channel>('email')` (default email). Channel switcher tabs (Mail / MessageCircle icons). Renders `<EmailsClient hideHeader />` or `<MessagesClient hideHeader />`. `hideHeader` suppresses each child's `<h1>`.

**`EmailsClient.tsx`**: fetches `GET /api/dashboard/emails`, client-filters `status==='sent'`, sorts by `sentAt||createdAt` desc. Table: Subject / Sent / Recipients / Actions. Each row → `<Link href={/dashboard/emails/new?templateId={c.id}}>` "Reuse as Template". "New Campaign" button → `/dashboard/emails/new` (hidden when `hideHeader`).

**`EmailComposeClient.tsx`** (`/dashboard/emails/new`):
- Reads `?templateId` via `useSearchParams`; on mount fetches `GET /api/dashboard/emails/[id]` and pre-fills **only `subject` + `body`** (senderName, images, recipients intentionally reset). Header copy switches to "Starting from a previous campaign. Recipients reset — pick them below."
- Body editor has `html`/`visual` modes; visual mode renders into a sandboxed iframe via `writeToIframe`. Preview uses `renderBodyForPreview` = `bodyContainsHtml(t) ? t : plainTextToEmailHtml(t)`.
- On send: computes `isHtml = bodyContainsHtml(trimmedBody)`, POSTs `{ senderName, subject, body: finalBody, bodyIsHtml: isHtml, images, recipientSelection }` to `/api/dashboard/emails`, then `window.location.href = '/dashboard/emails'`.
- Image upload posts each file to `/api/campaigns/upload-image` (client-side 5MB guard, same type whitelist); appended to `images[]` with sequential `order`. "Copy Image URL" lets the user paste URLs into HTML templates.
- Status options for the `status` recipient type: `new, contacted, quoted, booked, completed, lost`.

**`MessagesClient.tsx`** (manual SMS thread hub + SMS campaign launcher):
- Two-panel list/thread with the `mobileChatOpen` pattern. Loads `GET /api/dashboard/messages`, thread via `GET /api/dashboard/messages/[id]`.
- **Manual send** posts `{ to, body, conversationId }` to `/api/dashboard/messages/send` (existing thread) or `{ to, body }` (new conversation — `to` from selected contact or typed phone). On success appends `data.message` and reloads list.
- **`/api/dashboard/messages/send`** actual body is `{ to, body, conversationId? }` (NOT `{ conversationId, text }`). It resolves/creates a `Conversation` by `callerPhone`, sets **`manualMode: true`**, sends via Telnyx, writes outbound `Message`, fire-and-forget `findOrCreateContact(source:'sms_conversation')`. Returns `{ conversationId, message: {id, body, direction, createdAt, status}, contactLabel: null }`.
- **`manualMode` toggle:** every manual/campaign send path sets `manualMode:true` on the conversation (which suppresses inbound AI replies in the SMS webhook). There is no explicit toggle UI in this file — `manualMode` is set as a side effect of manual/campaign sending.
- **SMS campaign modal** (launched via "Send Campaign"): textarea with `{{name}}`/`{{business_name}}` hint, 160-char counter (soft, turns red over limit but does not block), `Enable merge fields` checkbox. Recipient filter buttons (`all`/`tags`/`status`/`manual`) only set the filter **type** — the modal explicitly notes "Advanced filters ... are configured via the API selection for now," so the UI cannot pick specific tag/status/contact IDs; in practice it sends `type:'all'` (or empty-array filters that resolve to all contacts). "Preview" → `POST .../campaign/preview`; "Send Campaign" → `POST .../campaign`, then renders `{ sent, failed, skipped }`. Footer states "send via Telnyx in small batches of 10 every 2 seconds."

### Shared helper — `lib/email-format.ts`
```ts
bodyContainsHtml(text): boolean          // /<[a-z][\s\S]*?>/i — any tag-like token
plainTextToEmailHtml(text): string       // escapeHtml() then wrap in <div style="white-space: pre-wrap; font-family: Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.5; color: #111;">
```
`escapeHtml` escapes `& < > " '`. Used by both the compose client (preview parity) and the email send route (`bodyIsHtml===false` path).

---

## 11. Frontend Dashboard Shell & Pages

Ground-truth reference for the authenticated dashboard shell, navigation construction, feature gating, the Overview page, the new combined Leads area, the Conversations viewer, Analytics, and the supporting list pages. Reflects the actual code (not CLAUDE.md prose).

### Dashboard layout & nav construction — `app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx`

Async server component. Flow:
1. `auth()` → `userId`; redirect `/sign-in` if absent.
2. `db.user.findUnique({ where: { clerkId: userId }, include: { business: true } })`.
3. `getBusinessForDashboard(userId, user?.business ?? null)` → `{ business, isAdminViewAs }`. **Note:** this is now destructured for `isAdminViewAs` too (CLAUDE.md only mentions `{ business }`).
4. If `!business` → redirect `/onboarding`.
5. `getNavigation(business)` builds the nav array; `userLabel = user?.firstName || user?.email || 'Admin'`.
6. Renders `<DashboardShellClient navigation businessName isAdminViewAs userLabel>{children}</DashboardShellClient>`.

`getNavigation(business)` calls `getBusinessFeatures(business)` then assembles `{ name, href, icon }[]` where `icon` is a string key resolved client-side via `ICON_MAP`. **Exact construction order and gating:**

| Order | Item | href | icon string | Condition |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Overview | `/dashboard` | `LayoutDashboard` | always |
| 2 | Leads | `/dashboard/leads` | `Mailbox` | always |
| 3 | Conversations | `/dashboard/conversations` | `MessagesSquare` | `features.hasMissedCallAi` |
| 4 | Outreach | `/dashboard/outreach` | `Send` | always |
| 5 | Analytics | `/dashboard/analytics` | `BarChart3` | always |
| 6 | Scheduled Quotes | `/dashboard/appointments` | `Calendar` | `features.hasMissedCallAi` |
| 7 | Voicemails *(label varies)* | `/dashboard/voicemails` | `Mail` | see below |
| 8 | Screened Calls | `/dashboard/blocked-calls` | `PhoneOff` | `features.hasAnyScreening` |
| 9 | Contacts | `/dashboard/contacts` | `Users` | always |
| 10 | Jobs | `/dashboard/jobs` | `Briefcase` | always |
| 11 | Google Ads *(label varies)* | `/dashboard/ads` | `Megaphone` | `business.googleAdsEnabled` |
| 12 | Settings | `/dashboard/settings` | `Settings` | always |

**Voicemails nav label logic** (`layout.tsx:39-46`):
- `!features.hasMissedCallAi` → label `"Voicemails"`.
- else if `business.knownContactVoicemailEnabled` → label `"Voicemails from Contacts"` (AI clients who route known contacts to voicemail).
- else → no Voicemails nav item.

**Google Ads label:** `business.googleAdsTabLabel || 'Google Ads'`.

`getNavigation`'s param type accepts a new field `knownContactVoicemailEnabled` (a Business column not documented in CLAUDE.md's schema section). The `import { DashboardShellClient }` is unusually placed mid-file at line 67 (after `getNavigation`).

### `app/(dashboard)/DashboardShellClient.tsx`

`'use client'`. Props: `{ children, navigation: NavItem[], businessName, isAdminViewAs?, userLabel? }`. `NavItem = { name; href; icon: string }`.

- `ICON_MAP: Record<string, ComponentType>` maps icon strings → lucide-react components. Keys present: `LayoutDashboard, MessageSquare, MessageCircle, MessagesSquare, Calendar, Settings, PhoneOff, Mail, Users, Briefcase, Mailbox, BarChart3, Megaphone, Send, Globe`. Fallback in render: `ICON_MAP[item.icon] ?? LayoutDashboard`.
- Single state: `const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(false)`.
- `sidebarContent` JSX shared between desktop and mobile drawer: brand link to `/`, Business name block, `<nav>` mapping `navigation`, and a bottom-pinned Clerk `<UserButton afterSignOutUrl="/" />` + `userLabel`. Nav links use `min-h-[44px]` touch targets and close the drawer on click.
- **Desktop sidebar:** `<aside class="fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 w-64 ... hidden md:block">`.
- **Mobile drawer:** rendered only when `sidebarOpen` — a `bg-black/40` overlay (`md:hidden`, click closes) + slide-in `<aside w-64 max-w-[85vw] ... md:hidden animate-in slide-in-from-left>` with a close (X) button.
- **Mobile top bar:** `<header md:hidden fixed top-0>` with hamburger (`Menu`, `setSidebarOpen(true)`), truncated business name, and a `UserButton`.
- **Main:** `<main class="md:pl-64 pt-14 md:pt-16">`. When `isAdminViewAs`, renders an amber banner: `Viewing as client: {businessName}` + an `Exit view` link to `/api/admin/view-as?exit=1`. Content wrapped in `<div class="p-4 md:p-6 lg:p-8">`.

**Drift:** CLAUDE.md says the shell exposes a "View as Client" admin *toggle*. The actual shell has no view-as toggle to enter view-as; it only renders an **exit** banner when already in view-as mode (entered elsewhere via `/api/admin/view-as`). The "View as Client" entry mechanism is not in this component.

### `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx` — Overview server logic

Server component. After standard auth/business resolution:

```ts
const bizFeatures = getBusinessFeatures(business)
const features = {
  ...bizFeatures,
  googleAds: business.googleAdsEnabled ?? false,
  knownContactVoicemailEnabled: business.knownContactVoicemailEnabled ?? false,
}
const showVoicemails = !features.hasMissedCallAi || features.knownContactVoicemailEnabled
```

The `features` object passed to `OverviewClient` is `BusinessFeatures` **plus** `googleAds` and `knownContactVoicemailEnabled` (two extras).

Server-side data fetched and passed as props (no `SpamOnlyDashboard` branch — see below):

1. **`initialVoicemails`** (only fetched when `showVoicemails`): latest 5 `Conversation` where `recordingUrl != null`, `orderBy createdAt desc`, `take 5`. Contact names resolved by a single `db.contact.findMany` keyed on normalized phone (`namesForPhones()` → `Map<normalizedPhone, name>`, lookup via `normalizePhoneNumber`). Shape per row: `{ conversationId, callerPhone, contactName|null, recordingUrl, voicemailTranscription, createdAt: ISO }`.
2. **`callLog`** (always, latest ~15, NOT period-scoped): merges two queries via `Promise.all`:
   - `db.screenedCall.findMany` (take 15) → `{ kind:'call', phone, result, ... }` (id prefixed `call_`).
   - `db.activity.findMany({ type:'missed_call' }, include contact, take 15)` → `{ kind:'lead', phone: contact.phoneNumber, name: contact.name, description, ... }` (id prefixed `lead_`).
   - Merged, sorted newest-first, `.slice(0, 15)`. `CallLogItem = { id; kind:'call'|'lead'; phone|null; name|null; result|null; description|null; createdAt }`.

Always renders `<OverviewClient features initialVoicemails callLog />`. **There is no SpamOnlyDashboard branch in the current page** — `OverviewClient` handles all client types via feature flags.

### `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/SpamOnlyDashboard.tsx` — DEAD CODE

`'use client'` component `SpamOnlyDashboard()` exists but is **never imported or rendered anywhere** (confirmed via grep: only its own definition matches). It is a self-contained light-theme dashboard that fetches `/api/dashboard/screened-calls?days=1`, `?days=30`, and `/api/dashboard/voicemails`, computes today's blocked/passed/voicemail counts, and shows a combined recent-activity list (calls + voicemails, top 10). Its functionality has been superseded by `OverviewClient`'s feature-gated sections. Treat as removable.

### `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/OverviewClient.tsx`

`'use client'`. Props: `{ features: Features, initialVoicemails?: VoicemailRow[], callLog?: CallLogItem[] }`.

`Features` type (mirrors `BusinessFeatures` + two extras):
```ts
type Features = {
  hasSpamFilter; hasIvrScreener; hasAnyScreening; hasMissedCallAi;
  hasForwarding; hasCalendar; showScreeningCards; showAiCards;
  totalCallsMode: 'screened' | 'calls';
  googleAds: boolean;
  knownContactVoicemailEnabled: boolean;
}
```

State: `period` (`useState<Period>('month')`), `loading`, `error`, `analytics`, `upcomingAppointments`, `adsTotals`, `reloadKey`. `showVoicemails = !features.hasMissedCallAi || features.knownContactVoicemailEnabled` (recomputed client-side).

Uses shared UI primitives from `@/app/components/ui` (`PageHeader, PeriodSelector, MetricCard, Card/CardHeader/CardBody/CardTitle/CardSubtitle, Badge, StatusBadge, EmptyState, ErrorState, LoadingState, Skeleton, periodLabel, type Period`).

**Data fetching** (`useEffect` keyed on `[period, reloadKey, features.hasCalendar, features.googleAds]`):
- Always: `GET /api/dashboard/analytics?period=${period}`.
- If `features.hasCalendar`: `GET /api/appointments`, filtered to `scheduledAt >= now && status === 'confirmed'`, sliced to 5 → `upcomingAppointments`.
- If `features.googleAds`: `GET /api/dashboard/google-ads?startDate&endDate` (last 30 days) → `adsTotals = d.totals`.

**`AnalyticsResponse` type note:** has both `leadsCapured` (typo, required) and `leadsCaptured?` (optional) — the typo'd key is the canonical one the API returns and is mirrored across Overview/Analytics clients.

**Lead-source reconciliation:** `leadSources = { ...analytics.leadSources, website_form: analytics.websiteLeads }` so the card and chart agree. `missedCallLeads = leadSources?.missed_call ?? 0`.

**Feature-gated metric cards** (`cards` array with `show` flags, then `.filter(c => c.show)`):

| Card | `show` condition | href |
|---|---|---|
| Total Calls | `true` (always) | `/dashboard/blocked-calls` |
| Calls Blocked | `features.hasAnyScreening` | `/dashboard/blocked-calls` |
| Calls Passed | `features.hasAnyScreening` | `/dashboard/blocked-calls` |
| Voicemails *(label = `features.hasMissedCallAi ? 'Voicemails from Contacts' : 'Voicemails'`)* | `showVoicemails` | `/dashboard/voicemails` |
| Missed Call Leads | `features.hasMissedCallAi` | `/dashboard/conversations` |
| Website Leads | `features.hasMissedCallAi` | `/dashboard/leads` |

Total Calls subLabel: `${totalCallsMode === 'screened' ? 'Screened' : 'Inbound'} calls · ${pLabel}`. `formatDelta()` produces the trend chip from `previousTotalCalls`/`previousLeadsCaptured`.

**Other gated sections:**
- Google Ads summary `<Card>` (4 `AdStat` tiles: Ad Spend, Clicks, CTR, Cost/Conv) — only when `features.googleAds`.
- Upcoming Quote Visits — only when `features.hasCalendar && !loading && upcomingAppointments.length > 0`.
- Lead Sources bar chart + Recent Activity (`CallLogRow` list from server `callLog`) — always rendered side-by-side (`lg:grid-cols-2`).
- Recent voicemails with inline `<audio>` players — only when `showVoicemails && initialVoicemails.length > 0` (uses server-fetched data, no client fetch).

Module-scope sub-components: `AdStat`, `CallLogRow` (defined at module scope, not inside the parent — per the React convention).

### `app/components/FeatureGate.tsx`

`'use client'` is **absent** — this is a pure server component (matches CLAUDE.md). Discriminated-union props:

```ts
type LockedProps = { mode:'locked'; feature:string; valueProp:string; businessName:string }
type NeedsSetupProps = { mode:'needs-setup'; feature:string; setupDescription:string; setupLabel:string; setupHref:string }
type FeatureGateProps = (LockedProps | NeedsSetupProps) & { enabled:boolean; children:ReactNode }
```

**Drift vs CLAUDE.md:** the prop names differ from documented (`mode, feature, businessName?, setupHref?, setupLabel?`). Actual: locked mode requires `valueProp` (not optional `businessName`); needs-setup mode requires `setupDescription` (CLAUDE.md doesn't list it). Both modes are driven by a single `enabled` boolean — when `enabled` is true the gate renders `{children}` directly; when false it renders blurred children (`blur-sm opacity-40 pointer-events-none`) under a dark overlay card.
- `locked`: shows `<Lock>` icon, `valueProp`, and a `mailto:jacob@alignandacquire.com` CTA with subject `Unlock ${feature} for ${businessName}` (URI-encoded). Button "Contact us to unlock" (blue).
- `needs-setup`: shows `<Wrench>` icon, `setupDescription`, and an `<a href={setupHref}>` green button labeled `setupLabel`.

### `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/conversations/page.tsx`

Server component. Reads `searchParams?.selected` (optional thread to pre-select when arriving from the Leads list). Wraps content in a header (`<h1>Conversations</h1>`, supports `dark:text-white`) and a `FeatureGate mode="locked" enabled={business.missedCallAiEnabled !== false} feature="AI Conversations" valueProp=... businessName=...`. Renders `<ConversationsClient selectConversationId={selected} />`.

### `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/conversations/ConversationsClient.tsx`

`'use client'`. Props: `{ embedded?: boolean = false, selectConversationId?: string|null = null }`.

**Buckets/tabs:** uses `getConversationBucket`, `BUCKET_COLORS` from `@/lib/conversation-buckets`. `TabKey = 'all' | ConversationBucket`. `TABS = ['all','cold','active','stalled','closed']`. Client-facing labels via local `TAB_LABELS`: `all→All, cold→No Reply, active→In Progress, stalled→Went Quiet, closed→Closed`. `BucketedConversation = Conversation & { bucket }`.

**State:** `conversations`, `loading`, `error`, `selectedConvo`, `activeTab` (`'all'`), `mobileChatOpen` (`false`).

**Effects:**
- On mount: `GET /api/dashboard/conversations` → `setConversations(data.conversations || [])`.
- When `selectConversationId` provided and data loaded: find that conversation, `setSelectedConvo`, force `activeTab='all'`, `setMobileChatOpen(true)` (deep-link from Leads list).

**Derived memos:** `bucketed` (maps each conv → `{ ...c, bucket }`); `tabCounts` (per-bucket counts + `all`); `visible` (filter by active bucket unless `all`, sorted by `lastMessageAt` desc).

**Layout:**
- Container: `embedded` → bordered rounded card; otherwise `min-h-screen bg-gray-950 text-white -m-6 md:-m-8` (dark theme bleeding edge-to-edge past the padded shell).
- Tab bar: horizontally scrollable with hidden scrollbar (`overflow-x-auto [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden [-ms-overflow-style:none] [scrollbar-width:none]`), each tab `whitespace-nowrap flex-shrink-0` with a count pill. Switching tab resets `selectedConvo` to null and `mobileChatOpen` to false.
- **Mobile (`md:hidden`):** list shown when `!(mobileChatOpen && selectedConvo)`; thread (full-screen, no height cap) shown when `mobileChatOpen && selectedConvo`, with a header containing an `ArrowLeft` back button (`min-h-[44px] min-w-[44px]`), contact name, and bucket badge.
- **Desktop (`hidden md:block`):** `grid-cols-5` split — list `col-span-2`, thread `col-span-3` inside a `sticky top-6` card; `ThreadBody scrollable` caps message list at `max-h-[560px] overflow-y-auto`.

**Module-scope sub-components** (per React convention): `ConvoCard` (props `convo, isSelected, onSelect, activeTab`), `ThreadBody` (props `convo, scrollable?`), `EmptyState` (props `activeTab`). `ThreadBody` renders the header (phone, intent, serviceRequested, summary, captured email/address/timeframe with emoji) and the message bubbles (outbound = right/blue, inbound = left/gray).

### Combined Leads area (NEW — absent from CLAUDE.md)

This is a new top-level dashboard section not documented in CLAUDE.md. Three files under `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/leads/`.

**`leads/page.tsx`** — server component. Standard auth/business resolution. Header `<h1>Leads</h1>` + subtitle "Missed call and website leads in one place". `FeatureGate mode="locked" enabled={business.missedCallAiEnabled !== false} feature="Leads" valueProp="See every missed-call and website lead your AI captures, in one place"`. Renders `<LeadsClient />`. **This is the canonical "Leads" nav destination.**

**`leads/LeadsClient.tsx`** — `'use client'`. Thin wrapper: `useRouter()`, defines `openConversation(id)` → `router.push('/dashboard/conversations?selected=' + encodeURIComponent(id))`, renders `<CombinedLeadsList onOpenConversation={openConversation} />`. This is how a closed missed-call lead row deep-links into the Conversations thread viewer (consumed by `ConversationsClient`'s `selectConversationId` effect).

**`leads/CombinedLeadsList.tsx`** — `'use client'`. Props: `{ onOpenConversation: (conversationId: string) => void }`.

Merges two data sources into one unified list:
- `GET /api/dashboard/conversations` → `conversations` (only those whose `getConversationBucket()` is `'closed'`, i.e. captured/converted leads with email/address/timeframe or a linked appointment; cold/active/stalled threads are intentionally excluded and stay in the Conversations tab).
- `GET /api/dashboard/website-leads` → `websiteLeads` (`wData.leads`).

`UnifiedLead = { kind:'missed_call'|'website'; key; conversationId?; name; summary; statusLabel; statusClass; email; phone; address; message; sortTs; dateStr }`. Missed-call rows derive `statusLabel` from `BUCKET_LABELS[bucket]` and `statusClass` from a local `BUCKET_LIGHT` (light-theme palette). Website rows map status via local `WEBSITE_STATUS` map (`new/contacted/converted/closed`). Both merged and sorted by `sortTs` desc.

Source filter tabs: `all | missed_call | website` (counts shown). Each row shows a source badge (`SOURCE_BADGE`: Missed Call=orange, Website=blue) + status badge + name/phone/email/address + relative date + `ChevronRight`.

**Row click behavior** (`handleRowClick`): missed-call rows with a `conversationId` call `onOpenConversation(id)` (navigate to Conversations); website rows open an in-page modal (`detail` state) showing name/phone/email/address/message. The modal is bottom-sheet on mobile (`items-end md:items-center`, `rounded-t-2xl md:rounded-xl`).

### `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/analytics/AnalyticsClient.tsx`

`'use client'`. No props — self-fetching. State: `period` (`'month'`), `loading`, `error`, `data`. `useEffect` keyed on `[period]` fetches `GET /api/dashboard/analytics?period=${period}`.

`AnalyticsResponse` includes optional `features?: AnalyticsFeatures` and `totalCallsMode?`. `effectiveLeadsCaptured = data.leadsCaptured ?? data.leadsCapured` (handles both the typo and corrected key).

**Period picker:** Today / This Week / This Month / All Time (`'today'|'week'|'month'|'all'`).

**Feature-gated metric cards** (each gated by `(loading || data?.features?.…)` so cards render during initial load, then disappear if the flag is false):

| Card | Gate |
|---|---|
| Total Calls (desc "Screened calls" vs "Inbound calls" per `totalCallsMode`) | always |
| Calls Blocked (Spam) | `loading \|\| data?.features?.hasAnyScreening` |
| Calls Passed (Real) | `loading \|\| data?.features?.hasAnyScreening` |
| Leads Captured | `loading \|\| data?.features?.hasMissedCallAi` |
| Website Leads (has `emptyCta` "Get a website to capture more leads" when 0) | `loading \|\| data?.features?.hasMissedCallAi` |
| Messages Sent | `loading \|\| data?.features?.hasMissedCallAi` |

Always-shown lower sections: Lead Sources bar chart (keys `missed_call, website_form, referral, google_ad, imports, manual`) and Recent Activity timeline (driven by `data.recentActivity`, with `ACTIVITY_TYPE_LABELS`/`ACTIVITY_ICONS` maps including many import-source variants). Local `MetricCard` sub-component at module scope.

### Supporting list pages

**`appointments/page.tsx`** — dual FeatureGate computed server-side: `isLocked = !calendarEnabled` (→ `mode="locked"` feature "Scheduled Quotes"), `needsSetup = calendarEnabled && !googleCalendarConnected` (→ `mode="needs-setup"`, setupHref `/api/auth/google?businessId=${business.id}`), else render `<AppointmentsClient />` plainly. **`AppointmentsClient.tsx`** fetches `GET /api/appointments`, splits upcoming (`scheduledAt >= now && status==='confirmed'`) vs past/other, MiniStat tiles (Total/Upcoming/Completed/Cancelled), per-card SMS-vs-Website source badge, status relabeling (confirmed→"Quote Scheduled", completed→"Quote Completed"). Actions: `CancelBookingButton` (upcoming), per-card delete `DELETE /api/bookings/${id}` (two-click confirm), and `POST /api/bookings/delete-past` for "Delete All Past". **Inconsistency:** page-level header in the loading state says "Scheduled Quotes" but the loaded view's `<h1>` says "Appointments".

**`voicemails/page.tsx`** — server gate via redirect (no FeatureGate): `canSeeVoicemails = !features.hasMissedCallAi || (business.knownContactVoicemailEnabled ?? false)`; if false → `redirect('/dashboard')`. **`VoicemailsClient.tsx`** fetches `GET /api/dashboard/voicemails`, inline `<audio>` players, transcription, per-row delete `DELETE /api/dashboard/voicemails/${conversationId}` with `window.confirm` + toast.

**`blocked-calls/page.tsx`** — header "Screened Calls" + `FeatureGate mode="locked" enabled={features.hasAnyScreening} feature="Call Screening"`. **`BlockedCallsClient.tsx`** fetches `GET /api/dashboard/screened-calls?days=30`, filter tabs all/blocked/passed, table of caller phone / datetime / status.

**`website-leads/page.tsx`** — now a pure redirect: `redirect('/dashboard/leads?tab=website')` (old URL preserved). **`WebsiteLeadsClient.tsx`** still exists and is used by the standalone client UI: fetches `GET /api/dashboard/website-leads`, status pill config (new/contacted/converted/closed), inline status-change buttons `PATCH /api/dashboard/website-leads` `{ leadId, status }`.

### Contacts / Jobs client components (listed, not deep-read)

- `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/contacts/ContactsClient.tsx`, `contacts/[id]/` (detail), `contacts/import/` (importer), `contacts/page.tsx`.
- `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/jobs/JobsClient.tsx`, `jobs/page.tsx`.

### React conventions confirmed in code

- Helper components defined at **module scope** (not nested in parents): `AdStat`/`CallLogRow` (OverviewClient), `ConvoCard`/`ThreadBody`/`EmptyState` (ConversationsClient), `MetricCard` (AnalyticsClient), `MiniStat`/`AppointmentCard` (AppointmentsClient).
- Mobile list/detail uses the `mobileChatOpen` single-boolean pattern with a desktop CSS-grid split-pane (`md:grid-cols-5`), and 44px minimum touch targets throughout.

---

## 12. Super-Admin Panel & Admin APIs

Super-admin surface at `/admin`, gated entirely by `userId === process.env.ADMIN_USER_ID`. There is **no middleware admin check** — gating is done per-route (the server page and every admin API route handler check it independently).

### Authorization model (per-route, not middleware)

| Surface | Check | On failure |
|---|---|---|
| `app/admin/page.tsx` (server) | `if (!userId \|\| userId !== process.env.ADMIN_USER_ID) redirect('/dashboard')` | `redirect('/dashboard')` |
| `/api/admin/businesses` GET | same compare | `403 { error: 'Unauthorized' }` |
| `/api/admin/businesses/[id]` PATCH | same | `403` |
| `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/contacts` GET/POST/DELETE | `unauthorized()` helper | `403` |
| `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/contacts/bulk` POST | same | `403` |
| `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/conversations` GET | same | `403` |
| `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/screened-calls` GET | same | `403` |
| `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/blocked-numbers` GET/POST/DELETE | `unauthorized()` helper | `403` |
| `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/voicemails` GET | same | `403` |
| `/api/admin/view-as` GET | same | `redirect('/dashboard')` (NOT 403) |

Note: admin routes return **403** for unauthorized, not 401 (dashboard routes use 401/404). `view-as` redirects rather than erroring. Several files capture `const ADMIN_USER_ID = process.env.ADMIN_USER_ID` at module scope; `app/admin/page.tsx` reads `process.env.ADMIN_USER_ID` inline.

### `app/admin/page.tsx` — enriched server fetch

Lean server shell. Auth-checks, then calls a local `fetchBusinesses()` (NOT the API route — but logically identical to `/api/admin/businesses` GET) and renders `<AdminClient initialBusinesses={businesses} />`. The page version `.toISOString()`s `createdAt`/`updatedAt` (the API route does not — see drift).

**Exact stat computation** (identical in `page.tsx:fetchBusinesses` and `/api/admin/businesses/route.ts`):

Four parallel queries via `Promise.all`:
1. `db.business.findMany({ orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' }, include: { _count: { select: { conversations: { where: { messages: { some: {} } } }, appointments: true, users: true, screenedCalls: true } } } })` — `_count.conversations` only counts conversations that have at least one message (this becomes `conversationsAllTime`).
2. `db.conversation.findMany` WHERE `createdAt >= lastMonthStart` AND `messages: { some: {} }`, selecting `id, businessId, createdAt, customerEmail, customerAddress, customerTimeframe, appointment: { id }`.
3. `db.conversation.groupBy({ by: ['businessId'], where: { messages: { some: {} }, OR: [customerEmail not null, customerAddress not null, customerTimeframe not null, appointment isNot null] }, _count: true })` → `leadsAllTime` per business.
4. `db.screenedCall.groupBy({ by: ['businessId'], where: { result: 'blocked', createdAt: { gte: thirtyDaysAgo } }, _count: true })` → `blockedCalls30d`.

Date boundaries:
```js
const now = new Date()
const thisMonthStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1)   // local-time month start
const lastMonthStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() - 1, 1)
const thirtyDaysAgo = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)
```

Per-business reduction over query #2 results:
- `conversationsThisMonth` = count where `createdAt >= thisMonthStart`
- `leadsThisMonth` = subset of this-month convos where `customerEmail || customerAddress || customerTimeframe || appointment` truthy
- `conversationsLastMonth` = count where `!isThisMonth && createdAt >= lastMonthStart`
- `conversationsAllTime` = `biz._count.conversations`
- `leadsAllTime` = from groupBy #3 (`_count`), default 0

A "lead" = a conversation (with ≥1 message) that captured email/address/timeframe OR has a linked appointment. Same OR predicate used both for `leadsThisMonth` (in-memory) and `leadsAllTime` (DB groupBy).

### `app/admin/AdminClient.tsx` — main client shell

State: `businesses` (seeded from `initialBusinesses`), `selectedBusiness`, `search`, `statusFilter` (`'all' | 'active' | 'trialing' | 'past_due' | 'canceled'`), `sortKey` (`'last_activity' | 'name' | 'mrr' | 'convos'`), `toasts`, `refreshing`.

- `addToast(message, type)` — pushes `{ id, message, type }`, auto-dismisses after 4000ms.
- `updateBusiness(updated)` — replaces in `businesses` array and syncs `selectedBusiness` if same id.
- `refreshBusinesses()` — `GET /api/admin/businesses`, replaces full list, re-syncs selected business.
- `filtered` (useMemo) — search matches `name`, `telnyxPhoneNumber`, or `businessType` (case-insensitive); status filter; sort. Sort: `name`→localeCompare, `mrr`→`monthlyFee` desc, `convos`→`conversationsThisMonth` desc, `last_activity` (default)→`updatedAt` desc.

Renders `<HeaderKPIs>`, controls bar, `<ClientTable>`, conditional `<ClientDetailPanel>`, and a fixed toast stack (`z-[100]`). `Toast` interface is exported here.

### `app/admin/ClientTable.tsx`

Mobile-first: `md:hidden divide-y` card list + `hidden md:table` desktop table. `STATUS_COLORS` / `STATUS_LABELS` maps for `active`/`trialing`/`past_due`/`canceled`; unknown status falls back to `canceled` color and raw status string as label.

`FeatureIcons` (module scope) renders 5 icons via `FeatureIcon` (blue when active, gray-700 when inactive):

| Icon (lucide) | Active condition | Tooltip variants |
|---|---|---|
| `Shield` | `callScreenerEnabled \|\| spamFilterEnabled` | "Call Screener + Spam Filter" / "Call Screener" / "Spam Filter" / "No screening" |
| `MessageCircle` | `missedCallAiEnabled` | "MissedCall AI on/off" |
| `Calendar` | `calendarEnabled && googleCalendarConnected` | "Calendar connected" / "Calendar enabled, not connected" / "Calendar off" |
| `Megaphone` | `googleAdsEnabled` | "Google Ads on/off" |
| `Globe` | `calendarEnabled` | "Online booking on/off" |

Desktop columns: Name (+phone or red "No number"), Status, MRR (`$X/mo`, dash if canceled or null fee), Features, **Convos** (this-month over all-time, links to `/admin/${biz.id}/conversations`), **Leads** (this-month over all-time, not a link), Actions. Actions: View-as link (`/api/admin/view-as?businessId=${biz.id}`, amber Eye icon), Conversations link (`/admin/${biz.id}/conversations`, MessageSquare), Edit/Detail button (calls `onSelect(biz)`, Settings icon). All action cells use `onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}` so they don't open the detail panel. Has `SkeletonRow`/`TableHead` and a `loading` prop and empty-state.

### `app/admin/ClientDetailPanel.tsx` — slide-out, 3 tabs

Width `w-full sm:w-[520px] lg:w-[600px]`, fixed right, `z-50`, with a `z-40` black/40 backdrop. Escape key closes (`useEffect` keydown). Resets to `'toggles'` tab when `business.id` changes (render-phase `prevId` comparison, not effect). Header shows name + status badge + "View as Client" amber link + X close. Tab bar: `toggles` / `settings` / `tools` (capitalized labels). Content area is `overflow-y-auto`.

### TogglesTab — `app/admin/ClientDetailPanel/TogglesTab.tsx`

**Optimistic patch pattern** — shared `patch(field, value, label)`:
```js
const original = { ...business }
onUpdateBusiness({ ...business, [field]: value })   // optimistic
setSaving(field)
const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/businesses/${business.id}`, { method:'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value }) })
const data = await res.json()
if (!res.ok) { onUpdateBusiness(original); onToast(data.error || `Failed to update ${label}`, 'error') }
else { onUpdateBusiness({ ...original, ...data.business, _count: original._count,
        conversationsThisMonth, conversationsLastMonth, leadsThisMonth (all from original) }) }
// catch → revert + error toast; finally → setSaving(null)
```
On success it **merges the DB response but explicitly preserves `_count` and the computed monthly stats from the original** (the PATCH response is a raw `business` row without those). Note: `conversationsAllTime`/`leadsAllTime` are NOT in the preserved set, so they can be lost from the in-memory object after a toggle (drift candidate / minor bug).

Toggle rows and their fields:

| Row | Icon | Field patched | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Call Forwarding | `Phone` | `forwardingNumber` (string\|null) | Not a plain toggle — toggle-on opens an inline `<input type=tel>` + Save/Cancel; toggle-off patches `null`. `saveForwarding()` patches `forwardingNum.trim() \|\| null`. |
| MissedCall AI | `MessageCircle` | `missedCallAiEnabled` | |
| Send known contacts to voicemail | `Voicemail` | `knownContactVoicemailEnabled` | **NEW field** not in CLAUDE.md schema/toggle docs |
| Call Screener (IVR) | `Shield` | `callScreenerEnabled` | Expandable child: inline edit of `callScreenerMessage` (textarea + Save/Cancel). `saveScreenerMessage()` patches `screenerMsg \|\| null`. Placeholder preview: `Thank you for calling ${business.name}. To be connected, please press 1.` |
| Spam Filter | `ShieldCheck` | `spamFilterEnabled` | |
| Online Booking | `Globe` | `calendarEnabled` | |
| SMS Auto-Booking | `MessageSquare` | `smsBookingEnabled` | **NEW toggle** not in CLAUDE.md |
| Google Calendar | `Calendar` | (read-only) | Status badge Connected/Not Connected + link `/api/auth/google?businessId=...` (Connect/Reconnect) |
| Google Ads Dashboard | `Megaphone` | `googleAdsEnabled` | |
| Notify by SMS | `MessageSquare` | `notifyBySms` | |
| Notify by Email | `Mail` | `notifyByEmail` | |
| Mass Outreach | `Mailbox` | `massMessagingEnabled` | |

### SettingsTab — `app/admin/ClientDetailPanel/SettingsTab.tsx`

Bulk-edit form (not per-field optimistic). `initForm(b)` seeds string-coerced values; `useEffect` resets form when `business.id` changes. JSON fields (`servicesOffered`, `businessHours`) are `JSON.parse`-validated client-side before save (toast error on invalid). Empty `businessHours` defaults to `DEFAULT_BUSINESS_HOURS`. Sends one PATCH with the full payload below; on success merges response + preserves `_count`/monthly stats (same pattern as TogglesTab), re-seeds form, "Saved successfully" toast.

Sections: Business Info (name, telnyxPhoneNumber, forwardingNumber, timezone select [6 US zones], businessType select from `BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS` in `@/lib/industry-defaults`), Billing (subscriptionStatus select, setupFee, monthlyFee), Notifications (ownerEmail, ownerPhone), AI Configuration (aiGreeting, aiInstructions, aiContext, missedCallVoiceMessage), Booking (slotDurationMinutes [15/30/45/60/90/120], bufferMinutes [0/15/30/45/60], bookingPageTitle, bookingPageServiceLabel, bookingPageConfirmation, maxMessagesPerConversation), Services & Hours (servicesOffered JSON, businessHours JSON + "Default" button), Google Ads (googleAdsCustomerId, googleAdsTabLabel), Admin Only (adminNotes, smsCooldownDays, cooldownBypassNumbers comma-joined). Numeric fields parsed with `parseFloat`/`parseInt`; `maxMessagesPerConversation` sent as `undefined` when blank (omitted), others as `null`.

### ToolsTab — `app/admin/ClientDetailPanel/ToolsTab.tsx`

Five tool sections:
- **Conversations** — link to `/admin/${business.id}/conversations`.
- **Screened Calls Log** — lazy `ScreenedCallsSummary` fetches `GET /api/admin/businesses/${id}/screened-calls?days=30` on demand; renders blocked/passed/blockRate.
- **Bulk Import Contacts** — `BulkImportSection`: client-side `parseContactFile()` (from `@/lib/import-contacts`, dynamic import) to preview count, then POSTs in batches of **100** to `/api/admin/businesses/${id}/contacts/bulk` with `{ contacts: [{ phoneNumber, name }] }`, progress bar by batch. Description warns imported contacts "skip automated SMS" (source IS NULL semantics).
- **Blocked Numbers** — `BlockedNumbersSection`: lazy GET `/blocked-numbers`; add via POST `{ phoneNumber, label }`; remove via `DELETE ...?id=<id>`.
- **Voicemails** — links to `/api/admin/view-as?businessId=${id}` ("Open Client Dashboard"), i.e. uses view-as rather than a dedicated admin voicemail UI.

### HeaderKPIs — `app/admin/HeaderKPIs.tsx`

3-card grid (`grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3`):
- **MRR (Active + Past Due)** — sum of `monthlyFee` for businesses with status `active` OR `past_due` (note: past_due included in MRR).
- **Clients** — counts by status (active/trialing/past_due/canceled) + total.
- **Conversations This Month** — sum of `conversationsThisMonth`; delta vs last month = `round((thisMonth - lastMonth)/lastMonth * 100)` (null if lastMonth is 0), green if ≥0 else red.

### AdminTools — `app/admin/AdminTools.tsx`

Dropdown ("Tools" button, click-outside close). Actions (`ActionKey = 'telnyx'|'ads'|'sheets'|'export'|'refresh'`):

| Item | Call |
|---|---|
| Refresh Table | `onRefresh()` (re-fetch businesses) |
| Sync Telnyx Usage | `POST /api/admin/usage/sync?dateRange=last_90_days` → toasts `mdrsProcessed`/`cdrsProcessed` |
| Sync Google Ads | `POST /api/admin/google-ads/sync` → toasts `synced` count |
| Sync Google Sheets | `POST /api/admin/usage/sheets-sync` |
| Export to Excel | `window.open('/api/admin/usage/export?preset=this_month', '_blank')` |

### `app/admin/components/CallScreenerCard.tsx` (NEW, undocumented in CLAUDE.md)

Self-contained card component for per-business call-screener display. **Not currently imported by any admin component** (the TogglesTab IVR row supersedes it for the live panel). Props: `businessId, businessName, callScreenerEnabled, callScreenerMessage?, onToggle(businessId, enabled), onUpdateMessage(businessId, message)`. Local state for enabled/message/edit/log toggling. On enable (or mount when enabled) fetches `GET /api/admin/businesses/${businessId}/screened-calls?days=30` and renders `stats` (blocked/passed/blockRate, last-30-days) + `recentCalls` log (slice 20), inline IVR message editor, and a disabled-state hint. Default message: `Thank you for calling ${businessName}. To be connected, please press 1.`

### `/admin/[businessId]/conversations` sub-route (NEW, undocumented)

`app/admin/[businessId]/conversations/page.tsx` — client component. Reads `businessId` from `useParams`, fetches `GET /api/admin/businesses/${businessId}/conversations`; on 403 `router.push('/dashboard')`. Buckets each conversation via `getConversationBucket()` (from `@/lib/conversation-buckets`). Tabs use **raw bucket names as labels** — `All / Cold / Active / Stalled / Closed` (NOT the client-facing `BUCKET_LABELS` used in the customer dashboard; this admin view deliberately exposes internal names). Tab badge counts per bucket. Desktop split-pane (`md:grid-cols-5`, list `col-span-2`, thread `col-span-3 sticky top-6`, messages `max-h-[560px] overflow-y-auto`). Thread shows caller, intent, serviceRequested, summary, captured lead fields (email/address/timeframe), and message bubbles (outbound=blue right, inbound=gray left). Local `formatPhone`/`relTime` helpers.

**API:** `GET /api/admin/businesses/[id]/conversations` — selects `business { name, telnyxPhoneNumber }`, then `conversation.findMany` WHERE `businessId=id`, `messages: { some: {} }`, and (if business has a telnyx number) `callerPhone: { not: telnyxPhoneNumber }` (excludes forwarding-loop self-calls). Ordered `lastMessageAt desc`, includes all messages (asc) + appointment id. Returns `{ businessName, conversations: [...] }` with bucket-classification fields. No pagination.

### `/api/admin/businesses/[id]` PATCH — allowedFields & special processing

Whitelist (`allowedFields`); only keys present (`!== undefined`) in body are copied to update data:
```
name, calendarEnabled, telnyxPhoneNumber, forwardingNumber, timezone, businessHours,
servicesOffered, aiGreeting, aiInstructions, aiContext, subscriptionStatus, spamFilterEnabled,
adminNotes, setupFee, monthlyFee, callScreenerEnabled, callScreenerMessage,
missedCallVoiceMessage, missedCallAiEnabled, knownContactVoicemailEnabled, slotDurationMinutes,
bufferMinutes, smsBookingEnabled, cooldownBypassNumbers, bookingPageTitle,
bookingPageServiceLabel, bookingPageConfirmation, bookingRequiresAddress, businessType,
maxMessagesPerConversation, ownerEmail, ownerPhone, googleAdsEnabled, googleAdsCustomerId,
googleAdsTabLabel, smsCooldownDays, massMessagingEnabled
```
`notifyBySms`/`notifyByEmail` are toggled by TogglesTab but are **NOT in `allowedFields`** — those PATCHes silently no-op server-side (drift / bug). `knownContactVoicemailEnabled` and `smsBookingEnabled` ARE whitelisted (both new vs CLAUDE.md).

Special processing:
- **`telnyxPhoneNumber`**: if truthy non-empty string → `normalizeToE164(raw.trim()) || null`; else `null`.
- **`cooldownBypassNumbers`**: array → map `normalizeToE164(v.trim())`, filter falsy; string → split on `/[,;\s]+/`, map `normalizeToE164`, filter falsy; otherwise `[]`.

Returns `{ business }` (raw updated row), `500 { error: 'Failed to update business' }` on throw. No Zod/schema validation beyond the whitelist; invalid enum values for `subscriptionStatus` etc. are accepted as-is.

### `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/contacts` (GET/POST/DELETE)

- **GET** — all contacts for business, `createdAt desc`.
- **POST** — `{ phoneNumber (required), name? }`; 404 if business missing; `normalizePhoneNumber`, reject if `<10` digits; `upsert` on `(businessId, phoneNumber)` (create with name, update name). Created contacts have **no `source`** → treated as existing (skip automated SMS).
- **DELETE** — body `{ id? , phoneNumber? }` (at least one required, 400 otherwise, 400 if body not JSON); deletes by id first, falls back to normalized phone match; always returns `{ ok: true }` (does not surface 0-deleted).

### `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/contacts/bulk` POST

Body `{ contacts: [{ phoneNumber, name? }] }`. 404 if business missing. Normalizes + dedupes by phone (in-memory `Set`), drops `<10` digits, then `createMany` in batches of **100** with `skipDuplicates: true`. Returns `{ ok: true, created, total }`.

### `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/screened-calls` GET

Query `days` (default 30). Returns `{ stats: { blocked, passed, total, blockRate (rounded %), days }, recentCalls (up to 50, createdAt desc) }`. Counts via three parallel `screenedCall.count` (result `'blocked'`/`'passed'`/total) since `now - days`.

### `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/blocked-numbers` (GET/POST/DELETE)

- GET → `{ blockedNumbers }` (createdAt desc).
- POST `{ phoneNumber (required, raw trim — NOT normalized), label? }` → 404 if business missing; `upsert` on `(businessId, phoneNumber)`. Note: phone stored **as-entered** (no normalization), unlike contacts.
- DELETE `?id=<id>` (query param, 400 if missing) → `deleteMany { id, businessId }`, `{ ok: true }`.

### `/api/admin/businesses/[id]/voicemails` GET

404 if business missing. Returns conversations WHERE `recordingUrl != null` (createdAt desc) mapped to `{ conversationId, callerPhone, recordingUrl, voicemailTranscription, createdAt }`. Selects `missedCallAiEnabled` on the business but does not filter on it.

### `/api/admin/view-as` GET — cookie mechanics

`GET /api/admin/view-as?businessId=<id>` or `?exit=1`. Non-admin → `redirect('/dashboard')`. With `exit=1` → `response.cookies.delete('adminViewAs')` and redirect to `/admin`. Otherwise, if `businessId` resolves to an existing business → `response.cookies.set('adminViewAs', businessId, { path: '/', maxAge: 60*60*24 (24h), sameSite: 'lax' })` and redirect to `/dashboard`.

**Cookie consumption** (`lib/get-business-for-dashboard.ts`): reads `cookies().get('adminViewAs')`; only honored when `userId === ADMIN_USER_ID`; if set + valid business found, returns `{ business, isAdminViewAs: true }`. Also read in `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/settings/page.tsx` to alter behavior when admin is impersonating. Cookie has `maxAge: 24h` (NOT session-scoped as CLAUDE.md claims — it persists across browser restarts up to 24h). Not `httpOnly`/`secure` flagged in the set call.

### Mobile responsiveness patterns

- Admin table: `md:hidden` card list + `hidden md:table` desktop table (`ClientTable`).
- Detail panel: `w-full sm:w-[520px] lg:w-[600px]`.
- KPI strip: `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3`.
- All SettingsTab two-column rows: `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2`.
- Tab/section content padding: `px-4 sm:px-6` across TogglesTab/SettingsTab/ToolsTab.
- Header bar `flex-wrap`; search input `flex-1 min-w-[140px] sm:w-56 sm:flex-none`.
- `/admin/[businessId]/conversations` is **desktop-grid only** (`md:grid-cols-5`) — no mobile list/detail toggle like the customer-facing ConversationsClient.

## 13. Auth, Middleware, Multi-Tenancy, Feature Flags, Config & Onboarding

Ground-truth reference for authentication, request routing, tenant scoping, feature-flag derivation, app config, industry presets, onboarding, and Google OAuth. All file paths absolute under repo root `/Users/jacobholtzer/Desktop/Align And Acquire/Apps/missedcall-ai`.

### Middleware (`middleware.ts`)

Clerk-based. Three route matchers; matching order in the handler matters (public checks short-circuit before protect):

```ts
const isProtectedRoute = createRouteMatcher(['/dashboard(.*)', '/onboarding(.*)', '/settings(.*)'])
const isPublicRoute    = createRouteMatcher(['/book/(.*)'])
const isPublicApiRoute = createRouteMatcher(['/api/webhooks/(.*)', '/api/contact'])

export default clerkMiddleware(async (auth, request) => {
  if (isPublicRoute(request)) return        // /book/* — no auth (iframe-embeddable)
  if (isPublicApiRoute(request)) return      // webhooks + /api/contact — no auth
  if (isProtectedRoute(request)) { await auth.protect() }
})
```

| Matcher | Patterns | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| `isProtectedRoute` | `/dashboard(.*)`, `/onboarding(.*)`, `/settings(.*)` | `auth.protect()` → redirect to sign-in if no session |
| `isPublicRoute` | `/book/(.*)` | returns early, never protected |
| `isPublicApiRoute` | `/api/webhooks/(.*)`, **`/api/contact`** | returns early, never protected |

Matcher config:
```js
matcher: [
  '/((?!_next|book|[^?]*\\.(?:html?|css|js(?!on)|jpe?g|webp|png|gif|svg|ttf|woff2?|ico|csv|docx?|xlsx?|zip|webmanifest)).*)',
  '/(api|trpc)(.*)',
]
```
Excludes `_next`, the `book` path segment, and a list of static extensions (note `js(?!on)` keeps `.json` routed). All `/api/*` and `/trpc/*` are matched.

**Admin authorization is NOT in middleware.** No `ADMIN_USER_ID` check exists here. Admin gating is per-route: each `/api/admin/*` handler (and `/admin/page.tsx`) individually checks `userId === process.env.ADMIN_USER_ID`. Any authenticated-route falling outside `isProtectedRoute` is also not protected — e.g. `/api/dashboard/*` routes are NOT in `isProtectedRoute`, so they rely entirely on `requireDashboardBusiness()` returning 401 inside the handler, not on middleware.

### Clerk setup (`app/layout.tsx`)

Root layout wraps the entire tree in `<ClerkProvider>`. Uses `Inter` Google font (`subsets:['latin']`). Body classes: `bg-gray-950 text-white antialiased` (dark default). Renders `<MetaPixel />`, then `<ConditionalNavBar />`, then `{children}`.

Metadata: title default `'Align & Acquire — Catalyzing Business Growth'`, template `'%s | Align & Acquire'`.

Clerk publishable/secret keys are never referenced in app code — the SDK reads `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` / `CLERK_SECRET_KEY` from env automatically.

**`MetaPixel` (`app/components/MetaPixel.tsx`)** — NOT documented in CLAUDE.md. Client component. Reads `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FACEBOOK_PIXEL_ID`. If unset, renders `null` and does nothing. On mount injects the standard Facebook `fbq` pixel `<Script strategy="afterInteractive">` with `fbq('init', PIXEL_ID)` + initial `PageView`. A `usePathname` effect fires `fbq('track','PageView')` on every client-side route change (skips the very first render via a `firstRender` ref).

**`ConditionalNavBar` (`app/components/ConditionalNavBar.tsx`)** — Hides `<NavBar />` when `pathname` starts with `/dashboard`, `/admin`, includes `/embed`, or starts with `/book`. On `/sign-in` or `/sign-up` it wraps NavBar in `hidden md:block` (mobile-hidden on auth pages). NavBar shows otherwise.

### Auth helpers (`lib/auth.ts`)

Server-only. Use `auth()` + `currentUser()` from `@clerk/nextjs/server`.

| Function | Signature | Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `getCurrentBusiness()` | `() => Promise<Business \| null>` | `auth()` → if no `userId` return null; `db.user.findUnique({where:{clerkId:userId}, include:{business:true}})` → returns `user?.business ?? null` |
| `getCurrentUser()` | `() => Promise<(User & {business:Business}) \| null>` | Same lookup, returns the user record with `business` included |
| `needsOnboarding()` | `() => Promise<boolean>` | Returns `false` if no `userId`; else `true` when user row missing OR `user.businessId` falsy |

### Dashboard API auth (`lib/dashboard-auth.ts`)

```ts
export type DashboardAuthResult =
  | { business: NonNullable<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getBusinessForDashboard>>['business']> }
  | NextResponse

export async function requireDashboardBusiness(): Promise<DashboardAuthResult>
```
Flow: `auth()` → no `userId` → `NextResponse.json({error:'Unauthorized'},{status:401})`. Else `db.user.findUnique({where:{clerkId:userId}, include:{business:true}})`. Then `getBusinessForDashboard(userId, user?.business ?? null)`. If no business → `NextResponse.json({error:'No business found'},{status:404})`. Else `{ business }`. Callers must check `instanceof NextResponse` (or `'business' in result`) to branch. Used by every `/api/dashboard/*` route as the sole authn/authz + tenant-scoping mechanism.

### View-as / multi-tenant resolution (`lib/get-business-for-dashboard.ts`)

```ts
const ADMIN_USER_ID = process.env.ADMIN_USER_ID
export interface DashboardBusinessResult { business: Business | null; isAdminViewAs: boolean }
export async function getBusinessForDashboard(userId: string, userBusiness: Business | null): Promise<DashboardBusinessResult>
```
Reads the `adminViewAs` cookie via `cookies()` (async). **Only when** `viewAsId` is set **AND** `userId === ADMIN_USER_ID`: looks up `db.business.findUnique({where:{id:viewAsId}})`; if found returns `{ business, isAdminViewAs:true }`. Otherwise returns `{ business: userBusiness, isAdminViewAs:false }`. A non-admin with a stale/forged `adminViewAs` cookie is ignored — falls through to their own business. The `isAdminViewAs` flag is currently discarded by `requireDashboardBusiness` (which only destructures `business`).

### Feature flags (`lib/business-features.ts`)

Single source of truth for all feature-flag derivation. **Reconciliation with CLAUDE.md: the `BusinessFeatures` type as actually defined does NOT include the `googleAds` field.** CLAUDE.md and the Overview/Ads pages describe a `googleAds: boolean` on a `Features` type — that is a separate page-local type that mirrors `BusinessFeatures` plus `googleAds`; it is not part of `getBusinessFeatures`'s return.

```ts
export type BusinessFeatures = {
  hasSpamFilter: boolean
  hasIvrScreener: boolean
  hasAnyScreening: boolean
  hasMissedCallAi: boolean
  hasForwarding: boolean
  hasCalendar: boolean
  showScreeningCards: boolean
  showAiCards: boolean
  totalCallsMode: 'screened' | 'calls'
}
type BusinessLike = {
  spamFilterEnabled?: boolean | null
  callScreenerEnabled?: boolean | null
  missedCallAiEnabled?: boolean | null
  forwardingNumber?: string | null
  calendarEnabled?: boolean | null
  googleCalendarConnected?: boolean | null
}
export function getBusinessFeatures(business: BusinessLike): BusinessFeatures
```

Exact derivations:

| Field | Derivation |
|---|---|
| `hasSpamFilter` | `business.spamFilterEnabled === true` |
| `hasIvrScreener` | `business.callScreenerEnabled === true` |
| `hasAnyScreening` | `hasSpamFilter \|\| hasIvrScreener` |
| `hasMissedCallAi` | `business.missedCallAiEnabled !== false` (i.e. `true`/`null`/`undefined` all yield `true`) |
| `hasForwarding` | `Boolean(business.forwardingNumber)` |
| `hasCalendar` | `calendarEnabled === true && googleCalendarConnected === true` |
| `showScreeningCards` | `=== hasAnyScreening` |
| `showAiCards` | `=== hasMissedCallAi` |
| `totalCallsMode` | `hasAnyScreening ? 'screened' : 'calls'` |

`totalCallsMode`: `'screened'` → count `ScreenedCall` records; `'calls'` → count `Conversation WHERE callSid IS NOT NULL`. The function does NOT reference `googleAdsEnabled`, `massMessagingEnabled`, or `smsBookingEnabled`.

### Industry defaults (`lib/industry-defaults.ts`) — NOT in CLAUDE.md

New module providing per-industry presets, consumed by `app/onboarding/page.tsx` (server action) and `app/onboarding/OnboardingForm.tsx` (client, for live placeholders/defaults).

```ts
export type IndustryConfig = {
  name: string
  services: string[]
  aiGreeting: string
  bookingPageTitle: string
  bookingPageServiceLabel: string
  bookingRequiresAddress: boolean
  sampleGreeting?: string  // @deprecated, use aiGreeting
  questions?: string[]
  commonCustomerQuestions?: string[]
  specialInstructions?: string
  urgencyKeywords?: string[]
  cannotHelpPlaceholder?: string
}
export const BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  'Landscaping / Lawn Care',
  'Car Detailing / Auto Detailing',
  'HVAC (Heating, Ventilation & Air Conditioning)',
  'Other',
] as const
export type BusinessType = (typeof BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS)[number]
export const industryDefaults: Record<string, IndustryConfig>
export function getIndustryDefaults(businessType: string): IndustryConfig   // falls back to industryDefaults['Other'] on unknown key
export function getAllIndustries(): string[]                                 // [...BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS]
```

Preset summary (each has a tailored `aiGreeting`, `bookingPageTitle`, `bookingPageServiceLabel`, `bookingRequiresAddress:true`):

| Key | services | bookingPageTitle | urgencyKeywords |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Landscaping / Lawn Care` | Hardscaping, Lawn Care, Landscape Design & Installation, Tree Service, Snow Removal | "Schedule a Free In-Person Quote" | — |
| `Car Detailing / Auto Detailing` | Basic Interior Detail, Full Interior Detail, Exterior Wash & Wax, Full Detail Package, Ceramic Coating, Paint Correction | "Schedule Your Detail" | — |
| `HVAC (...)` | AC Repair, Heating Repair, AC Installation, Furnace Installation, Maintenance / Tune-Up, Duct Cleaning | "Schedule a Service Call" | `no heat, no AC, no air, not working, emergency, broken, water leak, smell gas` |
| `Other` | `[]` (empty) | "Schedule an Appointment" | — |

Each also carries `specialInstructions` and `cannotHelpPlaceholder`. Note these business-type label strings differ from the `"Landscaping" \| "Car Detailing" \| "HVAC" \| "Other"` values CLAUDE.md lists for `Business.businessType`.

### Onboarding (`app/onboarding/page.tsx` + `OnboardingForm.tsx`)

Server page: `auth()` + `currentUser()`; redirect `/sign-in` if unauthenticated. If the user already has a business → `redirect('/dashboard')`. Renders `<OnboardingForm createBusiness={createBusiness} />` passing a server action.

`createBusiness(formData)` server action (`'use server'`):
- Re-auths inside the action; throws `'Not authenticated'` if no user.
- Reads form fields: `businessName`, `businessType`, `services`, `businessHours`, `specialInfo`, `cannotHelp`.
- Slug = lowercased `businessName`, non-alnum runs → `-`, trimmed leading/trailing `-`, then suffixed with `'-' + Date.now()` (guarantees uniqueness; not the same as `lib/utils.ts:slugify`).
- `getIndustryDefaults(businessType)` supplies `aiGreeting`, `bookingPageTitle`, `bookingPageServiceLabel`, `bookingRequiresAddress`.
- Builds `aiContext` (multiline: Business Type / Hours / About Us / Cannot Help With) and `aiInstructions` (fallback callback line + a "Do NOT try to help with: {cannotHelp}" directive when provided).
- `db.$transaction`: `business.create({ data: { name, slug, businessType, aiGreeting, aiContext, aiInstructions, servicesOffered: services.split(',').map(trim) (or []), businessHours: businessHours ? {description: businessHours} : undefined, bookingPageTitle, bookingPageServiceLabel, bookingRequiresAddress }})`, then `user.upsert({where:{clerkId}, ...})` linking `businessId`, syncing Clerk email/firstName/lastName/imageUrl, `create` defaults `role:'owner'`.
- `revalidatePath('/dashboard')` then `redirect('/dashboard')`.

Note: `businessHours` is stored as `{ description: <freeform string> }`, NOT the structured `{monday:{open,close,closed},...}` shape elsewhere in the schema — onboarding writes freeform text; structured hours are configured later in Settings.

`OnboardingForm.tsx` (client): industry `<select>` populated from `BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS`; on change it recomputes placeholders/default values via `getIndustryDefaults`. Services textarea uses `key={businessType||'empty'}` to force remount + reset `defaultValue` to the industry's joined services when the type changes. All fields required except `cannotHelp`. Light theme (`bg-gray-50`), branded "MissedCall AI".

### Auth pages

- `app/(auth)/sign-in/[[...sign-in]]/page.tsx`: renders Clerk `<SignIn afterSignInUrl="/dashboard" appearance={...}>` with a heavily customized dark "Align and Acquire · Client portal" theme (orange `#EE6B1A` primary, `!rounded-none` square brutalist styling).
- `app/(auth)/sign-up/[[...sign-up]]/page.tsx`: exists, renders Clerk `<SignUp>` (confirmed present; CLAUDE.md lists it).

### Google OAuth (Calendar)

**Start — `app/api/auth/google/route.ts` (GET, `dynamic='force-dynamic'`):**
- `auth()`; if no `userId` → `redirect('/sign-in')`.
- Requires `?businessId=` query param; missing → `400 {error:'Missing businessId'}`.
- Loads user (`include:{business:true}`); `isAdmin = userId === process.env.ADMIN_USER_ID`; `ownsBusiness = user?.businessId === businessId`. If neither → `403 {error:'Unauthorized'}`.
- `getAuthUrl(businessId)` → `NextResponse.redirect(authUrl)`. Errors → `500 {error:'OAuth failed'}`.

**Callback — `app/api/auth/google/callback/route.ts` (GET, `dynamic='force-dynamic'`):** No Clerk auth (relies on the `state` round-trip). Reads `code`, `state` (= businessId), `error`. On `error` → redirect `/dashboard/settings?google_error=denied`. Missing `code`/`state` → `?google_error=missing_params`. Else `await exchangeCodeForTokens(code, state)` → redirect `?google_connected=1`. Thrown errors → `?google_error=exchange_failed`.

**OAuth construction (`lib/google-calendar.ts`):**
```ts
const SCOPES = ['https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar']     // full read/write calendar
const REDIRECT_URI = process.env.GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI!
getOAuth2Client(): new google.auth.OAuth2(GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET, REDIRECT_URI)
getAuthUrl(state): generateAuthUrl({ access_type:'offline', prompt:'consent', scope:SCOPES, state })
```
`exchangeCodeForTokens(code, businessId)`: `oauth2.getToken(code)` → `business.update`:
- `googleAccessToken: tokens.access_token ?? null`
- `googleCalendarConnected: !!(tokens.access_token && (tokens.refresh_token ?? true))` (true whenever an access token exists, since `refresh_token ?? true` is truthy even without a refresh token)
- `googleRefreshToken` only written when `tokens.refresh_token` present (`prompt:'consent'` forces a fresh refresh token each time). Returns raw `tokens`.

Token storage is per-business on the `Business` row (`googleAccessToken`, `googleRefreshToken`, `googleCalendarConnected`). No service-account flow for calendar (calendar uses per-business OAuth). A separate Google service account IS used elsewhere for Sheets sync (`lib/google-sheets-sync.ts`, `app/api/admin/usage/sheets-sync/route.ts`) via `GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL` / `GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_PRIVATE_KEY` / `GOOGLE_SHEET_ID`.

### App config

**`next.config.js`:**
- Global header on `'/:path*'`: `X-Frame-Options: ALLOWALL` **and** `Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors *` — every route (incl. dashboard) is iframe-able. Intentional for `/book` embeds.
- `experimental.serverActions.bodySizeLimit = '2mb'`.
- `images.remotePatterns`: only `https://img.clerk.com`. (CLAUDE.md doesn't enumerate image domains — this is the sole allowed remote host.)

**`vercel.json`** — NOT in CLAUDE.md. Defines one cron:
```json
{ "crons": [ { "path": "/api/admin/usage/sync", "schedule": "0 6 * * *" } ] }
```
Triggers daily 06:00 UTC Telnyx MDR/CDR usage sync. (CLAUDE.md mentions `CRON_SECRET` "not enforced" and a sync route but doesn't document this scheduled cron.)

**`tsconfig.json`:** `strict:true`, `moduleResolution:'bundler'`, `module:'esnext'`, `jsx:'preserve'`, `noEmit`, `allowJs`, `skipLibCheck`, `resolveJsonModule`, `isolatedModules`, `incremental`, next plugin. Path alias `@/*` → `./*`.

**`lib/db.ts`:** Prisma singleton — `const db = globalForPrisma.prisma ?? new PrismaClient()`; in non-production attaches to `globalThis` to survive HMR. No explicit datasource overrides (uses schema `DATABASE_URL`/`DIRECT_URL`).

**`lib/utils.ts`:** `cn(...)` (clsx+twMerge); `formatPhoneNumber(phone)` → `(XXX) XXX-XXXX` (strips non-digits, optional leading `1`); `formatRelativeTime(date|string)` → `'Just now' / 'N min ago' / 'N hours ago' / 'Yesterday' / 'N days ago' / locale date`; `slugify(text)` → lowercase, strip non-word, spaces→`-`, collapse `-`, trim.

**`lib/business-hours.ts`:** `DEFAULT_BUSINESS_HOURS: Record<string, {open,close}|null>` — Mon–Fri `{open:'09:00',close:'17:00'}`, Sat/Sun `null`. **Shape drift:** actual default uses `null` for closed days and `{open,close}` objects with NO `closed` boolean — CLAUDE.md documents `{ open, close, closed:false }` and "Sat–Sun closed" via a `closed` flag.

Compiled from `grep process.env` over all `.ts`/`.tsx`/`.js` plus `schema.prisma`. Bold = referenced in code but **NOT** in CLAUDE.md's env tables; strikethrough-noted = in CLAUDE.md but **never referenced in code**.

| Env var | Referenced in code | In CLAUDE.md? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ADMIN_USER_ID` | yes | yes | admin gate (per-route + view-as + google start) |
| `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` | yes | yes | |

| `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` / `_SECRET` / `GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI` | yes | yes | calendar OAuth |
| **`GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL`** | yes (`lib/google-sheets-sync.ts`, sheets-sync route) | **no** | Sheets usage sync service account |

| **`VERCEL_URL`** | yes (`lib/notify-owner.ts` fallback) | **no** | fallback link base when `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` unset → `https://${VERCEL_URL}`; final fallback hardcoded `https://alignandacquire.com` |
| `NODE_ENV` | yes | implicit | prisma singleton |
