## 14. Telnyx Usage/Cost Sync, Exports & Google Sheets Sync

This subsystem pulls billing data (Messaging Detail Records / MDR and Call Detail Records / CDR) from the Telnyx **Detail Records Search API**, attributes each record to a tenant `Business` by phone-number match, persists it idempotently into `TelnyxUsageRecord`, and exposes it via admin-only CSV/XLSX export, a Google Sheets push, and a per-business usage dashboard endpoint. There is no webhook involvement — sync is poll-based (manual admin trigger or daily Vercel cron).

### Data model: `TelnyxUsageRecord` (prisma/schema.prisma:116)

```prisma
model TelnyxUsageRecord {
  id              String   @id @default(cuid())
  businessId      String
  business        Business @relation(fields: [businessId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  recordType      String   // schema comment: "sms" | "call"  (code ALSO writes "call_forwarding")
  telnyxRecordId  String   @unique   // Telnyx MDR uuid or CDR id — the idempotency key
  cost            Float    @default(0)   // USD charged by Telnyx
  occurredAt      DateTime
  metadata        Json?
  createdAt       DateTime @default(now())
  @@index([businessId])
  @@index([businessId, recordType])
  @@index([occurredAt])
}
```

`recordType` actual runtime values: `'sms'`, `'call'`, `'call_forwarding'`. The `@unique` on `telnyxRecordId` is what makes every upsert idempotent — re-running a sync over the same window never double-counts.

`metadata` actual shapes (NOT what the schema comment says):
- SMS: `{ cli, cld, direction, status, parts }`
- Call / call_forwarding: `{ cli, cld, direction, call_sec, billed_sec }`

---

### `lib/telnyx-usage-sync.ts` — `syncTelnyxUsage`

```ts
export interface SyncResult {
  mdrsProcessed: number   // count of TelnyxUsageRecord upserts for SMS
  cdrsProcessed: number   // count of upserts for voice (call + call_forwarding)
  messagesUpdated: number // Message rows whose cost was backfilled
  errors: string[]
  debugLog: string[]      // verbose, line-by-line trace of every comparison
}
export async function syncTelnyxUsage(dateRange: string = 'last_90_days'): Promise<SyncResult>
```

`dateRange` is a **string preset**, not a `{start,end}` object. Accepted: `yesterday`, `last_7_days`, `last_30_days`, `last_90_days`. `mapDateRangeToFilter()` maps `'yesterday'` → `'last_7_days'` (Telnyx's `filter[date_range]` has no `yesterday`), everything else passes through, default `last_7_days`.

**Telnyx API call shape** (`fetchTelnyx` → `GET https://api.telnyx.com/v2/detail_records`):

| Query param | Value |
|---|---|
| `filter[record_type]` | `messaging` \| `call` \| `call-control` \| `sip-trunking` |
| `filter[date_range]` | `last_7_days` \| `last_30_days` \| `last_90_days` |
| `page[number]` | incrementing |
| `page[size]` | `100` |
| `sort` | `-created_at` |

Auth: `Authorization: Bearer ${process.env.TELNYX_API_KEY}` (throws `'TELNYX_API_KEY is not set'` if missing). `fetchAllDetailRecords` paginates via `meta.total_pages` until exhausted.

**Control flow of a sync run:**

1. Load all businesses with `telnyxPhoneNumber != null` (`select: { id, telnyxPhoneNumber, forwardingNumber }`).
2. **MDR (messaging)** fetched first via `fetchWithFallback('messaging', filter)` — this is blocking; a failure aborts the whole sync (caught at top level, pushed to `errors`).
3. **Voice CDR** fetched via `fetchVoiceRecordsWithFallback(filter)` inside a `try/catch` — **non-blocking**: if it throws, `voiceFetchFailed=true`, MDR results are still saved and the sync returns OK.
4. Process MDR records → upsert `TelnyxUsageRecord(recordType:'sms')` + backfill `Message.cost`.
5. Process CDR records → upsert `TelnyxUsageRecord(recordType:'call'|'call_forwarding')`.

**Two-level fallback chain.** This is deeper than a single chain — there are TWO nested fallbacks:

- **Voice record-type fallback** (`fetchVoiceRecordsWithFallback`): tries `call` → `call-control` → `sip-trunking` in order. First success wins.
- **Date-range fallback** (`fetchWithFallback`, applied per record type): if `last_90_days` errors, retry `last_30_days` then `last_7_days`; if `last_30_days` errors, retry `last_7_days`. (No date fallback for `last_7_days` / `messaging` baseline.)
- **Usage Reports API "last resort"** (`fetchUsageReportsFallback`): only runs if ALL three voice record types fail. It hits `GET /v2/usage_reports?product=sip-trunking&dimensions=direction&metrics=cost,billed_sec&date_range=...`, sums `cost`/`billed_sec`, and **only writes the totals to `debugLog`** — it is explicitly account-level and produces **no per-business `TelnyxUsageRecord` rows**. After logging it, `fetchVoiceRecordsWithFallback` still rethrows the original error, so the caller treats voice as failed.

**Business matching** (`getBusinessIdForRecord`): collects `[record.cli, record.cld, record.from, record.to]`, and for each business compares its `telnyxPhoneNumber` (normalized via `normalizeToE164`) against each record phone using `phonesMatchE164`. First match wins; business number can be either party. No match → record skipped (counted in `mdrUnmatched`/`cdrUnmatched`). Every comparison is logged to `debugLog`, making the log extremely verbose.

**call vs call_forwarding distinction** (telnyx-usage-sync.ts:413): a CDR is reclassified as `'call_forwarding'` when the business has a `forwardingNumber` AND `record.cld` (the dialed/called number) matches it — i.e. the B-leg forwarding dial. Otherwise it is `'call'`.

**Idempotency key derivation:**
- MDR: `record.uuid ?? record.id ?? mdr-${created_at}-${cli}-${cld}`
- CDR: `record.id ?? record.uuid ?? cdr-${started_at ?? created_at}-${cli}-${cld}`

`getOccurredAt` falls back through `created_at → completed_at → sent_at → started_at → finished_at → now()`. `parseCost` parses the string `cost` field, returns 0 on null/empty/NaN.

**`Message.cost` backfill** (telnyx-usage-sync.ts:389): only for MDR records where `record.uuid` is present AND `cost > 0`. Runs `db.message.updateMany({ where: { telnyxSid: record.uuid }, data: { cost } })` and adds `updated.count` to `messagesUpdated`. This is the only cross-table side effect; CDRs do not touch any other table.

Per-record errors are caught individually and pushed to `result.errors` as `MDR <uuid>: ...` / `CDR <id>: ...` without aborting the loop.

---

### `lib/usage-export.ts` — aggregation for CSV/XLSX

**`parseExportDateRange(preset, startDate?, endDate?)` → `{ start: Date; end: Date }`**

| `preset` | Range |
|---|---|
| `this_week` | `startOfWeek/endOfWeek(now, { weekStartsOn: 1 })` (Mon-start) |
| `this_month` | `startOfMonth/endOfMonth(now)` |
| `last_month` | `subMonths(now,1)` → start/end of that month |
| `custom` | `startOfDay(parseISO(startDate))` → `endOfDay(parseISO(endDate))`; throws if either missing |

Invalid preset throws `Invalid date range preset: ...`.

**`getUsageForExport(range)` → `ExportResult`**

```ts
interface DailyUsageRow {
  date: string; businessId: string; businessName: string; phoneNumber: string;
  inboundSms: number; outboundSms: number; smsCost: number;
  inboundCalls: number; outboundCalls: number; callMinutes: number;
  voiceCost: number; totalCost: number;
}
interface BusinessSubtotal { /* same fields minus date/businessId */ }
interface ExportResult { dailyRows: DailyUsageRow[]; businessSubtotals: BusinessSubtotal[]; grandTotal: BusinessSubtotal }
```

Logic: loads all `telnyxPhoneNumber != null` businesses + all `TelnyxUsageRecord` with `occurredAt` in `[startOfDay(range.start), endOfDay(range.end)]`. Pre-seeds a `Map` keyed `"YYYY-MM-DD|businessId"` for **every (day × business)** pair via `eachDayOfInterval` (so the matrix is dense — zero rows are emitted for days/businesses with no usage). For each record: reads `metadata.direction` (lowercased) and `metadata.billed_sec ?? call_sec` for minutes. SMS → inc inbound/outbound + add `smsCost`; `call` → inc inbound/outbound calls + minutes + `voiceCost`; `call_forwarding` → minutes + `voiceCost` (no call count increment). `callMinutes = sec/60`. **Note:** `call_forwarding` cost is tracked separately internally (`callForwardingCost`) but folded INTO `voiceCost` in the emitted `DailyUsageRow.voiceCost` (`row.voiceCost + row.callForwardingCost`). Money rounded to 4 dp, minutes to 1 dp. `grandTotal.businessName === 'TOTAL'`.

---

### `lib/google-sheets-sync.ts` — Google Sheets push (UNDER-DOCUMENTED in CLAUDE.md)

```ts
export interface SheetsSyncResult { ok: boolean; rowsAppended: number; sheetName: string; error?: string }
export async function syncUsageToGoogleSheets(date: Date = subDays(new Date(), 1)): Promise<SheetsSyncResult>
```

`import 'server-only'`. Auth is a **Google service account** (NOT the per-business OAuth used elsewhere), scope `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets`, via `new google.auth.GoogleAuth({ credentials: { client_email, private_key }, scopes })`. Private key has `\\n` → `\n` un-escaping applied.

**Required env vars (none in CLAUDE.md):**

| Var | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL` | Service account `client_email`; the target sheet must be shared with this address |
| `GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_PRIVATE_KEY` | Service account private key (with literal `\n`) |
| `GOOGLE_SHEET_ID` | Target spreadsheet ID |

Missing email/key → throws; missing sheet id → throws.

**Behavior:**
1. `ensureMonthlyTab` — tab name is `format(date, 'MMMM yyyy')` (e.g. `"June 2026"`). If a sheet/tab with that title doesn't exist, it's created via `spreadsheets.batchUpdate({ addSheet })`. One tab per calendar month.
2. `getUsageForDate(date)` — same aggregation logic as the exporter but for a single day, returning one object per business. Here `call_forwarding` cost is kept in its own `callForwardingCost` field (NOT folded into voice), and `totalCost = smsCost + voiceCost + callForwardingCost`.
3. Builds rows with a **12-column** layout (note the extra `Call Forwarding Cost` column vs the 11-column CSV/XLSX export):

   `Date | Business Name | Phone Number | Inbound SMS | Outbound SMS | SMS Cost | Inbound Calls | Outbound Calls | Call Minutes | Voice Cost | Call Forwarding Cost | Total Cost`

4. Appends a `TOTAL` summary row (sums across businesses).
5. **Header detection:** reads `'<tab>'!A1`; if it equals `'Date'`, headers already exist → `values.append` (range `'<tab>'!A:L`, `INSERT_ROWS`, `USER_ENTERED`). Otherwise → `values.update` writing `[headers, ...rows, summary]` to `'<tab>'!A1:L<n>`. Money 4 dp, minutes 1 dp.

`rowsAppended` = number of value rows written (rows + summary, plus header on first write).

---

### Admin routes

All admin routes gate on `userId === process.env.ADMIN_USER_ID` (Clerk `auth()`), returning `403 { error: 'Unauthorized' }` otherwise. None use `requireDashboardBusiness`.

**`/api/admin/usage/sync`** (`route.ts`) — **GET and POST** (shared `handleSync`).
- Query: `?dateRange=yesterday|last_7_days|last_30_days|last_90_days`.
- **CRON bypass:** if header `Authorization: Bearer ${CRON_SECRET}` matches, `isCron=true`, the Clerk admin check is **skipped**, and `dateRange` defaults to `'yesterday'` (manual default is `'last_90_days'`). So `CRON_SECRET` *is* enforced here (contradicting CLAUDE.md's claim it's unused).
- 500 if `TELNYX_API_KEY` unset. Returns `{ ok: true, ...SyncResult }` or 500 `{ error, detail }`.
- **Driven by `vercel.json` cron:** `{ "path": "/api/admin/usage/sync", "schedule": "0 6 * * *" }` — daily 06:00 UTC. (The cron call carries no `dateRange` and, per Vercel, no `CRON_SECRET` header unless configured — meaning the cron hits the route as an unauthenticated request and would 403 unless `CRON_SECRET` is wired into the cron, or it relies on the default no-auth path; the code requires the bearer header to bypass auth.)

**`/api/admin/usage/export`** (`route.ts`) — **GET only**.
- Query: `dateRange` (default `this_month`), `startDate`, `endDate`, `format` (`csv` default, or `xlsx`).
- ⚠️ **Param mismatch:** `app/admin/AdminTools.tsx:67` calls `?preset=this_month`, but the route reads `dateRange`. `preset` is ignored; it only works because `dateRange` defaults to `this_month`.
- Builds a single AoA: header row, daily rows, blank row, `'Per business subtotals'` banner, header row again, subtotal rows, blank, grand-total row. **11-column** layout (`Call Forwarding Cost` column is omitted — folded into `Voice Cost` by `getUsageForExport`).
- `xlsx`: `XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet` → workbook sheet `'Usage Report'` → `XLSX.write({ type:'buffer', bookType:'xlsx' })`; `Content-Type: application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet`, attachment `usage-report-<start>-to-<end>.xlsx`.
- `csv`: hand-rolled `toCsv` with `escapeCsv` (quotes fields containing `, " \n \r`), CRLF line endings, attachment `.csv`.
- Errors → 400 `{ error: msg }`.

**`/api/admin/usage/sheets-sync`** (`route.ts`) — **POST only**.
- Query: `?date=YYYY-MM-DD` (optional; invalid → 400; absent → `subDays(now,1)`).
- 500 if any of the 3 Google Sheets env vars unset (explicit message naming all three).
- Returns `syncUsageToGoogleSheets(date)` result; errors → 500 `{ ok: false, error }`.

**`/api/admin/telnyx-test`** (`route.ts`) — **GET only**, debug. Hits `GET /v2/detail_records` with `filter[record_type]=messaging&filter[date_range]=last_7_days&page[size]=100&sort=-created_at` (single page). Returns `{ totalCount, meta, records, raw }` where each record is normalized `{ from: cli??from, to: cld??to, direction, cost, created_at, uuid }`. On Telnyx non-OK: status `502` if Telnyx ≥500 else `400`, with `raw` passthrough. 500 if `TELNYX_API_KEY` unset.

**`/api/admin/businesses/[id]/usage`** (`route.ts`) — **GET only**. Async params `{ id }`. 404 if business not found. Runs 12 parallel queries. Returns a rich per-business object:

```ts
{
  business: { id, name },
  sms: { thisWeek, allTime },                 // outbound Message counts
  missedCallSmsTriggered,                       // conversations with ≥1 outbound message
  skips: { cooldown, existingContact, blocked, total },  // CooldownSkipLog by reason
  moneySaved,                                   // totalSkips * avgSmsCost (avgSmsCost = smsCostAllTime/smsAllTime, fallback 0.0079)
  cost: { smsThisWeek, voiceThisWeek, callForwardingThisWeek, totalThisWeek,
          smsAllTime, voiceAllTime, callForwardingAllTime, totalAllTime },  // from TelnyxUsageRecord.groupBy(recordType)._sum.cost
  calls: { thisWeek, allTime, minutesThisWeek, minutesAllTime },  // from call/call_forwarding records' billed_sec/call_sec
  skipLogs,                                     // last 50 CooldownSkipLog
  recentMessages,                               // last 30 messages, content truncated to 100 chars, per-message cost (4 dp) or null
}
```

`weekStart` = `now - 7 days` set to local midnight. Cost sums are read by `recordType` key from the `groupBy` result (`sms` / `call` / `call_forwarding`). All money rounded to 2 dp (except `recentMessages[].cost` 4 dp), minutes 1 dp.

---

### Consumers / wiring (`app/admin/AdminTools.tsx`)

A "Tools" dropdown in the admin header:
- **Sync Telnyx Usage** → `POST /api/admin/usage/sync?dateRange=last_90_days`, toasts `mdrsProcessed`/`cdrsProcessed`.
- **Sync Google Ads** → `POST /api/admin/google-ads/sync` (out of this subsystem).
- **Sync Google Sheets** → `POST /api/admin/usage/sheets-sync` (no date → yesterday).
- **Export to Excel** → `window.open('/api/admin/usage/export?preset=this_month')` — but the route ignores `preset` (see param mismatch above), and opening a `format`-less URL yields **CSV**, not XLSX, despite the label "Export to Excel".

---

## 15. Google Ads Integration

Optional, per-business Google Ads dashboard. Campaign-level performance metrics are pulled from the Google Ads API via GAQL, stored as daily-per-campaign rows in `GoogleAdsSnapshot`, and surfaced on `/dashboard/ads` (summary cards, recharts daily trend, campaign table). Sync is manual/admin-triggered — there is **no cron job** wired in this codebase; the UI empty-state copy claims "Data syncs daily" but nothing in these files schedules a daily sync.

### Source files

| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `lib/google-ads.ts` | API client singleton + sync functions (`getGoogleAdsClient`, `syncGoogleAdsData`, `syncAllBusinessAds`) |
| `app/api/dashboard/google-ads/route.ts` | `GET` aggregated ad data for the authed business (403 if `!googleAdsEnabled`) |
| `app/api/dashboard/google-ads/sync/route.ts` | `POST` manual sync trigger for the authed business |
| `app/api/admin/google-ads/sync/route.ts` | `POST` admin sync (one business or all) |
| `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/ads/page.tsx` | Server wrapper, FeatureGate, renders `<AdsClient />` |
| `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/ads/AdsClient.tsx` | Client UI: date pickers, 6 cards, recharts line chart, campaign table, refresh |
| `app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:58-59` | Sidebar nav gating |
| `prisma/schema.prisma:561-580` | `GoogleAdsSnapshot` model |

---

### `lib/google-ads.ts`

```typescript
let _client: GoogleAdsApi | null = null

export function getGoogleAdsClient(): GoogleAdsApi
export async function syncGoogleAdsData(
  businessId: string,
  startDate?: string,   // YYYY-MM-DD, default 30 days ago
  endDate?: string,     // YYYY-MM-DD, default yesterday
): Promise<{ rowsSynced: number; errors: string[] }>
export async function syncAllBusinessAds(): Promise<{ synced: number; errors: string[] }>
```

#### `getGoogleAdsClient()` (lib/google-ads.ts:13-21)
Module-scope singleton (`_client`). Constructs `new GoogleAdsApi({ client_id, client_secret, developer_token })` from env vars, all non-null-asserted (`!`):

| Env var | Used as |
|---|---|
| `GOOGLE_ADS_CLIENT_ID` | `client_id` |
| `GOOGLE_ADS_CLIENT_SECRET` | `client_secret` |
| `GOOGLE_ADS_DEVELOPER_TOKEN` | `developer_token` |

Note: `GOOGLE_ADS_REFRESH_TOKEN` and `GOOGLE_ADS_MCC_ID` are **not** passed to the client constructor — they are passed per-customer inside `syncGoogleAdsData` (see below).

#### `syncGoogleAdsData(businessId, startDate?, endDate?)` (lib/google-ads.ts:33-149)

Control flow:
1. Fetch business `{ googleAdsCustomerId, googleAdsEnabled, name }` by id.
2. Guard: if not found → `{ rowsSynced: 0, errors: ['Business not found'] }`.
3. Guard: if `!googleAdsEnabled || !googleAdsCustomerId` → `{ rowsSynced: 0, errors: ['Google Ads not enabled or no customer ID'] }`.
4. Compute default date range from `new Date()`: `defaultEnd` = now − 1 day (yesterday), `defaultStart` = now − 30 days. `start`/`end` use the passed args or these defaults, formatted as `toISOString().split('T')[0]` (YYYY-MM-DD).
5. Build the customer handle:
   ```typescript
   client.Customer({
     customer_id: business.googleAdsCustomerId,
     refresh_token: process.env.GOOGLE_ADS_REFRESH_TOKEN!,
     login_customer_id: process.env.GOOGLE_ADS_MCC_ID,
   })
   ```
   So `GOOGLE_ADS_REFRESH_TOKEN` → `refresh_token`, `GOOGLE_ADS_MCC_ID` → `login_customer_id` (for MCC child-account access).
6. **GAQL query** (string-interpolated date range — not parameterized):
   ```sql
   SELECT
     campaign.id, campaign.name,
     metrics.impressions, metrics.clicks, metrics.cost_micros,
     metrics.conversions, metrics.ctr, metrics.cost_per_conversion,
     segments.date
   FROM campaign
   WHERE segments.date BETWEEN '${start}' AND '${end}'
   ORDER BY segments.date DESC
   ```
7. `customer.query(query)` wrapped in try/catch. On failure: logs `[GOOGLE ADS] Query failed...` and returns `{ rowsSynced: 0, errors: [msg] }`.
8. Per-row loop (each row = one campaign on one date), each iteration in its own try/catch (row errors are collected into `errors[]`, loop continues):
   - `campaignId = String(row.campaign?.id ?? '')`, `campaignName = String(row.campaign?.name ?? 'Unknown Campaign')`, `dateStr = String(row.segments?.date ?? '')`.
   - Skip row if `!campaignId || !dateStr`.
   - `cost = costMicros / 1_000_000` (cost_micros → USD).
   - **costPerConversion derivation** (lib/google-ads.ts:108-109):
     ```typescript
     const rawCpc = Number(row.metrics?.cost_per_conversion ?? 0)
     const costPerConversion = conversions > 0
       ? (rawCpc > 0 ? rawCpc / 1_000_000 : cost / conversions)
       : null
     ```
     If conversions == 0 → `null`. If the API returned a `cost_per_conversion` (in micros) → divide by 1e6. Else fall back to computed `cost / conversions`.
   - `date = new Date(dateStr + 'T00:00:00.000Z')` (start-of-day UTC).
   - Upsert into `googleAdsSnapshot` keyed on the compound unique `businessId_campaignId_date`. `create` writes all fields; `update` writes everything **except** `businessId/campaignId/date` (the key fields) — notably `campaignName` is refreshed on update.
   - `rowsSynced++` on success.
9. Logs `[GOOGLE ADS] Synced N rows for business "<name>"` and returns `{ rowsSynced, errors }`.

#### `syncAllBusinessAds()` (lib/google-ads.ts:156-180)
`db.business.findMany({ where: { googleAdsEnabled: true, googleAdsCustomerId: { not: null } } })`, then sequentially `await syncGoogleAdsData(biz.id)` for each (default date range). Aggregates `totalSynced` and prefixes each error with `[biz.name]`. Returns `{ synced, errors }`.

---

### `GET /api/dashboard/google-ads` (route.ts)

Auth via `requireDashboardBusiness()` (returns early if it's a `NextResponse`). **403** `{ error: 'Feature not available' }` if `!business.googleAdsEnabled`.

Query params:
| Param | Default | Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `groupBy` | `'day'` | Read into a `groupBy` variable but **never used** — both `daily` and `campaigns` aggregations are always computed and returned regardless. |
| `startDate` | now − 30 days | Parsed as `new Date(startDate + 'T00:00:00.000Z')` |
| `endDate` | now (`new Date()`) | Parsed as `new Date(endDate + 'T23:59:59.999Z')` |

Queries `googleAdsSnapshot.findMany({ where: { businessId, date: { gte: startDate, lte: endDate } }, orderBy: { date: 'asc' } })` and aggregates in JS:

- **totals**: sums `impressions/clicks/cost/conversions`; `avgCtr = impressions>0 ? clicks/impressions : 0` (recomputed, not summed from per-row ctr); `avgCostPerConversion = conversions>0 ? cost/conversions : null`.
- **daily**: `Map` keyed by `date.toISOString().split('T')[0]`, summed, sorted ascending by date string. Each entry `{ date, impressions, clicks, cost, conversions }` (no ctr/costPerConversion on daily rows).
- **campaigns**: `Map` keyed by `campaignId`, summed; `campaignName` overwritten with the latest snapshot's value; then `.map` adds `ctr = impressions>0 ? clicks/impressions : 0` and `costPerConversion = conversions>0 ? cost/conversions : null`; sorted by `cost` **descending** (highest spend first).
- **lastSyncedAt**: max `createdAt` across the matched snapshots → ISO string, or `null` if no snapshots.

Response shape (200):
```json
{
  "totals": { "impressions", "clicks", "cost", "conversions", "avgCtr", "avgCostPerConversion" },
  "daily": [ { "date", "impressions", "clicks", "cost", "conversions" } ],
  "campaigns": [ { "campaignId", "campaignName", "impressions", "clicks", "cost", "conversions", "ctr", "costPerConversion" } ],
  "lastSyncedAt": "ISO | null"
}
```

---

### `POST /api/dashboard/google-ads/sync` (sync/route.ts)

`POST()` with no body. Auth via `requireDashboardBusiness()`. Guards:
- **403** `{ error: 'Feature not available' }` if `!business.googleAdsEnabled`.
- **400** `{ error: 'Google Ads Customer ID is not configured' }` if `!(business as any).googleAdsCustomerId`.

Calls `syncGoogleAdsData(business.id)` (default 30-day range). On success returns:
```json
{ "success": true, "rowsSynced": <n>, "errors": [...], "lastSyncedAt": "<now ISO>" }
```
`lastSyncedAt` here is `new Date().toISOString()` (current time, not derived from snapshots). On thrown error: logs `[DASHBOARD GOOGLE ADS SYNC]` and returns **500** `{ error: 'Sync failed', detail: <message> }`.

---

### `POST /api/admin/google-ads/sync` (admin/google-ads/sync/route.ts)

Admin-only: `const { userId } = await auth()`; **403** `{ error: 'Unauthorized' }` if `!userId || userId !== process.env.ADMIN_USER_ID`. Body parsed with `request.json().catch(() => ({}))` (tolerant of empty body).

- If `body.businessId` present → `syncGoogleAdsData(businessId)` → `{ synced: result.rowsSynced, errors }`.
- Else → `syncAllBusinessAds()` → `{ synced: result.synced, errors }`.
- On throw: logs `[ADMIN GOOGLE ADS SYNC]`, returns **500** `{ error: 'Sync failed', detail }`.

Note the response key is `synced` (not `rowsSynced`) in both branches, differing from the dashboard sync route.

---

### `GoogleAdsSnapshot` model (prisma/schema.prisma:561-580)

| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | `String @id @default(cuid())` | |
| `businessId` | `String` | FK → Business, `onDelete: Cascade` |
| `date` | `DateTime` | Reporting date, start-of-day UTC |
| `campaignId` | `String` | Google Ads campaign ID |
| `campaignName` | `String` | Name at time of sync (refreshed on upsert) |
| `impressions` | `Int @default(0)` | |
| `clicks` | `Int @default(0)` | |
| `cost` | `Float @default(0)` | USD (cost_micros ÷ 1e6) |
| `conversions` | `Float @default(0)` | |
| `ctr` | `Float @default(0)` | 0.0–1.0 |
| `costPerConversion` | `Float?` | null if 0 conversions |
| `createdAt` | `DateTime @default(now())` | used for `lastSyncedAt` |

Constraints: `@@unique([businessId, campaignId, date])` (upsert key `businessId_campaignId_date`), `@@index([businessId, date])`. No `updatedAt` column — re-syncing a row does not change `createdAt`, so `lastSyncedAt` reflects the *first* time a given (campaign,date) row was inserted, not the most recent re-sync.

### Business fields (prisma/schema.prisma:57-59, relation :107)

| Field | Type | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `googleAdsCustomerId` | `String?` | Customer ID, no dashes (e.g. `"1234567890"`) |
| `googleAdsEnabled` | `Boolean @default(false)` | Gates nav item, dashboard page, and all routes |
| `googleAdsTabLabel` | `String? @default("Google Ads")` | Custom sidebar label |
| `googleAdsSnapshots` | `GoogleAdsSnapshot[]` | Relation |

---

### `AdsClient.tsx` (UI)

Client component. State: `range: '7d'|'30d'|'90d'|'custom'` (default `'30d'`), `customStart`, `customEnd`, `loading` (default `true`), `error`, `data: AdsResponse|null`, `syncing`, `lastSyncedAt`.

- `rangeToParams(range, customStart, customEnd)` builds `startDate=YYYY-MM-DD&endDate=YYYY-MM-DD`. Custom requires both dates set; presets compute start = now − {7,90, else 30} days, end = today.
- `fetchData` (useCallback, deps `[range, customStart, customEnd]`) GETs `/api/dashboard/google-ads?<params>`; throws `'Failed to load ad data'` if `!res.ok`; sets `data` and `lastSyncedAt` (if present). Runs on mount and whenever `fetchData` identity changes via `useEffect`.
- `handleRefresh` POSTs `/api/dashboard/google-ads/sync`, on error throws `json.error || 'Sync failed'`, sets `lastSyncedAt` to response value or `new Date().toISOString()`, then re-`fetchData()`. Button shows spinning `RefreshCw` and "Syncing…" while `syncing`.

**Date range picker**: pill button group `Last 7 Days / Last 30 Days / Last 90 Days` (from `RANGE_OPTIONS`) plus a `Custom` button. When `range === 'custom'`, two `<input type="date">` fields (Start/End) render.

**6 summary cards** (grid `grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6`), each via `SummaryCard({ icon, label, value })`:

| Card | Icon (lucide) | Value |
|---|---|---|
| Total Spend | `DollarSign` (green) | `formatCurrency(totals.cost)` |
| Total Clicks | `MousePointerClick` (blue) | `formatNumber(totals.clicks)` |
| Impressions | `Eye` (purple) | `formatNumber(totals.impressions)` |
| Avg CTR | `TrendingUp` (orange) | `formatPercent(totals.avgCtr)` (`×100, 2dp, %`) |
| Conversions | `Target` (indigo) | `formatNumber(totals.conversions)` |
| Cost / Conv. | `DollarSign` (red) | `formatCurrency(avgCostPerConversion)` or `—` if null |

**Daily trend chart**: recharts `LineChart` inside `ResponsiveContainer` (h-72), dual Y-axis — left axis `$`-formatted with a `Spend` line (`dataKey="cost"`, blue `#2563eb`), right axis with a `Clicks` line (`dataKey="clicks"`, green `#16a34a`). X-axis uses `formatShortDate` ("Mon D"). Tooltip formats `Spend` as currency, others as number.

**Campaign breakdown table**: columns Campaign / Impressions / Clicks / CTR / Spend / Conversions / Cost / Conv., one row per `data.campaigns` entry (already sorted by spend desc server-side). Cost/Conv. shows `—` when null.

**States**: loading spinner; red error box; empty state ("No ad data available yet" / "Data syncs daily. Check back after your first sync.") shown when `data.daily.length === 0`. Main content renders only when `data.daily.length > 0`.

### Page wrapper + nav gating

- `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/ads/page.tsx`: server component. Resolves business via `getBusinessForDashboard(userId, user?.business)` (supports admin view-as); redirects to `/sign-in` or `/onboarding` as needed. Wraps `<AdsClient />` in `<FeatureGate mode="locked" enabled={business.googleAdsEnabled} feature="Google Ads" valueProp="See your Google Ads performance and ROI alongside your leads" businessName={business.name} />`.
- Sidebar nav (`app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:58-59`): item pushed only when `business.googleAdsEnabled` is truthy, label = `googleAdsTabLabel || 'Google Ads'`, `href: '/dashboard/ads'`, icon `'Megaphone'`. Inserted after Contacts/Jobs and **before Settings**.

### Security / behavior notes
- GAQL date range is string-interpolated into the query (`BETWEEN '${start}' AND '${end}'`). In `syncGoogleAdsData` the values are derived from `Date.toISOString()` so they're safe; but the dashboard/admin sync routes always call `syncGoogleAdsData` with no date args, so caller-supplied dates never reach the GAQL string in this codebase.
- The dashboard `GET` route does not enforce `googleAdsCustomerId` — only `googleAdsEnabled`. A business with `googleAdsEnabled=true` but no customer ID returns empty aggregates (no snapshots) rather than an error.
- `groupBy` query param is accepted but inert (both aggregations always returned).

## 16. Public/Marketing Surface, Public Booking & Shared Components

The public surface has been rebranded from the single-product **"MissedCall AI"** into a multi-service **"Align and Acquire"** growth-ops agency for trade businesses (HVAC, landscaping, plumbing, detailing). All new marketing pages share one design system ("Industrial Dispatch" — asphalt-black `#16181C`, safety-orange `#EE6B1A`, work-blue `#1A4A70`, concrete off-white `#F2F0EB`, steel-gray `#6E7681`), the `<BrandFooter />` footer, and helper components (`ScrollReveal`, `Marquee`, `CountUp`, `SmsThread`). Global chrome is `app/layout.tsx` → `ClerkProvider` → `<MetaPixel />` + `<ConditionalNavBar />`.

### Marketing route catalog

| Route | File | Purpose | Data source / form POST | Notable |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `/` | `app/page.tsx` (server) | Homepage | `<ContactForm>` → `POST /api/contact`; `<ROICalculator>` (client-only, no API) | 3-service grid links to `/services#missedcall-ai`, `#websites`, `#campaigns`. Hero uses animated `<SmsThread>`. NO DemoForm here (drift). |
| `/missedcall-ai` | `app/missedcall-ai/page.tsx` (client) | Flagship product page | `<DemoForm>` → `POST /api/book-demo` → `/demo-requested`; `<ROICalculator>` | Hero `<SmsThread>`, `<Marquee>`/`<CountUp>` stats, FAQ `<details>`, anchor `#book-demo`. FAQ still says "Plans start at $200/month" (stale copy). |

| `/services` | `app/services/page.tsx` (server) | All 7 services overview | Links to sub-pages + `/pricing`/`/book` | Inline mockups: `GoogleAdsMockup`, `CampaignMockup`, `SpamMockup`, calendar/CRM mockups. Uses `next/image` portfolio thumbnails. |
| `/websites` | `app/websites/page.tsx` (server) | Web-design portfolio | `<WebsiteQuoteForm>` → `POST /api/book-demo` → `/demo-requested` | 6 `Project` cards (Fraaza, Jack of All Blades, Aesthetic Gardener, Learning Logs, Apex Detail Studio, Breeze Tees) with live URLs + `next/image`. |

Renders its own logo/nav/footer + scoped `<style>`. **Holds the OLD prices** (Growth $200 / Pro $290 / All In $385). `/embed` segment → `ConditionalNavBar` renders null. |

### Pricing (ground truth, all hardcoded literals)

`/pricing` tiers (`TIERS[]`):

| Tier | Monthly | Setup | Popular | Spam add-on line |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Catch | $349 | $400 | no | "+$75/mo" add-on |
| Grow | $549 | $500 | yes | "+$75/mo" add-on |
| Automate | $900 | $750 | no | Spam screening **included** |

`/pricing` à-la-carte (`ALA_CARTE[]`): MissedCall AI $349/$299 · Custom Website $179/$250 · Google Ads $225/$300 · Email & SMS Campaigns $175/$150 · Leads Dashboard $129/$0 · Calendar Integration $99/$0 · Spam Call Screening $89/$150. The dedicated `/campaigns` ($75/$100) and `/ads-management` ($125/$250) pages disagree with these à-la-carte numbers.

### Public booking (`/book/[businessSlug]` and `/embed`)

Two near-identical client components: `app/book/[businessSlug]/page.tsx` (full page) and `app/book/[businessSlug]/embed/page.tsx` (iframe; posts `{type:'booking-embed-height', height}` to `window.parent`, has on-screen `fetchStatus`/`mountError` debug). Both:
- Read `slug` from `useParams()`.
- `GET /api/bookings/available-slots?businessSlug=${slug}` for config (`businessName`, `bookingPageTitle`, `bookingPageServiceLabel`, `bookingPageConfirmation`, `bookingRequiresAddress`, `servicesOffered`, `calendarEnabled`, `today`), then again with `&start=&end=` per selected date for `{ slots, noMoreAvailabilityToday }`.
- Render `<BookingPageHeader>` + `<BookingCalendar>` (month grid, Sun-first, `today`..`maxDate=today+30d`, blue selected `#2563eb`).
- Submit `POST /api/bookings/create` with `{ businessSlug, customerName, customerPhone, customerEmail?, slotStart, serviceType, notes, customerAddress? }`. Confirmation interpolates `{businessName}/{date}/{time}/{service}` into `bookingPageConfirmation`.
- Gate: render "Quote Scheduling Not Available" unless `calendarEnabled===true && businessName` and no "not connected" error. These pages use the **light blue/white theme**, not the new brand.

### `/book` discovery funnel (distinct from slug booking)

```
Step type: 'qualify-business'|'disqualified'|'qualify-trade'|'qualify-needs'|'calendar'|'form'|'confirmation'
STEPS = ['qualify-business','qualify-trade','qualify-needs','calendar','form']  // progress bar
EXTRA_NEEDS_OPTIONS = ['Website','Ads','Spam call blocking','Just the Missed-Call AI system']
```
`GET /api/marketing-bookings` returns `{ days: ApiDay[] }` where `ApiDay = { date, isToday, label, timezoneLabel, slots:{iso,display}[] }`. Booking window (route constants): **8:00 AM–4:00 PM ET, 30-min slots, 15-min buffer ⇒ starts every 45 min** (8:00, 8:45, 9:30…); `MIN_NOTICE_HOURS=2`, `MAX_DAYS_AHEAD=14`. Submit `POST /api/marketing-bookings` `{ name, phone, email, businessName, tradeType?, extraNeeds[], notes?, smsConsent, slotStart }`. Business resolved via `MARKETING_BUSINESS_ID`/`MARKETING_BUSINESS_SLUG`; confirmation SMS from `MARKETING_TELNYX_NUMBER`. Funnel fires `fbq('track','Schedule')` once on confirmation (`scheduleFiredRef`).

### Shared form components → API targets

| Component | Where used | POST target | Payload | On success |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ContactForm.tsx` | homepage `#contact` | `/api/contact` | `{ name, phone?, message, smsConsent }` — **no businessId/slug/email** | inline "Message sent." |
| `WebsiteQuoteForm.tsx` | `/websites` | `/api/book-demo` | `{ name, business, email, phone, businessType, message?, smsConsent }` | `router.push('/demo-requested')` |
| `DemoForm.tsx` | `/missedcall-ai` | `/api/book-demo` | `{ name, business, email, phone?, businessType, smsConsent }` | `router.push('/demo-requested')` |
| `/book` form | `/book` | `/api/marketing-bookings` | see above | step→`confirmation` |
| `/book/[slug]` form | slug booking | `/api/bookings/create` | see above | inline confirmation |

`/api/contact` (verified): destructures `{ name, phone, message, smsConsent, businessId, businessSlug, email }`. If **neither** `businessId` nor `businessSlug` is present and `RESEND_API_KEY`+`YOUR_EMAIL` set → Resend email from `Align and Acquire <onboarding@resend.dev>` (all fields `escapeHtml`'d). When a business is resolved → `findOrCreateContact({source:'website_form'})` + `WebsiteLead` create + `notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead()`. Because `ContactForm` never sends a business id, the homepage form always takes the marketing-email path.

## 17. Drift from CLAUDE.md & Doc Accuracy

This appendix reconciles the `CLAUDE.md` reference (self-dated "as of May 2026") against the **actual source tree** as it exists now. Every drift claim below cites a `file:line` or grep result. Use this as the override layer: where CLAUDE.md and this appendix disagree, **trust this appendix**.

### A. Owner email delivery: now Resend everywhere, NOT nodemailer/SMTP

This is the single biggest behavioral drift. CLAUDE.md repeatedly states owner notifications (booking/lead/human-needed/AI-failed) go out via **nodemailer / Gmail SMTP** (Sections 8, 12, gotchas 23–25). In reality `lib/notify-owner.ts` still *imports* and *configures* a nodemailer transporter (`getTransporter()` at `lib/notify-owner.ts:662-691`) but **`sendEmail()` no longer uses it** — it sends via Resend:

```ts
// lib/notify-owner.ts:696-714
async function sendEmail(to, subject, text, html?) {
  const resend = new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY)
  const result = await resend.emails.send({
    from: 'notifications@alignandacquire.com',
    to, subject,
    html: html ?? plainTextToEmailHtml(text),
  })
  ...
}
```

Consequences for the doc:
- All owner notification emails send **from `notifications@alignandacquire.com` via Resend**, not from `SMTP_FROM`/`SMTP_USER` via Gmail. Gotcha #25 ("Emails send from `SMTP_FROM` or `SMTP_USER`") is **false**.
- The required env var for owner email is now `RESEND_API_KEY`, not `SMTP_USER`/`SMTP_PASS`. Gotcha #23 ("`SMTP_USER`/`SMTP_PASS` missing = silent email failure") and #24 ("Gmail SMTP requires App Password") are **stale** — the SMTP transporter is dead code (`getTransporter` is never called).
- The Section 12 split ("nodemailer for owner notifications; Resend for voicemail alerts") is wrong: **Resend is used for both.** Voicemail alert in the voice webhook also uses Resend (`app/api/webhooks/voice/route.ts:39,730,748-749`, `from: 'notifications@alignandacquire.com'`).

### B. `Contact.isClientContact` + known-contact voicemail routing (entire feature undocumented)

CLAUDE.md's `Contact` model (Section 4) and gotcha #8 describe SMS-skip logic keyed purely on `source IS NULL`. The schema has since gained a **new boolean column `isClientContact`** (`prisma/schema.prisma:154`) and the `Business` model a **new flag `knownContactVoicemailEnabled`** (`prisma/schema.prisma:70`). None of this is in CLAUDE.md.

- `isExistingContact()` **still** queries `source: null` (`lib/contacts-check.ts:17-22`) — that part of gotcha #8 is still accurate.
- A **new function `isClientVoicemailContact(businessId, callerPhone)`** (`lib/contacts-check.ts:36-51`) queries `where: { businessId, isClientContact: true }` using `phonesMatch` (last-10). This is a *separate* gate used for call routing, not SMS.
- The voice webhook references `business.knownContactVoicemailEnabled && isClientVoicemailContact(...)` in **9 distinct branches** (`app/api/webhooks/voice/route.ts:155, 330, 359, 408, 514, 547, 868`). When on, missed calls from the client's own saved contacts go to **voicemail instead of AI SMS** — a call-flow variant entirely absent from Section 5.
- A backfill script exists: `scripts/backfill-isClientContact.ts` (maps `source IS NULL`, `*_import` sources, and `manual`/`manual_list` → `isClientContact=true`; dry-run by default, `--apply` to commit). Raw SQL migrations live in `scripts/sql/2026-06-02_add_isClientContact.sql` and `scripts/sql/2026-06-02_add_knownContactVoicemailEnabled.sql`. Neither scripts nor these SQL files are mentioned in CLAUDE.md (which claims migrations are Prisma-only).

### C. New `notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead` function (undocumented)

Section 8 lists only four `notify-owner.ts` exports. There is a **fifth**: `notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead(business, { name, phone?, email?, message? }): Promise<NotifyOwnerResult>` (`lib/notify-owner.ts:532-660`). It sends an SMS (`📩 New website lead!`) and a **hand-built HTML email** (not the plain-text-wrapped path) with `escapeHtml()` on all fields, plus a plain-text fallback. This is the function the `/api/contact` route uses for client-tenant submissions.

### D. Dashboard navigation — substantially restructured (Section 9 "dashboard shell" is stale)

Actual nav is built in `app/(dashboard)/layout.tsx:7-65` (`getNavigation`). CLAUDE.md's nav list is wrong in several ways:

| CLAUDE.md claim | Reality (`layout.tsx`) |
|---|---|
| Always: Overview, **Conversations**, Outreach, Analytics, Contacts, **Jobs**, Settings | Always: Overview, **Leads** (`/dashboard/leads`, icon `Mailbox`), Outreach, Analytics, Contacts, Jobs, Settings (`:22-24,32-33,53-56,62`) |
| `hasMissedCallAi`: **Website Leads**, Scheduled Quotes | `hasMissedCallAi`: **Conversations** (`:28-30`) + **Scheduled Quotes** (`:35-37`). There is **no "Website Leads" nav item** — it was merged into "Leads". |
| `!hasMissedCallAi`: Voicemails | Same, but **also** a new branch: `hasMissedCallAi && knownContactVoicemailEnabled` → nav item **"Voicemails from Contacts"** (`:40-46`) |
| Blocked Calls only for screening-only (`!hasMissedCallAi && hasAnyScreening`) | Now shown for **any** client with `hasAnyScreening`, labelled **"Screened Calls"** (`:49-51`) — the `!hasMissedCallAi` restriction was removed |

### E. New `/dashboard/leads` page replaces standalone Website Leads (Conversations is back)

- `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/leads/page.tsx` + `LeadsClient.tsx` + `CombinedLeadsList.tsx` are **new and undocumented**. `CombinedLeadsList` fetches **both** `/api/dashboard/conversations` and `/api/dashboard/website-leads` and merges them client-side (`CombinedLeadsList.tsx:101-102`). There is **no `/api/dashboard/leads` route** (the page composes existing endpoints).
- `/dashboard/website-leads` **now redirects** to `/dashboard/leads?tab=website` (`website-leads/page.tsx:6`). CLAUDE.md still describes it as a live FeatureGate'd page with a `WebsiteLeadsClient`. (`WebsiteLeadsClient.tsx` still exists on disk but the page is a redirect.)
- Conversations remains a standalone page (`/dashboard/conversations`), consistent with CLAUDE.md's claim that it's "its own page again."

### F. `FeatureGate` prop API changed — CLAUDE.md prop names are all wrong

CLAUDE.md (Section 9) documents props `mode`, `feature`, `businessName?`, `setupHref?`, `setupLabel?`, and says it's a server component with no state. Actual signature (`app/components/FeatureGate.tsx:3-23`):

```ts
type LockedProps     = { mode: 'locked';      feature; valueProp;        businessName }
type NeedsSetupProps = { mode: 'needs-setup'; feature; setupDescription; setupLabel; setupHref }
type FeatureGateProps = (LockedProps | NeedsSetupProps) & { enabled: boolean; children }
```

Drifts: a **required `enabled: boolean`** prop now drives the gate (`if (enabled) return children`); `valueProp` replaces the old description for locked mode; `setupDescription` replaces it for needs-setup; `businessName` is **required** (not optional) in locked mode. The component has **no `'use client'`** and no hooks (that part is accurate). The mailto target is `jacob@alignandacquire.com` (subject `Unlock {feature} for {businessName}`).

### J. Things CLAUDE.md describes that have CHANGED or no longer match
