## Appendix A — Consolidated Drift Notes

Every discrepancy each agent flagged between the **actual code** and the legacy `CLAUDE.md`. 238 total. Grouped by the subsystem that reported it.

### Database Schema (Prisma) (8)

- NEW FIELD not in CLAUDE.md: Business.knownContactVoicemailEnabled (Boolean @default(false)). Added via scripts/sql/2026-06-02_add_knownContactVoicemailEnabled.sql. When true, missed calls from contacts with isClientContact=true route to voicemail instead of AI SMS. Read in app/api/webhooks/voice/route.ts at 8 sites and gates the dashboard Voicemails tab.
- NEW FIELD not in CLAUDE.md: Contact.isClientContact (Boolean @default(false)). Added via scripts/sql/2026-06-02_add_isClientContact.sql. This SUPERSEDES the CLAUDE.md-documented 'source IS NULL = existing contact' mechanism. lib/contacts-check.ts now queries isClientContact:true (not source IS NULL). CLAUDE.md Gotcha #8 and the Contact 'CRITICAL' note about source=null blocking SMS are STALE.
- Conversation.status: the schema's own inline comment (line 244) omits several status values the code actually writes. CLAUDE.md lists more values than the schema comment, but neither matches code exactly. Confirmed code-written values include screening_blocked, forwarding, and lead_captured in addition to the schema-comment list (active, booking_in_progress, appointment_booked, closed, human_needed, completed, no_response, screening). CLAUDE.md additionally lists needs_review — that value was NOT found written anywhere in app/ or lib/.
- TelnyxUsageRecord.recordType: schema comment says only 'sms | call'. CLAUDE.md says 'sms | call | call_forwarding'. Actual code writes/reads four values: 'sms', 'messaging', 'call', 'call_forwarding' (lib/telnyx-usage-sync.ts:419 writes call_forwarding; 'messaging' also written). CLAUDE.md is missing 'messaging'.
- No prisma/migrations/ directory exists — schema is synced via `prisma db push`, not migrations. CLAUDE.md's directory tree implies a managed prisma/ folder but does not document the db-push workflow or the scripts/sql/ raw-SQL review files.
- Business has an explicit @@index([stripeCustomerId]) that is redundant with the field's @unique constraint — not mentioned in CLAUDE.md (minor).
- Contact.updatedAt carries BOTH @default(now()) AND @updatedAt (schema.prisma:158); CLAUDE.md shows it only as `updatedAt DateTime @default(now()) @updatedAt` — matches, but worth noting the double annotation is intentional and unusual.
- Appointment→Conversation relation is onDelete: SetNull (schema.prisma:314); CLAUDE.md's multi-tenant section claims 'All models use onDelete: Cascade from Business' which is true for the Business FK but omits that the Appointment.conversation FK is SetNull, and that PhoneNumber has no FK to Business at all (assignedToBusinessId is a plain nullable String, not a relation).

### Voice / Telnyx Call Control Flow (12)

- NEW SUBSYSTEM (undocumented): known-contact voicemail routing. New Business field `knownContactVoicemailEnabled Boolean @default(false)` (schema:70) and Contact field `isClientContact Boolean @default(false)` (schema:154). New lib helper `isClientVoicemailContact(businessId, callerPhone)` in lib/contacts-check.ts queries Contact WHERE isClientContact=true. New voice/route.ts helper `startClientContactVoicemail()`. When enabled, missed calls from the client's own saved contacts go to voicemail instead of AI SMS — checked at call.initiated (no-screener path), gather.ended (both forwarding and standard paths), every forwarding-fallback fork, and handleForwardingFallback.
- NEW ClientState field `voicemailReason?: string` (value 'client_contact') — 10th field, not in CLAUDE.md's ClientState list. It is threaded through startRecording's client_state and read in call.recording.saved to decide owner notification.
- CHANGED behavior: voicemail owner-notification now fires when `!missedCallAiEnabled || voicemailReason === 'client_contact'` (route.ts:703). CLAUDE.md states voicemail notifications are sent only when missedCallAiEnabled == false. Known-contact voicemails now notify the owner even with AI SMS enabled.
- SCHEMA COMMENT CONTRADICTS CODE: prisma/schema.prisma:154 says isClientContact is 'Written & displayed only; NOT read during call routing.' but isClientVoicemailContact() reads exactly this field and is called throughout the voice webhook's routing logic.
- voice/route.ts call.answered B-leg contact lookup uses ANY Contact (no source filter) matched via phonesMatch to produce the 'Connecting to {name}' whisper. CLAUDE.md's B-leg description only says 'look up contact by callerPhone' without noting it loads all contacts and matches by normalized last-10.
- voice-dial-status/route.ts: `answeredBy.includes('unknown')` is treated as machine (triggers the duration>=2 SMS path), and no-answer/busy/failed have NO duration gate. CLAUDE.md/comment only mentions 'machine'. Also `dialCallStatus` is read from `payload.state ?? payload.hangup_cause`.
- isSpamCall toll-free list in code is exactly ['+1833','+1844','+1855','+1866','+1877','+1888','+1800'] (route.ts:1103) — matches CLAUDE.md's set; documenting exact order for ground truth.
- gatherUsingSpeak uses timeout_millis: 8000 and valid_digits: '0123456789' (route.ts:144-152). CLAUDE.md Flow-2 says timeout=8000ms which matches, but does not document valid_digits or minimum_digits/maximum_digits = 1.
- BlockedNumber guard in sendMissedCallSMS uses an EXACT phoneNumber equality match (db.blockedNumber.findFirst where phoneNumber: callerPhone), NOT a normalized phonesMatch — unlike the contact/cooldown checks. A blocked number stored in a different format than the inbound `from` would not match.
- startRecording config is { format:'mp3', channels:'single', max_length:120, timeout_secs:5, play_beep:true } (route.ts:289-308). CLAUDE.md omits channels:'single'.
- Voicemail email uses Resend (from notifications@alignandacquire.com) directly in the voice webhook; SMS voicemail notification is sent via telnyx.messages.send with a hardcoded dashboard URL https://www.alignandacquire.com/dashboard/voicemails (not NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL).
- The three legacy handlers (voice-gather, voice-after-dial, voice-dial-status) are NOT wired into the primary Call Control flow in route.ts — route.ts never configures callback URLs pointing to them. They are alternate/legacy XML-mode endpoints. voice-gather and voice-dial-status use a DIFFERENT default greeting string ('Hi! Sorry we missed your call at {name}. I'm an automated assistant - how can I help you today?') than route.ts's sendMissedCallSMS ('Sorry we missed your call at {name}. How can we help?').

### End-to-End Flow & Architecture (13)

- notify-owner.ts: ALL owner emails are sent via Resend (`notifications@alignandacquire.com`) through `sendEmail()` (line 696-714), NOT via nodemailer/Gmail SMTP. The nodemailer `getTransporter()` singleton (line 662-691) still exists but is dead code — never called. CLAUDE.md Section 8/12 says owner notifications use nodemailer/SMTP and that voicemail Resend is the only Resend path; in reality SMTP env vars are unused for notifications.
- New `notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead()` function in lib/notify-owner.ts (line 532-660) — sends SMS + a hand-built HTML Resend email for website contact-form submissions. Not documented in CLAUDE.md's 'four scenarios' list (there are now five+ notifiers).
- New Business field `knownContactVoicemailEnabled Boolean @default(false)` (schema.prisma:70) and a full known-contact voicemail routing path in voice/route.ts (startClientContactVoicemail at line 929; isClientVoicemailContact checks woven into every missed-call fork at :154,:329,:358,:407,:513,:546,:867). When on, calls from the client's own saved contacts (Contact.isClientContact=true) go to voicemail instead of AI SMS, and the owner is alerted via Resend even when missedCallAiEnabled is true. Entirely undocumented in CLAUDE.md.
- New Contact field `isClientContact Boolean @default(false)` (schema.prisma:154) and new helper `isClientVoicemailContact()` in lib/contacts-check.ts (line 36-51). Separate from `isExistingContact()` (which gates SMS on source IS NULL). Not in CLAUDE.md's Contact model or contacts-check docs.
- DEFAULT_MAX_MESSAGES_PER_CONVERSATION is 25 in code (sms/route.ts:91), but CLAUDE.md and the Conversation flow doc say the default is 23. The DB column default IS 23 (schema.prisma:79); the in-code fallback constant when the column is null is 25 — a real mismatch.
- Voicemail recording params drifted: startRecording uses max_length=120, timeout_secs=5, play_beep=true (voice/route.ts:289) — matches CLAUDE.md — but the recording handler reads `payload.recording_urls ?? payload.public_recording_urls` (voice:656), and the owner-notification block now fires for BOTH `!missedCallAiEnabled` AND `voicemailReason === 'client_contact'` (voice:703), not only the AI-disabled case CLAUDE.md describes.
- `sendMissedCallSMS` conversation lookback window is 24h (voice/route.ts:1012), not the value implied elsewhere; and the duplicate-SMS idempotency check is 'any outbound Message exists for this conversation' (voice:1024), consistent with CLAUDE.md but worth noting it is a per-conversation outbound check, not per-call.
- AI model is `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` (sms/route.ts:1033, 2286, 2439) with max_tokens 256 and 3x retry on HTTP 529 (3000ms delay) for booking-mode only. CLAUDE.md says model `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` (correct) but does not document the 529 retry loop or that lead-mode/extraction calls do NOT retry.
- Booking state machine has more steps than CLAUDE.md's 6-step table: actual BookingFlowState.step union (sms:752) includes greeting, awaiting_name, awaiting_name_and_preference, awaiting_service, awaiting_notes, awaiting_address, awaiting_time, awaiting_confirmation, awaiting_name_and_address, awaiting_name_after_slot, awaiting_address_after_slot, confirmed. The flow asks name and address in SEPARATE messages (awaiting_name_after_slot → awaiting_address_after_slot) and never shows numbered slot lists (it checks the exact requested time via isSpecificSlotAvailable and offers getTwoClosestSlotsOnDay alternatives). CLAUDE.md's 'show_slots / send 2-3 options' description is outdated.
- SMS handler has AI-booking safety nets not in CLAUDE.md: fake-confirmation interception (sms:494-523) and AI-guiding-to-booking detection (sms:525-553) that set bookingFlowState='awaiting_name_and_preference' / status='booking_in_progress'; plus a lead-capture BACKSTOP (sms:656-727) that captures a lead when the AI omitted [READY_TO_CAPTURE] (fires at >=2 inbound msgs with address or timeframe, notify-before-flip semantics).
- The `[APPOINTMENT_BOOKED:...]` AI tag is ONLY honored when calendar is NOT connected (`canAiBook = !calendarEnabled || !googleCalendarConnected`, sms:570). When calendar is connected the structured flow is the only booking path and the AI is explicitly instructed never to emit the tag. CLAUDE.md frames [APPOINTMENT_BOOKED] as a general 'legacy AI-side booking' path without this gate.
- Conversation status set `NO_AI_RESPONSE_STATUSES` (sms:233) includes `needs_review`, and schema comment lists `no_response`/`screening`, but no code path actually SETS needs_review or no_response — they are dead enum values in the current webhooks.
- Forwarding-only-without-screener: in the current no-screener branch (voice:153-174) a call with a forwardingNumber but no screener does NOT dial the B-leg — it goes straight to sendMissedCallSMS or voicemail. Actual B-leg dialing only happens inside the screener-passed (digit=1) path and the forwardingPending speak.ended fallback. CLAUDE.md's Flow descriptions imply forwarding works independently of the screener.

### SMS Webhook Inbound Pipeline, Delivery Status, and Send Helpers (19)

- DEFAULT_MAX_MESSAGES guard mismatch: the code constant DEFAULT_MAX_MESSAGES_PER_CONVERSATION is 25 (route.ts:91), but Prisma's Business.maxMessagesPerConversation default is 23 (schema.prisma:79). The route only falls back to 25 when the field is null/undefined; for normal businesses the effective limit is 23. CLAUDE.md says 'default 23' in some places and '23' in the conversation pipeline section, but the in-code fallback constant is 25, not 23 as CLAUDE.md's comment at the constant would imply.
- STOP/opt-out is an EXACT-match check, not a contains-match. route.ts:177-178 requires text.toLowerCase().trim() to exactly equal one of ['stop','unsubscribe','cancel','quit']. CLAUDE.md lists 'STOP, UNSUBSCRIBE, CANCEL, QUIT' but does not state it is exact-equality — e.g. 'please stop' or 'STOP texting me' does NOT trigger the opt-out path; it falls through to normal AI processing. The opt-out reply is 'You\'ve been unsubscribed. Reply START to resubscribe.' and there is NO actual suppression list write — START is never handled anywhere in this file.
- 'never mind'/'not interested' uses CONTAINS matching against ['never mind','nevermind','not interested','no thanks','no thank you'] (route.ts:185-186), and on match it: saves the inbound msg, sends a goodbye, sets conversation.status='closed' with summary='Customer said not interested', AND calls notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured() with service='Partial interest - customer declined'. CLAUDE.md says 'goodbye + notify owner as partial lead' but omits that it reuses notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured (not a dedicated function) and force-closes the conversation.
- Empty/blank inbound guard is undocumented: route.ts:227-230 returns 200 OK without saving when text is empty/whitespace (e.g. MMS with no text body), to avoid blank bubbles and to prevent Anthropic 400s on empty content blocks. Not in CLAUDE.md's pipeline steps.
- NO_AI_RESPONSE_STATUSES is ['appointment_booked','lead_captured','closed','human_needed','needs_review','completed'] (route.ts:233) and the lookback window is 90 days (closedWindowDays=90). CLAUDE.md section 6 step 4 says '90 days' and lists similar statuses but the actual early-return behavior differs by status: appointment_booked answers appointment questions then sets status='closed'; lead_captured sends a warm pleasantry/handoff then sets status='closed' and never re-notifies owner. These per-status branches are richer than CLAUDE.md describes.
- Spam/duplicate guard is 30s (SPAM_WINDOW_SECONDS=30, route.ts:115,329) and matches on identical inbound content within the window — consistent with CLAUDE.md's '30s'.
- Routing decision differs from CLAUDE.md's flat list. Actual order after saving inbound: (a) if !calendarEnabled || !smsBookingEnabled → handleSmsLeadFlow (which only returns true to short-circuit when status==='lead_captured'; otherwise returns false and falls through to AI); (b) handleSmsBookingFlow (the booking state machine); (c) if booking returned false → generateAIResponse. So lead 'flow' is NOT a separate conversational handler — lead capture is done by the general AI (generateAIResponse with calendarEnabled=false using a distinct lead-only system prompt) plus post-processing for [READY_TO_CAPTURE] and a backstop. CLAUDE.md's description of handleSmsLeadFlow as a 'conversational lead capture' function is inaccurate; the real logic lives in generateAIResponse + POST post-processing.
- generateAIResponse model is 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001' (route.ts:1033,1286,2439) — matches CLAUDE.md ('claude-haiku-4-5-20251001'). Note: CLAUDE.md section 5 header still claims the SMS AI uses Claude generally; the exact id is haiku-4-5.
- generateAIResponse has retry logic NOT documented: 3 attempts with 3000ms delay, but ONLY retries on HTTP 529 (overloaded). The lead-only branch (calendarEnabled=false) does NOT retry at all — single attempt, returns aiFailed on any error. CLAUDE.md mentions '503, overload' fallback but the code keys on 529 specifically and only in the booking-mode path.
- AI [APPOINTMENT_BOOKED:] tag is honored ONLY when canAiBook = (!calendarEnabled || !googleCalendarConnected) (route.ts:570). When calendar is connected the tag is stripped and ignored; the structured flow is the only booking path. The regex requires named fields in a fixed order: name, service, datetime, optional notes, optional address (route.ts:567-568). CLAUDE.md step-4 'legacy path: parse datetime, call createBooking(allowWithoutCalendar=true)' is correct but omits the canAiBook gate.
- Two AI-side safety nets exist for calendar-enabled businesses that CLAUDE.md does not document (route.ts:486-555): SCENARIO 1 intercepts AI 'fake confirmations' (regex on 'you're all set', 'booked for', etc.) and redirects into the structured flow with step='awaiting_name_and_preference'; SCENARIO 2 detects when the AI asked for BOTH name and time and pre-arms bookingFlowState for the next message. Both set status='booking_in_progress'.
- A lead-capture BACKSTOP exists (route.ts:648-727): when calendar disabled, no [READY_TO_CAPTURE], no [HUMAN_NEEDED], status not already captured, and inboundCount>=2, it calls extractLeadFromConversation and — if an address OR timeframe was gathered — notifies owner, flips status to 'lead_captured', upserts the contact, and sends buildLeadConfirmation. Critically, status is only flipped AFTER a successful notify (failed notify leaves status 'active' for retry). Undocumented in CLAUDE.md.
- extractLeadFromConversation is an extra Anthropic call (model claude-haiku-4-5-20251001, max_tokens 256) that returns structured JSON {customerName(required, defaults 'Customer'), customerEmail, customerAddress, customerService, customerTimeframe}. Used by both the [READY_TO_CAPTURE] path and the backstop. Undocumented.
- Delivery-status update handles BOTH 'message.finalized' AND 'message.sent' event types (route.ts:148), reading status from payload.to[0].status and updateMany on Message.telnyxSid. CLAUDE.md section 6 says 'message.finalized / message.sent' so this is consistent, but the field updated is telnyxStatus via updateMany (not a single update).
- sendSMSAndLog writes Message with BOTH telnyxSid AND telnyxStatus (initial status from Telnyx send response, default 'sent') in the deferred create — CLAUDE.md's Message-table description and section 6 only mention it creates a Message record; it also seeds telnyxStatus. The DB write + recordMessageSent are deferred via void .then().catch() (non-awaited), matching gotcha #10.
- sendSMS (no-log variant) is used for STOP opt-out, the message-limit cutoff, but NOT consistently — the 'never mind' path uses sendSMSAndLog (logs a Message), contradicting CLAUDE.md's claim that sendSMS-without-log is used for 'STOP/unsubscribe and message-limit cut-offs only'. The never-mind goodbye IS logged.
- Code references business.website (route.ts:2251,2401, '(business as any).website') but no 'website' field exists on the Business model in schema.prisma — this is always undefined and the prompt line never renders. Dead reference.
- On new-conversation creation (route.ts:303-314) a background findOrCreateContact({source:'missed_call'}) fires-and-forgets; CLAUDE.md mentions this but note it is keyed to creating the contact only when NO prior conversation existed in the 90-day window.
- maxMessages cutoff is exempted during active booking flow via inBookingFlow = Boolean(bookingFlowState?.step) (route.ts:339-340), consistent with gotcha #18. On cutoff: status='completed', summary='Conversation ended - message limit reached', sends a 'please call us' message via sendSMS (no log).

### SMS AI Brain — Prompt Construction, Lead Capture, Booking State Machine & Special Tags (18)

- MODEL: CLAUDE.md says the AI model is `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001` — this is CORRECT (matches sms/route.ts:2286, 2439, 1033). (CLAUDE.md Section 6 also incorrectly summarizes it once as the SMS model; no drift, just confirming.)
- max_tokens / temperature: Every Anthropic call uses `max_tokens: 256` and NO `temperature` param. CLAUDE.md never documents these values.
- DEFAULT_MAX_MESSAGES_PER_CONVERSATION is 25 in code (sms/route.ts:91), but CLAUDE.md repeatedly states the default is 23 (schema `maxMessagesPerConversation @default(23)` and Section 6 step 6). The fallback constant when the business field is null is 25, not 23.
- [LEAD_CAPTURED:...] tag is NOT parsed or acted on anywhere — it is only stripped from output (sms/route.ts:424) and the code comment at line 624 explicitly states 'no AI [LEAD_CAPTURED] path'. CLAUDE.md documents [LEAD_CAPTURED: name=... service=... email=... address=... timeframe=...] as an active special tag.
- [APPOINTMENT_BOOKED] field ORDER differs: actual regex is `name="..", service="..", datetime="..", notes?, address?` (sms/route.ts:568). CLAUDE.md documents the order as `datetime=".." service=".." name=".." address=".."`.
- [APPOINTMENT_BOOKED] is only honored when `canAiBook = !calendarEnabled || !googleCalendarConnected` (sms/route.ts:570). When the calendar IS connected, the tag is ignored and the structured flow is the only booking path. CLAUDE.md calls this a 'legacy AI-side booking' path without noting it is hard-disabled for calendar-connected businesses.
- Lead capture is NOT performed by handleSmsLeadFlow(). That function (sms/route.ts:1058) is a pure gate returning true only when already lead_captured. Actual capture happens inline in the POST handler via [READY_TO_CAPTURE] (line 430) and a separate backstop (line 648). CLAUDE.md describes handleSmsLeadFlow as the function that does field extraction and notifies the owner.
- A separate AI call `extractLeadFromConversation()` (claude-haiku-4-5-20251001, max_tokens 256, JSON-extraction prompt) is used to pull name/email/address/service/timeframe — CLAUDE.md describes extraction as happening 'from the conversation' but does not document this second Claude call.
- BACKSTOP lead capture (sms/route.ts:648): captures a lead when the AI forgot [READY_TO_CAPTURE], gated on inboundCount>=2 and (hasAddress OR hasTimeframe), with a notify-before-flip retry guard. Not documented in CLAUDE.md.
- Calendar 'fake confirmation' interceptors (SCENARIO 1 at sms/route.ts:494 and SCENARIO 2 at 525): when the AI claims a booking it didn't make, the response is suppressed/redirected and bookingFlowState.step is forced to 'awaiting_name_and_preference'. Entire safety-net mechanism is undocumented.
- Booking state machine has MANY more steps than CLAUDE.md's 6-step table. Actual steps: greeting, awaiting_name, awaiting_name_and_preference, awaiting_service, awaiting_notes, awaiting_address, awaiting_time, awaiting_confirmation, awaiting_name_and_address, awaiting_name_after_slot, awaiting_address_after_slot, confirmed. CLAUDE.md lists only ask_service/ask_date/show_slots/confirm_slot/confirmed (plus detect) — step names 'show_slots'/'confirm_slot' do not exist in code.
- The booking flow does NOT send numbered slot lists / '2-3 options' (CLAUDE.md 'show_slots' step). The current design checks the customer's EXACT requested time via isSpecificSlotAvailable and offers at most 1-2 closest alternatives (getTwoClosestSlotsOnDay). The prompt explicitly forbids numbered slot lists.
- The general/booking system prompt injects `business.website` (`(business as any).website`) into BUSINESS INFO (sms/route.ts:2251, 2401). `website` is not a documented Business field in CLAUDE.md Section 4.
- Lead-mode prompt instructs 'Do NOT ask for email. Ever.' yet extractLeadFromConversation and lead-capture writes still populate customerEmail. CLAUDE.md's lead-flow description lists email as a captured field without noting the AI is told never to ask for it.
- Booking mode does a 3-attempt retry loop on HTTP 529 (overload) with 3s delay before falling back. CLAUDE.md mentions handling '503, overload' generically but the actual retry trigger is 529 only, and only in booking mode (lead mode has no retry).
- 'never mind'/'not interested' handling notifies the owner as a partial-interest lead via notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured with service='Partial interest - customer declined' and sets status='closed' (sms/route.ts:186-221). CLAUDE.md mentions 'notify owner as partial lead' but not the closed status or the exact service label.
- Conversation lookback window for finding an existing conversation is 90 days (closedWindowDays), and an existing conversation in ANY of the 6 NO_AI statuses is reused (never a new conversation created) — with appointment_booked/lead_captured straggler handlers that auto-close. CLAUDE.md says 'look back 90 days' but does not document the straggler auto-close-to-'closed' behavior.
- generateAIResponse signature takes (business, conversation, latestMessage, bookingFlowState?, calendarEnabled?) and IGNORES latestMessage for building the messages array (history is rebuilt from conversation.messages). CLAUDE.md does not document the signature.

### Booking Creation & Google Calendar (19)

- app/api/bookings/[id]/route.ts has ONLY a DELETE handler (permanent hard-delete of an appointment, with Google Calendar event cleanup). CLAUDE.md Section 7 documents it as 'GET: appointment detail' — there is no GET handler on this route at all.
- /api/bookings/delete-past is a POST handler (not DELETE as CLAUDE.md states) and deletes ALL past/cancelled/completed appointments — there is NO 90-day age filter (CLAUDE.md says 'Remove appointments older than 90 days').
- createBooking's duplicate-booking detection window is ±slotDurationMinutes (business.slotDurationMinutes ?? 30), not the ±60 minutes implied by CLAUDE.md gotcha #11 (60s tolerance applies only to the Google slot-verification step, which is a separate check).
- createBooking now runs a DB-level time-overlap check (4h lookback window) that fires REGARDLESS of skipSlotVerification, returning 409 'This time slot was just booked. Please pick a different time.' — undocumented in CLAUDE.md.
- getAvailableSlotsInternal merges confirmed/non-cancelled DB appointments into the Google freebusy 'busy' list as a safety net so booked slots stay blocked even when Google Calendar is unreachable or the OAuth token is revoked — undocumented.
- getValidAccessToken sets googleCalendarConnected=false on the Business row and RE-THROWS when the OAuth refresh fails (CLAUDE.md describes it only as 'refreshing via refresh token if expired' and the signature is `Promise<string | null>`, not the documented `Promise<string>`).
- isSpecificSlotAvailable returns `TimeSlot | null` (the matching slot), not a boolean as CLAUDE.md's signature states.
- getAvailableSlots signature accepts `Date | string` for start/end (CLAUDE.md shows only string params); getAvailableSlotsWithMeta returns `noMoreAvailabilityToday?` as optional.
- getAuthUrl's parameter is named `state` (the caller passes businessId as the OAuth state), not `businessId` as CLAUDE.md's signature `getAuthUrl(businessId: string)` implies.
- exchangeCodeForTokens returns the Google tokens object (`Promise<Tokens>`), not `Promise<void>` as documented; it also conditionally preserves an existing refresh token when Google omits a new one.
- getAvailableSlotsWithDebug is exported but has zero callers in the codebase (dead export). getAvailableSlotsWithMeta and getAvailableSlots are the live ones.
- GET /api/appointments is side-effecting: it auto-completes past confirmed appointments (status='completed'), and cancels confirmed appointments whose Google event no longer exists — but ONLY for appointments created more than 1 hour ago (grace window). CLAUDE.md does not mention the completion pass or the 1h grace.
- createCalendarEvent event summary uses emoji prefixes (📱 for sms, 🌐 for website) and returns `string | null` (null when no calendar client), not a guaranteed eventId as CLAUDE.md states ('Returns eventId. Throws on failure.').
- createMarketingCalendarEvent signature is (businessId, start, end, customerName, options) where options is CreateMarketingCalendarEventOptions including customerPhone/businessName/servicesInterested/serviceType/message — richer than the CLAUDE.md `(businessId, start, end, customerName, options)` summary, and it adds 15-min popup+email reminders that the regular event does not.
- deleteCalendarEvent returns `Promise<boolean>` and calendarEventExists returns `Promise<boolean>` — CLAUDE.md lists deleteCalendarEvent as `Promise<void>`.
- GET /api/appointments returns 403 with body { error:'Feature not available' } (not the documented 'Feature not available' wording mismatch is minor, but the gate is on calendarEnabled only, not googleCalendarConnected).
- marketing-bookings POST sends NO confirmation SMS to the customer (it sends a Resend confirmation EMAIL to the customer instead); owner notification path is fully separate from notify-owner.ts (uses raw Resend fetch + Telnyx, env vars YOUR_EMAIL/OWNER_PHONE/MARKETING_TELNYX_NUMBER).
- POST /api/bookings/create requires `notes` for website bookings (conversationId absent) and requires customerAddress when business.bookingRequiresAddress — these required-field rules are website-only and not mentioned in CLAUDE.md's route table.
- available-slots returns calendarEnabled=false and 'Google Calendar not connected' cases as HTTP 200 (not error status), and includes extra response keys not in CLAUDE.md's documented shape: calendarEnabled, bookingRequiresAddress, today, timezone.

### Dashboard Read APIs (analytics, conversations, messages, voicemails, screened-calls, website-leads) (14)

- analytics: CLAUDE.md lists Period as today/week/month/all, but the code also supports 'year' (calendar-year-to-date with prior-year comparison) — getDateRanges() in analytics/route.ts:47-56.
- analytics: the response includes a misspelled duplicate key 'leadsCapured' alongside 'leadsCaptured' (both same value) — analytics/route.ts:280-281. Not documented.
- analytics: 'leadsCaptured' is computed as Contact.count where source IN ['missed_call','website_form'], NOT from Conversation status='lead_captured' as the conversation-flow docs imply.
- analytics: response contains several fields not documented in CLAUDE.md: voicemailsCount, previousTotalCalls, previousLeadsCaptured (null on period='all'), leadSources (with an 'imports' bucket aggregating 9 *_import source strings plus manual_list, and 'manual'/'referral'/'google_ad' buckets), and recentActivity (last 20 Activity rows with contact name/phone).
- analytics: date windows are computed in server local time via new Date(y,m,d), not business-timezone TZDate, contradicting the CLAUDE.md claim that all business-local conversions use TZDate.
- messages (list) route: CLAUDE.md documents query params page/search/status/limit, but app/api/dashboard/messages/route.ts accepts NO query params and does no pagination/filtering. It also filters out callerPhone == business.telnyxPhoneNumber and does an N+1 contact.findFirst per conversation; unreadCount is hardcoded 0.
- messages/send: CLAUDE.md says body is { conversationId, text }; actual body is { to, body, conversationId? } (SendBody type). 'to' is required and normalized to E.164; conversationId is optional and auto-resolved/created.
- messages/send: every send sets the conversation manualMode=true (disabling AI auto-reply), creates the Message synchronously (awaited, not the deferred-write pattern), and fire-and-forget findOrCreateContact with source='sms_conversation' (a source value not listed in the Contact.source enumeration in CLAUDE.md).
- messages/[conversationId]: response renames Message.content to 'body' and exposes telnyxStatus as 'status'; not documented.
- voicemails GET and screened-calls GET use raw auth()+db.user lookup+getBusinessForDashboard inline rather than requireDashboardBusiness() (functionally equivalent but not the documented helper).
- voicemails GET and screened-calls GET are NOT feature-gated server-side (no missedCallAiEnabled check), contrary to the implication that these are gated 'used when missedCallAiEnabled is false' features.
- voicemails/[id] DELETE soft-delete only nulls recordingUrl + voicemailTranscription on the Conversation; it does NOT delete the Vercel Blob mp3, leaving an orphaned blob in storage. CLAUDE.md describes the soft-delete but not the orphan.
- screened-calls GET supports a 'days' query param (default 30, clamped 1-90) with special start-of-today handling when days=1, and returns a stats object {blocked,passed,total,blockRate,days} + recentCalls (take 100) — none of this param/response shape is in CLAUDE.md.
- website-leads route has an undocumented PATCH handler that updates lead status (VALID_STATUSES = new/contacted/converted/closed). The PATCH is NOT feature-gated (only GET checks missedCallAiEnabled).

### Outreach: Bulk Email + SMS Campaigns + Image Upload (17)

- FeatureGate component signature is materially different from CLAUDE.md. Actual props (app/components/FeatureGate.tsx): a discriminated union on `mode` PLUS a required top-level `enabled: boolean`. Locked mode requires `feature`, `valueProp` (NOT `setupHref`/`setupLabel`), `businessName`. Needs-setup mode requires `feature`, `setupDescription` (NOT `setupLabel` alone), `setupLabel`, `setupHref`. CLAUDE.md documents props as `mode`, `feature`, `businessName?`, `setupHref?`, `setupLabel?` and says 'No state/hooks needed' — it omits the load-bearing `enabled` prop and renames `valueProp`->(implicit) and `setupDescription`->(missing). The gate renders children directly when `enabled` is true, and blurs+overlays when false; it does NOT gate on truthiness of a passed feature flag internally.
- outreach/page.tsx passes `enabled={business.massMessagingEnabled}` and `valueProp="Send targeted email and SMS campaigns to your contact list"` to FeatureGate — confirming the real prop names. CLAUDE.md's description of the outreach page gate ('FeatureGate locked on business.massMessagingEnabled') is directionally correct but omits the `enabled` prop mechanics and `valueProp`.
- The email send route (/api/dashboard/emails POST) accepts a `bodyIsHtml?: boolean` field in the request body that controls whether the body is sent as-is (true) or wrapped via plainTextToEmailHtml + image-injection (false). CLAUDE.md mentions plainTextToEmailHtml is 'only applied when bodyIsHtml === false' but does not document `bodyIsHtml` as a POST body field of /api/dashboard/emails.
- Email campaign send is fully SYNCHRONOUS and awaited inside the POST handler — it creates the campaign with status='sending', creates all EmailRecipient rows one-by-one (await in loop), sends every email via Resend in batches of 50 (BATCH_SIZE=50, but actually sends one-at-a-time within each batch via inner for-loop), then flips EmailRecipient.updateMany status='sent' and campaign status='sent' BEFORE returning. CLAUDE.md describes a draft->sending->sent transition but does not note that 'sending' is never observable to a client (the request blocks until 'sent') and that per-recipient status is always set to 'sent' regardless of individual Resend success/failure (Resend errors are caught per-batch and only console.error'd).
- EmailRecipient status is set to 'sent' for ALL recipients via updateMany after the send loop, even recipients whose Resend call threw (caught at batch level). There is no per-recipient delivered/bounced/failed tracking despite the schema supporting those values. CLAUDE.md lists EmailRecipient statuses (pending, sent, delivered, bounced, failed) but in practice only 'pending'->'sent' is ever written.
- Resend `from` address is `${campaign.senderName} <notifications@alignandacquire.com>` — the senderName is user-controlled (defaults to 'Align and Acquire'). CLAUDE.md's Resend section says voicemail emails send from notifications@alignandacquire.com but does not mention email CAMPAIGNS also send from that domain with a per-campaign display name.
- SMS campaign route (/api/dashboard/messages/campaign) does NOT interact with the SMS cooldown system (checkCooldown/recordMessageSent), does NOT check BlockedNumber, and does NOT call isExistingContact. It sends to every matched contact unconditionally. The audit prompt's hint about 'cooldown interaction' is moot — there is none. This is a notable gotcha not in CLAUDE.md: mass SMS campaigns bypass all the missed-call SMS guardrails.
- SMS campaign uses merge fields: `{{name}}` and `{{business_name}}` (case-insensitive, whitespace-tolerant regex), controlled by `mergeFields?: boolean` (default true). CLAUDE.md does not document merge fields at all.
- SMS campaign batching constants are BATCH_SIZE=10 and BATCH_DELAY_MS=2000 (10 messages per batch via Promise.all, then sleep 2s between batches). CLAUDE.md does not document these.
- SMS campaign per-recipient send creates/reuses a Conversation by callerPhone, sets manualMode=true on it, writes an outbound Message, and fire-and-forget findOrCreateContact(source='sms_conversation'). This means campaign sends mark conversations as manualMode (AI disabled). Not documented in CLAUDE.md.
- /api/dashboard/messages/send request body is `{ to, body, conversationId? }` (NOT `{ conversationId, text }` as CLAUDE.md states). It resolves/creates a conversation by `to` phone if no conversationId, sets manualMode=true, returns `{ conversationId, message: {id, body, direction, createdAt, status}, contactLabel: null }`. CLAUDE.md says 'Body: { conversationId, text }' which is wrong on both field names.
- Email recipient resolution for ALL selection types filters on `email: { not: null }`. The 'all' type selects all contacts with a non-null email. CLAUDE.md does not detail the email-not-null filtering on every branch.
- upload-image route: allowed types include 'image/jpg' (in addition to image/jpeg), max 5MB, filename sanitized via replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g,'_'), path `campaign-images/{campaignId}/{filename}` or `campaign-images/draft/{filename}`, access:'public', returns `{ url, filename }` where filename is the ORIGINAL file.name (not sanitized). Matches CLAUDE.md mostly; CLAUDE.md omits that the returned filename is the original unsanitized name while the blob path uses the sanitized name.
- EmailsClient only renders campaigns where status==='sent' and provides a 'Reuse as Template' link to /dashboard/emails/new?templateId={id}. There is no draft/sending UI. Matches CLAUDE.md. No drift here.
- OutreachClient default channel is 'email' (useState<Channel>('email')); SMS embeds MessagesClient (not a dedicated campaign-only view). The SMS campaign is launched via a modal button ('Send Campaign') inside MessagesClient. CLAUDE.md describes OutreachClient embedding both but doesn't note the campaign UI lives inside the MessagesClient modal.
- MessagesClient campaign modal recipient filter UI only switches the filter TYPE (all/tags/status/manual) but does NOT let the user pick specific tagIds/statuses/contactIds in the UI — it shows the note 'Advanced filters ... are configured via the API selection for now.' So in practice the SMS campaign from the UI almost always sends type:'all' (or an empty-array tags/status/manual which the route treats as no additional filter = all contacts). This is a significant functional gotcha not in CLAUDE.md.
- The email compose 'Reuse as Template' flow pre-fills only subject and body (from GET /api/dashboard/emails/[id]); senderName, images, and recipients are intentionally NOT pre-filled. Confirmed in EmailComposeClient. Matches CLAUDE.md.

### CRM, Contacts, Cooldown, Conversation Buckets & Phone Utils (17)

- Contact model gained an `isClientContact Boolean @default(false)` column not documented anywhere in CLAUDE.md (Section 4 Contact model omits it entirely).
- GET /api/dashboard/contacts now hard-filters `isClientContact: true` (client's own saved contacts only). CLAUDE.md describes the Contacts page as a general 'CRM contact list' with no such split; system-generated leads are now excluded and shown in a separate Leads area.
- `lib/contacts-check.ts` added a third exported function `isClientVoicemailContact(businessId, callerPhone)` (queries `isClientContact: true`), used ~10 times in the voice webhook for known-contact voicemail routing. Not in CLAUDE.md. It also contradicts the schema comment which says isClientContact is 'NOT read during call routing'.
- `findOrCreateContact` gained an `isClientContact?: boolean` param (CREATE-only; never upgrades/downgrades existing rows). CLAUDE.md's documented signature omits it.
- `POST /api/dashboard/contacts` sets `isClientContact: true` and creates a `manual` Activity; CLAUDE.md only documents body fields, not this flag/activity behavior. It uses direct db.contact.create (not findOrCreateContact), so a duplicate phone throws uncaught.
- `POST /api/dashboard/contacts/import` sends `isClientContact: true` for every imported contact and supports a `dryRun` mode returning {imported,duplicates,errors}; CLAUDE.md describes import as parseContactFile→findOrCreateContact with no dryRun and no isClientContact flag. Also note the real route receives an already-parsed `contacts[]` JSON array, not a raw file — parseContactFile runs in the import UI.
- `DELETE /api/dashboard/contacts/[id]` exists (hard delete, returns {ok:true}) but is not documented in CLAUDE.md (Section 7 lists only GET/PATCH for this path).
- `POST /api/dashboard/contacts/[id]/activities` exists (adds a `note_added` activity from {content}) but CLAUDE.md documents only GET for that path.
- BUCKET_LABELS in lib/conversation-buckets.ts now holds plain bucket names ('Cold/Active/Stalled/Closed'), NOT the client-facing labels. CLAUDE.md (Section 8) claims BUCKET_LABELS = cold→'No Reply', active→'In Progress', stalled→'Went Quiet', closed→'Closed' and says 'always use BUCKET_LABELS in UI'. Those client-facing labels now live in a local TAB_LABELS map inside ConversationsClient.tsx; BUCKET_LABELS is used by the admin conversations page and the Leads list, which therefore show 'Cold/Active/Stalled'.
- ConversationsClient.tsx now imports only `getConversationBucket` and `BUCKET_COLORS` from conversation-buckets (not BUCKET_LABELS), defining its own TAB_LABELS — CLAUDE.md implies it uses BUCKET_LABELS.
- New file/surface app/(dashboard)/dashboard/leads/CombinedLeadsList.tsx and app/admin/[businessId]/conversations/page.tsx consume the bucket helpers; the 'Leads area' concept (separate from Contacts) is not described in CLAUDE.md.
- `PATCH /api/dashboard/jobs/[id]` implements only PATCH — there is no DELETE handler in jobs/[id]/route.ts, but CLAUDE.md Section 7 lists 'PATCH/DELETE' for /api/dashboard/jobs/[id].
- import-contacts.ts PHONE_COLUMN_NAMES is broader than CLAUDE.md states (it includes 'telephone','contact','phonenumber','phone number' in addition to phone/mobile/cell/number) and has a fallback that scans up to 100 rows picking the column with >=50% phone-like values; CLAUDE.md only mentions header keyword detection.
- getCooldownDays ignores a business smsCooldownDays of 0 (requires > 0), so 0 cannot disable cooldown — a subtlety not captured in CLAUDE.md's 'business.smsCooldownDays > env > 7' description.
- findExistingContact email match is exact case-sensitive equality (db.contact.findFirst where email == trimmed), not a normalized/ILIKE match — CLAUDE.md just says 'email is fallback'.
- findOrCreateContact's skipUpdateIfExists branch returns source:null (hard-coded) rather than the existing contact's actual source.
- logContactSkip writes lastMessageSent: new Date(0) (epoch sentinel) for existing_contact skips; logCooldownSkip relies on the CooldownSkipLog.reason column default ('cooldown') rather than passing it — minor behavioral details absent from CLAUDE.md.

### Frontend Dashboard Shell & Pages (13)

- NEW UNDOCUMENTED SECTION: the entire `app/(dashboard)/dashboard/leads/` area (page.tsx, LeadsClient.tsx, CombinedLeadsList.tsx) does not exist in CLAUDE.md. 'Leads' is now the #2 nav item (always shown, icon `Mailbox`, href `/dashboard/leads`). It merges closed missed-call conversations (bucket==='closed') with website leads into one filterable list (all/missed_call/website); missed-call rows deep-link into Conversations via `?selected=<id>`, website rows open an in-page modal.
- NEW Business field `knownContactVoicemailEnabled` is used throughout the dashboard (layout nav, dashboard/page.tsx, OverviewClient, voicemails/page.tsx) but is not in CLAUDE.md's Business schema. It controls whether AI clients also see a 'Voicemails from Contacts' nav item/section.
- Nav item set changed vs CLAUDE.md's documented structure: CLAUDE.md lists 'Always: Overview, Conversations, Outreach, Analytics, Contacts, Jobs, Settings' and 'hasMissedCallAi: Website Leads, Scheduled Quotes'. Actual always-shown set is Overview, Leads, Outreach, Analytics, Contacts, Jobs, Settings. Conversations and Scheduled Quotes are gated on hasMissedCallAi. There is no separate 'Website Leads' nav item (folded into Leads). The screening nav item is labeled 'Screened Calls' (href /dashboard/blocked-calls) and is shown for ANY client with hasAnyScreening — NOT restricted to non-AI clients as CLAUDE.md states ('Blocked Calls ... screening-only clients only — NOT for AI clients').
- FeatureGate props differ from CLAUDE.md. Documented props: `mode, feature, businessName?, setupHref?, setupLabel?`. Actual discriminated union: locked mode requires `valueProp` and `businessName` (both required, no `businessName?` optional); needs-setup mode requires `setupDescription`, `setupLabel`, `setupHref`. All driven by a required `enabled` boolean. Mailto target is `jacob@alignandacquire.com` (not a generic address).
- SpamOnlyDashboard.tsx still exists but is DEAD CODE — never imported/rendered anywhere (grep confirms only its own definition). CLAUDE.md does not mention it at all, but more importantly the current dashboard/page.tsx has NO SpamOnlyDashboard-vs-OverviewClient branch; it always renders OverviewClient, which is itself feature-aware. Any handoff doc should not describe a SpamOnlyDashboard render path.
- getBusinessForDashboard now returns `{ business, isAdminViewAs }` and the layout destructures `isAdminViewAs`. CLAUDE.md documents the return as `{ business: Business | null }` only. The shell renders an admin 'Viewing as client' banner with an Exit link (`/api/admin/view-as?exit=1`) based on this flag.
- CLAUDE.md says the dashboard shell has a 'View as Client' admin toggle. The actual DashboardShellClient has NO entry toggle for view-as — it only shows an EXIT banner when already viewing as a client.
- OverviewClient/AnalyticsClient AnalyticsResponse carries a misspelled REQUIRED field `leadsCapured` (the canonical key the analytics API returns) alongside an optional corrected `leadsCaptured?`. Clients read `leadsCaptured ?? leadsCapured`. Not documented.
- website-leads/page.tsx is now a redirect to `/dashboard/leads?tab=website` (CLAUDE.md describes it as a live FeatureGate-wrapped WebsiteLeadsClient page). The WebsiteLeadsClient component still exists but the route no longer renders it directly.
- Overview's Total Calls / Calls Blocked / Calls Passed metric cards all link (href) to `/dashboard/blocked-calls`, and 'Website Leads' card links to `/dashboard/leads`. Overview now also renders a server-fed `callLog` 'Recent Activity' card merging ScreenedCall records and missed_call Activity rows — not described in CLAUDE.md's Overview section.
- voicemails/page.tsx gates via redirect to /dashboard (not a FeatureGate overlay); its gate is `!hasMissedCallAi || knownContactVoicemailEnabled`, extending voicemail visibility to AI clients with known-contact voicemail routing — beyond CLAUDE.md's '!hasMissedCallAi' description.
- AppointmentsClient has an internal header inconsistency: it renders `<h1>Appointments</h1>` (subtitle 'View and manage all booked appointments') in the loaded view while the loading state and nav label use 'Scheduled Quotes'.
- getBusinessFeatures (lib/business-features.ts) does NOT include `googleAds`, `knownContactVoicemailEnabled`, or `showVoicemails` in its returned BusinessFeatures type — those are layered on top by dashboard/page.tsx and the layout's nav function. CLAUDE.md's business-features doc omits that these extras are added by callers, not the helper.

### Auth, Middleware, Multi-Tenancy, Feature Flags, Config & Onboarding (14)

- lib/business-features.ts: BusinessFeatures type does NOT include a `googleAds` field. CLAUDE.md/Overview/Ads pages reference `googleAds: boolean`, but that lives on a page-local `Features` type, not on getBusinessFeatures' return.
- middleware.ts: isPublicApiRoute now includes `/api/contact` in addition to `/api/webhooks/(.*)`. CLAUDE.md's middleware section only lists `/api/webhooks/(.*)` as public API.
- lib/industry-defaults.ts is a NEW module not documented in CLAUDE.md: provides IndustryConfig type, BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS (['Landscaping / Lawn Care','Car Detailing / Auto Detailing','HVAC (Heating, Ventilation & Air Conditioning)','Other']), industryDefaults presets, getIndustryDefaults(), getAllIndustries(). Used by onboarding.
- businessType label values differ: industry-defaults uses long labels ('Landscaping / Lawn Care', etc.) while CLAUDE.md documents Business.businessType as 'Landscaping' | 'Car Detailing' | 'HVAC' | 'Other'.
- lib/business-hours.ts DEFAULT_BUSINESS_HOURS uses `{open,close}|null` (null for Sat/Sun), with NO `closed` boolean. CLAUDE.md documents `{open,close,closed:false}` objects with a `closed` flag for Sat/Sun.
- Onboarding stores businessHours as `{ description: <freeform string> }`, not the structured `{monday:{open,close,closed},...}` JSON the schema docs imply. Onboarding's aiContext/aiInstructions construction is undocumented.
- next.config.js images.remotePatterns allows only `https://img.clerk.com`. CLAUDE.md does not document image domains; also sets a global `Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors *` header alongside X-Frame-Options ALLOWALL.
- vercel.json (undocumented) defines a daily cron at 06:00 UTC hitting /api/admin/usage/sync — the real scheduled-job mechanism, not CRON_SECRET-gated.
- MetaPixel component (app/components/MetaPixel.tsx) and env var NEXT_PUBLIC_FACEBOOK_PIXEL_ID are undocumented; rendered in root layout, fires fbq PageView on route changes.
- Env vars referenced in code but absent from CLAUDE.md: GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL, GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_PRIVATE_KEY, GOOGLE_SHEET_ID (Sheets usage sync), NEXT_PUBLIC_FACEBOOK_PIXEL_ID, VERCEL_URL (notify-owner link fallback).
- Env vars in CLAUDE.md but NOT referenced anywhere in code: SMTP_FROM (so owner emails always send from SMTP_USER), TELNYX_PHONE_NUMBER (no global default-number fallback exists), TELNYX_PUBLIC_KEY (still no signature verification).
- exchangeCodeForTokens sets googleCalendarConnected via `!!(tokens.access_token && (tokens.refresh_token ?? true))` — connected whenever an access token exists, even if no refresh token is returned. Undocumented nuance.
- getBusinessForDashboard returns { business, isAdminViewAs } but requireDashboardBusiness discards isAdminViewAs; CLAUDE.md describes the cookie logic but not the dropped flag.
- Google calendar OAuth scope is the full `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar` (read/write), with access_type:'offline' and prompt:'consent' forcing a fresh refresh token each connect.

### Telnyx Usage/Cost Sync, Exports & Google Sheets Sync (10)

- lib/google-sheets-sync.ts is essentially undocumented in CLAUDE.md. The full Google Sheets integration exists: syncUsageToGoogleSheets(date), monthly-tab auto-creation (e.g. 'March 2026'), 12-column layout, append-vs-update logic, service-account auth. Three new env vars are NOT in CLAUDE.md's env table: GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL, GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_PRIVATE_KEY, GOOGLE_SHEET_ID.
- CLAUDE.md says syncTelnyxUsage signature is 'syncTelnyxUsage(dateRange?: { start: string; end: string })'. ACTUAL signature is syncTelnyxUsage(dateRange: string = 'last_90_days') — a single string preset (yesterday|last_7_days|last_30_days|last_90_days), NOT a {start,end} object. There is no per-date custom range support in the sync.
- CLAUDE.md describes the CDR fallback chain as 'call, call-control, sip-trunking, then Usage Reports API'. ACTUAL: that voice-type chain is correct, but EACH record type also has a SEPARATE date_range fallback (last_90_days -> last_30_days -> last_7_days) inside fetchWithFallback. The Usage Reports API fallback (fetchUsageReportsFallback) only LOGS aggregate cost to debugLog and is explicitly NOT stored per-business (account-level only) — it does not produce TelnyxUsageRecord rows. Also the whole voice CDR fetch is non-blocking: if it fails, MDR records are still saved and the sync returns successfully.
- CLAUDE.md's usage-export signature lists getUsageForExport returning dailyRows with fields { date, business, smsCount, smsTotal, callCount, callTotal }. ACTUAL DailyUsageRow fields are { date, businessId, businessName, phoneNumber, inboundSms, outboundSms, smsCost, inboundCalls, outboundCalls, callMinutes, voiceCost, totalCost } — far more granular and differently named. Same for BusinessSubtotal/grandTotal.
- CLAUDE.md's TelnyxUsageRecord model comment says recordType is '"sms" | "call" | "call_forwarding"'. The actual schema.prisma inline comment only says '"sms" | "call"' (omits call_forwarding), though the code does write recordType='call_forwarding' for B-leg forwarding records. metadata schema comment in schema.prisma ({ durationSeconds?, from?, to?, direction? }) does not match what code actually writes (cli, cld, direction, status, parts for SMS; cli, cld, direction, call_sec, billed_sec for calls).
- CLAUDE.md does not mention the export route's GET-only nature, its query params (dateRange/startDate/endDate/format), CSV-vs-XLSX dual output, or the multi-section sheet layout (daily rows + 'Per business subtotals' + grand total). It also under-documents that the XLSX export OMITS the 'Call Forwarding Cost' column entirely (folds it into Voice Cost) whereas the Google Sheets export has a dedicated 'Call Forwarding Cost' column.
- BUG / DRIFT: app/admin/AdminTools.tsx calls /api/admin/usage/export?preset=this_month, but the route reads url.searchParams.get('dateRange') and ignores 'preset'. It works by accident only because dateRange defaults to 'this_month'. CLAUDE.md documents the export query param as 'preset?' which matches the (broken) client, not the actual server which expects 'dateRange'.
- CLAUDE.md does not document that /api/admin/usage/sync supports BOTH GET and POST, nor that it has a CRON_SECRET bearer-token bypass path (isCron) which skips the ADMIN_USER_ID Clerk check and defaults dateRange to 'yesterday'. CLAUDE.md states CRON_SECRET is 'not currently enforced in code' — it IS enforced/used here. There is also a real vercel.json cron hitting /api/admin/usage/sync daily at 06:00 UTC (0 6 * * *), not mentioned in CLAUDE.md.
- CLAUDE.md does not document the /api/admin/usage/sheets-sync route at all in any detail (POST only, ?date=YYYY-MM-DD optional, defaults to yesterday, returns SheetsSyncResult).
- The per-business usage endpoint /api/admin/businesses/[id]/usage returns a much richer shape than CLAUDE.md implies ('Usage data for any business'). It includes sms (week/all-time), missedCallSmsTriggered, skips breakdown (cooldown/existingContact/blocked/total), a computed moneySaved heuristic (totalSkips * avgSmsCost, fallback 0.0079), cost breakdown (sms/voice/callForwarding x thisWeek/allTime), calls (count + minutes), skipLogs (last 50), and recentMessages (last 30 with per-message cost).

### Google Ads Integration (8)

- FeatureGate prop API drift: CLAUDE.md documents FeatureGate props as `mode`, `feature`, `businessName?`, `setupHref?`, `setupLabel?` with a mailto subject `Unlock {feature} for {businessName}`. The actual component (app/components/FeatureGate.tsx) uses a discriminated union requiring `enabled: boolean`, plus `valueProp` (locked mode) or `setupDescription`/`setupLabel`/`setupHref` (needs-setup mode). ads/page.tsx calls it with `enabled`, `valueProp`, `businessName` — not the documented shape.
- CLAUDE.md says the dashboard GET response's `lastSyncedAt` is 'the most recent createdAt across all snapshots in range' — true — but the GoogleAdsSnapshot model has NO updatedAt field and upsert does not touch createdAt, so re-syncing an existing (campaign,date) row never advances lastSyncedAt. lastSyncedAt reflects first-insert time, not last sync. This nuance is undocumented.
- CLAUDE.md describes the GET route's `groupBy` (day|campaign) as if it selects between daily and campaign groupings. In the actual code (app/api/dashboard/google-ads/route.ts:22) groupBy is read but never used — both `daily` and `campaigns` are always computed and returned.
- Admin sync route response key is `synced` in BOTH the single-business and all-business branches (app/api/admin/google-ads/sync/route.ts:27,33). CLAUDE.md's admin route doc says it returns `{ synced, errors }` which matches, but note it does NOT mirror the dashboard sync route's `rowsSynced` key — easy to conflate.
- Sidebar nav: CLAUDE.md's nav list (section 9 dashboard layout) does not mention the 'Leads' item (/dashboard/leads, Mailbox icon) which is now the second nav item, nor 'Jobs'. The Google Ads item is correctly gated on googleAdsEnabled and inserted before Settings, matching docs.
- Business fields googleAdsCustomerId/googleAdsEnabled/googleAdsTabLabel are documented as appearing in section 14 but are NOT listed in the section 4 Business model enumeration; they live at prisma/schema.prisma:57-59. GoogleAdsSnapshot relation `googleAdsSnapshots` exists on Business (schema:107) but is omitted from the section 4 relations list.
- No daily cron/scheduled sync exists in the codebase for Google Ads despite the UI empty-state ('Data syncs daily') and CLAUDE.md framing. All syncs are manual (dashboard Refresh button) or admin-triggered. CLAUDE.md does not claim a cron explicitly, but the 'syncs daily' UI copy is not backed by code in these files.
- getGoogleAdsClient passes only client_id/client_secret/developer_token to the GoogleAdsApi constructor; GOOGLE_ADS_REFRESH_TOKEN and GOOGLE_ADS_MCC_ID are supplied per-customer inside syncGoogleAdsData via client.Customer({ refresh_token, login_customer_id }). CLAUDE.md lists all five under getGoogleAdsClient/syncGoogleAdsData without this split distinction.

### Super-Admin Panel & Admin APIs (14)

- app/admin/ClientDetailPanel/TogglesTab.tsx adds two toggles NOT documented in CLAUDE.md: 'Send known contacts to voicemail' (field knownContactVoicemailEnabled, Voicemail icon) and 'SMS Auto-Booking' (field smsBookingEnabled, MessageSquare icon).
- knownContactVoicemailEnabled is a real Business field (prisma/schema.prisma:70, default false) and is in AdminBusiness type + PATCH allowedFields, but is entirely absent from CLAUDE.md Section 4 (Prisma schema) and the critical-flags list.
- BUG / drift: TogglesTab and SettingsTab toggle notifyBySms / notifyByEmail and PATCH them, but '/api/admin/businesses/[id]/route.ts' allowedFields does NOT include notifyBySms or notifyByEmail — those PATCHes are silently dropped server-side. CLAUDE.md lists Notify by SMS/Email as functioning toggles.
- app/admin/components/CallScreenerCard.tsx is a real file but CLAUDE.md does not list it in the Admin Dashboard files table; it also appears unused (not imported by any admin component — the live IVR editing is inside TogglesTab instead).
- The /admin/[businessId]/conversations sub-route uses RAW bucket names as tab labels (All/Cold/Active/Stalled/Closed) via the TABS array, NOT the client-facing BUCKET_LABELS (No Reply/In Progress/Went Quiet/Closed). CLAUDE.md implies BUCKET_LABELS are always used in UI.
- view-as cookie: CLAUDE.md says the adminViewAs cookie is 'session-scoped — it expires when the browser closes.' Actual code sets maxAge: 60*60*24 (24 hours), so it persists across browser restarts for up to 24h. Cookie is sameSite:'lax', path:'/', NOT httpOnly/secure.
- The TogglesTab/SettingsTab success-merge preserves _count, conversationsThisMonth, conversationsLastMonth, leadsThisMonth from original but NOT conversationsAllTime or leadsAllTime — those computed fields can be lost from the in-memory AdminBusiness after a toggle/save.
- ClientDetailPanel exposes a 'Voicemails' tool that links to /api/admin/view-as (impersonation) rather than a dedicated admin voicemail viewer; CLAUDE.md's ToolsTab description ('per-business tools: bulk import contacts, view conversations, etc.') omits the blocked-numbers manager, screened-calls summary, and view-as-based voicemail link actually present.
- AdminTools dropdown includes a 'Sync Google Ads' action (POST /api/admin/google-ads/sync) and a 'Refresh Table' action; CLAUDE.md's AdminTools description ('Admin-level tools (usage sync, export)') does not mention Google Ads sync, Sheets sync, or refresh.
- The PATCH route accepts subscriptionStatus / businessType / timezone with no enum validation (only a field whitelist) — invalid values are written verbatim. Not noted as a gotcha.
- Blocked-number POST stores phoneNumber as-entered (no normalizeToE164/normalizePhoneNumber), unlike contacts which normalize. CLAUDE.md does not flag this inconsistency.
- app/admin/page.tsx duplicates the entire enriched-business computation locally (fetchBusinesses) rather than calling /api/admin/businesses; the page version .toISOString()s createdAt/updatedAt while the API route returns Date objects. Two parallel implementations of the same logic exist.
- AdminBusiness type (app/admin/types.ts) includes knownContactVoicemailEnabled and bookingRequiresAddress, which CLAUDE.md's AdminBusiness documentation omits.
- Admin routes return HTTP 403 (not 401) for unauthorized; view-as returns a redirect to /dashboard rather than an error. CLAUDE.md generically says admin routes 'check userId == ADMIN_USER_ID' without specifying the 403/redirect distinction.

### Owner Notifications, Transactional Email & Website Contact Intake (10)

- CLAUDE.md says owner notification emails use 'SMTP via nodemailer' / 'Singleton nodemailer.createTransport' (Sections 8 & 12, gotchas 23-25). GROUND TRUTH: as of commit 735b7ce, sendEmail() in lib/notify-owner.ts:696-714 sends ALL owner-notification emails via Resend (new Resend(process.env.RESEND_API_KEY), from: 'notifications@alignandacquire.com'). nodemailer is imported and getTransporter()/the module-level `transporter` variable still exist (lib/notify-owner.ts:7,662-691) but are now DEAD CODE — sendEmail no longer calls them. SMTP_USER/SMTP_PASS/SMTP_HOST/SMTP_PORT env vars are no longer used by any active code path.
- CLAUDE.md gotcha 23-25 ('SMTP_USER/SMTP_PASS missing = silent email failure', 'Gmail SMTP requires App Password', 'Emails send from SMTP_FROM or SMTP_USER') are now stale/incorrect — owner emails send from notifications@alignandacquire.com via Resend, and require RESEND_API_KEY, not SMTP creds.
- CLAUDE.md lists four notify functions (notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated, notifyOwnerOnBookingRequestNoCalendar, notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured, notifyOwnerOnHumanNeeded, notifyOwnerOnAIFailed). It does NOT document notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead (lib/notify-owner.ts:532-660), a SIXTH exported notify function added for the contact-form flow. It sends a styled HTML email (commit 227bab6) via sendEmail(to, subject, textBody, htmlBody) — the only notifier that passes a custom html argument; all others pass plain text wrapped by plainTextToEmailHtml.
- sendEmail signature changed: it now accepts an optional 4th `html?: string` param (lib/notify-owner.ts:696). When html is provided it is sent as-is; when omitted, plain text is wrapped via plainTextToEmailHtml. CLAUDE.md documents the old 3-arg SMTP version.
- CLAUDE.md describes /api/contact as 'creates Contact + WebsiteLead in background fire-and-forget'. GROUND TRUTH (commit 965e758): for attributed (client-tenant) submissions the Contact create, WebsiteLead create, AND notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead are now all AWAITED sequentially before the response returns (app/api/contact/route.ts:75-121) — explicitly NOT fire-and-forget, with a comment explaining Vercel freezes the function after response.
- notifyOwnerOnBookingRequestNoCalendar (lib/notify-owner.ts:188-271) is exported but has ZERO callers anywhere in lib/ or app/ — it is dead/orphaned code. CLAUDE.md documents it as an active notifier.
- Notify SMS copy strings in CLAUDE.md are partially outdated. Actual booking SMS: '📅 New Quote Request! {name} wants a quote for {service} on {date} at {time}.' (not 'wants [service] on [date] at [time].'). Actual human-needed SMS: '⚠️ A customer needs your help! {name} ({phone}) needs a personal follow-up. Check your email for the full conversation.' Email subjects also differ from CLAUDE.md: booking email subject is 'New Quote Visit - ...', lead subject is 'New Lead - {name} - {service}', human-needed subject is 'Follow-Up Needed - {name}', AI-failed subject is 'AI Unavailable - {name} - Please Follow Up'.
- notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated and notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead return { smsSent: boolean; emailSent: boolean }; the other notifiers (LeadCaptured, HumanNeeded, AIFailed, BookingRequestNoCalendar) return Promise<void>. CLAUDE.md only documents the return shape for notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated.
- CLAUDE.md gotcha 17/email section doesn't mention that notify functions log via console.error (not console.log) with a '[NOTIFY OWNER]' prefix tag throughout, including non-error informational logging.
- Dashboard URL in notifyOwnerOnBookingCreated is hardcoded to 'https://alignandacquire.com/dashboard/appointments' (lib/notify-owner.ts:93), bypassing the baseUrl/NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL helper that the other notifiers use. conversation links in other notifiers point to /dashboard/conversations/{id} via baseUrl.

### Public/Marketing Surface, Public Booking & Shared Components (15)

- BRAND REPOSITIONING: The entire public surface has been rebranded from a single-product 'MissedCall AI' SaaS into a multi-service 'Align and Acquire' growth-ops agency for trades. CLAUDE.md still describes only homepage/pricing/spam-screening marketing pages. Actual nav (NavBar.tsx + NavMenu.tsx): MissedCall AI / Services / Pricing / About / Book a call.
- NEW MARKETING ROUTES not in CLAUDE.md: /missedcall-ai, /services, /websites, /campaigns, /ads-management, /about, /book (top-level qualifying funnel), /demo-requested, /preview/home/embed, /config/nav-services.ts. CLAUDE.md only lists app/page.tsx, /pricing, /spam-screening.
- PRICING COMPLETELY CHANGED. CLAUDE.md says tiers 'Growth $200/mo, Pro $290/mo, All In $385/mo (setup $400/$400/$500)' and spam screening at +$75/mo. Actual app/pricing/page.tsx tiers are: Catch $349/mo ($400 setup), Grow $549/mo ($500 setup, most popular), Automate $900/mo ($750 setup). Spam screening add-on still $75/mo/$150 setup on the pricing tier cards and /spam-screening page, BUT the à-la-carte builder on /pricing lists 'Spam Call Screening' at $89/mo/$150 setup. NOTE: the OLD prices ($200/$290/$385) survive only in the static /preview/home/embed mockup, which is explicitly 'not the live site'.
- PRICING INCONSISTENCY across pages (ground-truth, all hardcoded): /pricing à-la-carte Google Ads = $225/mo+$300 setup, but /ads-management page shows $125/mo+$250 setup. /pricing à-la-carte 'Email & SMS Campaigns' = $175/mo+$150 setup, but /campaigns page shows $75/mo+$100 setup. These are static literals, not from a shared config.
- app/components/ServicesDropdown.tsx (listed in CLAUDE.md directory tree) DOES NOT EXIST. app/config/nav-services.ts NAV_SERVICES (5 services) header comment references a ServicesDropdown that renders them, but no consumer remains; NAV_SERVICES appears unused by NavBar/NavMenu (both hardcode their own link lists).
- app/page.tsx (homepage) no longer renders the documented 'Hero, features, ROI calculator, demo form, testimonials'. It renders: SmsThread hero, stats band, 3-service grid (linking to /services#...), founder teaser, ROICalculator, about, ContactForm, final CTA. DemoForm is NOT on the homepage (it's on /missedcall-ai).
- /api/contact behavior drift: CLAUDE.md says it creates Contact + WebsiteLead and (marketing path) emails YOUR_EMAIL. Actual route imports notifyOwnerOnWebsiteLead (NOT notifyOwnerOnLeadCaptured). Marketing email is sent from 'Align and Acquire <onboarding@resend.dev>' only when NO businessId AND NO businessSlug. ContactForm.tsx sends NO businessId/businessSlug/email — only { name, phone?, message, smsConsent } — so the homepage contact form always hits the marketing email path.
- FORM API TARGETS: WebsiteQuoteForm.tsx (on /websites) and DemoForm.tsx (on /missedcall-ai) both POST to /api/book-demo (NOT /api/contact), then router.push('/demo-requested'). They send { name, business, email, phone?, businessType, message?, smsConsent }. CLAUDE.md does not document /websites or that WebsiteQuoteForm targets /api/book-demo.
- /book (app/book/page.tsx) is now a multi-step QUALIFYING FUNNEL (qualify-business -> qualify-trade -> qualify-needs -> calendar -> form -> confirmation) that calls /api/marketing-bookings GET (availability) + POST (book). CLAUDE.md only documents /book/[businessSlug] (client booking) and never describes the top-level /book funnel or that /api/marketing-bookings has a GET handler returning { days }.
- /api/marketing-bookings hours: 8:00 AM–4:00 PM ET, 30-min slots with 15-min buffer = 45-min slot cadence (8:00, 8:45, 9:30...), MIN_NOTICE_HOURS=2, MAX_DAYS_AHEAD=14. Business resolved via MARKETING_BUSINESS_ID/MARKETING_BUSINESS_SLUG; Telnyx confirmation from MARKETING_TELNYX_NUMBER.
- ConditionalNavBar now hides the global NavBar on /dashboard, /admin, any path containing /embed, AND all /book paths (CLAUDE.md only documented hiding on /dashboard). On /sign-in & /sign-up it wraps NavBar in 'hidden md:block'.
- NEW: MetaPixel.tsx (Meta/Facebook Pixel) is wired into app/layout.tsx, gated on NEXT_PUBLIC_FACEBOOK_PIXEL_ID env var; fires PageView on route change and the /book funnel fires fbq('track','Schedule') on confirmation. Not documented in CLAUDE.md (new env var NEXT_PUBLIC_FACEBOOK_PIXEL_ID).
- NEW shared components not in CLAUDE.md component list: BrandFooter.tsx (used by all new marketing pages), SmsThread.tsx (animated SMS demo, used on homepage/missedcall-ai/services), MetaPixel.tsx, and app/components/ui/ (Badge, Button, Card, MetricCard, PageHeader, PeriodSelector, states, index.ts).
- Legal pages (/terms, /privacy) use light-on-dark Tailwind theme (bg-gray-950) NOT the new industrial 'Align and Acquire' brand theme; both reference 'MissedCall AI service' and 'Align & Acquire LLC', LAST_UPDATED 'February 23, 2026', contact jacob@alignandacquire.com, governing law Michigan.
- spam-screening page now markets the IVR as 'Click 1 to Connect' and the standalone price card shows $75/mo / $150 setup. CLAUDE.md's note that spam screening is shown as an explicit ✗ in all packages is stale — the new /pricing shows it as an add-on line item on Catch/Grow and 'included' on Automate.

- Owner notification emails now send via Resend (from notifications@alignandacquire.com), NOT nodemailer/Gmail SMTP — lib/notify-owner.ts:696-714. The nodemailer transporter (getTransporter, :662-691) is dead code. Contradicts CLAUDE.md Sections 8, 12 and gotchas 23, 24, 25.
- Voicemail email alert in the voice webhook also uses Resend (app/api/webhooks/voice/route.ts:730,749) — both email paths are Resend; CLAUDE.md's 'nodemailer for owner / Resend for voicemail' split (Section 12) is false.
- New Business field knownContactVoicemailEnabled Boolean @default(false) (prisma/schema.prisma:70) — undocumented. Drives a 'route known contacts to voicemail instead of AI SMS' call-flow variant referenced in 9 branches of app/api/webhooks/voice/route.ts (155,330,359,408,514,547,868).
- New Contact field isClientContact Boolean @default(false) (prisma/schema.prisma:154) — undocumented. Plus new function isClientVoicemailContact() in lib/contacts-check.ts:36-51 (queries isClientContact:true via phonesMatch), separate from isExistingContact (which still uses source:null).

- New /dashboard/leads page (page.tsx + LeadsClient.tsx + CombinedLeadsList.tsx) merges /api/dashboard/conversations and /api/dashboard/website-leads client-side (CombinedLeadsList.tsx:101-102). No /api/dashboard/leads route exists. Undocumented.

- Entire scripts/ directory undocumented in CLAUDE.md tree: backfill-isClientContact.ts, cleanup-test-appointments.ts, export-conversations.ts, generate-favicons.mjs, optimize-logo.mjs, and scripts/sql/*.sql raw migrations (contradicts CLAUDE.md's 'migrations are Prisma-only' implication).
